# 六爻 · 疾病 · 生死

*Liu Yao (six-line coin divination) · Illness · Life or death: will the patient recover, and the signs of danger*

76 条。原文逐字核对过 `functions/_lib/doctrine/books/` 下的文本,行号是那份整理本的行号,不是原刻本的页码。「规则(整理)」是本库的归纳,引用时引原文。

## LY.ILL.BSQS.03 六神空亡诀·白虎空亡病可痊

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卜筮全书的六神空亡诀说：白虎落了空亡，病可以痊愈。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Bushi Quanshu's verse on the six spirits falling Void says: if the White Tiger is Void, the illness can be cured.

**《卜筮全书·卜筮歌诀·十九、六神空亡诀》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3139 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Bushi Quanshu verses, no. 19: the six spirits falling Void", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3139*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 白虎空亡病可痊， | 白虎落了空亡，病可以痊愈， | With the White Tiger Void, the illness can be cured, |

> **按**:这一句是六神空亡诀里的一句。全诀六句，每一句讲一位神落空亡的结果：青龙、朱雀、勾陈、螣蛇、白虎、玄武各一句，讲的分别是家里的喜事、官司、牵连、怪异、病和盗贼，这里只收和病有关的白虎一句。道理是白虎主丧病，它落了空亡，它的凶力暂时不作数。断易天机的六神章里也有「白虎空亡病无事」（LY.ILL.DYTJ.38）。
>
> **Note:** This is one line of the verse on the six spirits falling Void. The whole verse has six lines, one for each of the Azure Dragon, Vermilion Bird, Hook Earth, Coiling Snake, White Tiger and Dark Tortoise, dealing in turn with joy in the house, lawsuits, entanglements, strange happenings, illness and thieves; only the White Tiger line, which bears on illness, is taken here. The reason is that the White Tiger means mourning and illness, and once it is Void its force does not count for the time being. The chapter on the six spirits in the Duanyi Tianji also has 'the White Tiger Void: the illness comes to nothing' (LY.ILL.DYTJ.38).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1002 行:白虎空亡病可痊

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.38

---

## LY.ILL.BSQS.20 行人章·附健否：旺相不入墓爻活、囚死空亡随水沫；卦爻不化入墓中皆平善，反之必死

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占行人是否平安的歌里说：行人的爻旺相、没有入墓就活，囚死、落空亡就像水沫一样散去；卦和爻不变化入墓，都平安稳当，反过来一定死，只好去招魂、办殡宫。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The verse on whether a traveller is well says: if the traveller's line is strong and does not enter the tomb it lives; if it is confined, dead or Void it drifts away like foam on water; if the hexagram and lines do not change into a tomb, all is peaceful and secure; the reverse is certain death, and one can only go and call the soul and prepare the mourning hall.

**《卜筮全书·卜筮歌诀·行人章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3926–3928 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Bushi Quanshu verses: the chapter on travellers", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 3926–3928*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 旺相不入墓爻活， | 旺相，没有入墓，这一爻就活； | Strong and not entering the tomb, the line lives; |
| 囚死空亡随水沫。 | 囚死、落空亡，就像水上的泡沫散去。 | confined, dead or Void, it drifts away like foam on the water. |
| 又看上下卦如何， | 再看上卦下卦怎么样： | Then see how the upper and lower trigrams are: |
| 旺相相生行泼泼。 | 旺相又相生，行动就活泼泼的。 | thriving and generating each other, one moves about briskly. |
| 卦爻不化入墓中， | 卦和爻不变化入墓， | If the hexagram and lines do not change into a tomb, |
| 皆为平善得安隆。 | 都是平安顺遂，稳当兴隆； | all is peaceful and smooth, secure and prosperous; |
| 反之必死不须望， | 反过来就一定死，不必指望， | the reverse is certain death, with no hope, |
| 但去招魂作殡宫。 | 只好去招魂、办殡宫了。 | and one can only go and call the soul and prepare the mourning hall. |

> **按**:这一首歌收在占行人一章末尾的「附：健否」，问的是出门在外的人是死是活，和占病的生死是同一类判断：看用爻旺不旺、入没入墓、落没落空。「殡宫」是停放灵柩的地方。这里的行人是一个不在场的人，他的病死判断和本域「他人病」「生死」两类相通。
>
> **Note:** This poem is the 'Appendix: whether he is well' at the end of the chapter on travellers and asks whether a person away from home is alive or dead; it is the same kind of judgement as life and death in an illness: whether the Subject line is strong, whether it enters a tomb, whether it is Void. A 'mourning hall' is where the coffin is kept. The traveller is someone not present, and the judgement of his illness or death is akin to the 'illness of others' and 'life and death' topics in this domain.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 1504 行:又看上下卦如何;同文 第 1504 行:旺相相生行泼泼;同文 第 1504 行:卦爻不化入墓中;同文 第 1504 行:皆为平善得安隆;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1504 行:返之必死不须望;同文 第 1504 行:但去招魂作殡宫
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1324 行:旺相不入墓爻活;同文 第 1324 行:囚死空亡随水沫;同文 第 1324 行:又看上下卦如何;同文 第 1324 行:旺相相生行泼泼;同文 第 1324 行:卦爻不化入墓中;同文 第 1324 行:皆为平善得安隆;同文 第 1324 行:反之必死不须望;同文 第 1324 行:但去招魂作殡宫

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.27、LY.ILL.DYTJ.23

## LY.ILL.BSQS.35 疾病论·死生之兆(一)：财为禄命怕空亡；十死卦春忌需蒙、夏嫌观蛊、秋忧剥节、冬畏旅临；禄命随官难救、世身入墓难医；鬼入用爻当主死

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:疾病论里看死生的一段：财爻是禄命，怕落空亡，伏着的是命爻，要防克害；先拿十死卦当凶，最凶的是用爻不上卦、又落空亡，有救的是福位（子孙）扶持得力；春天忌需、蒙，夏天嫌观、蛊，秋天忧剥、节，冬天怕旅、临；禄命随着官鬼，难救，世爻和身入了墓，难医；官鬼遇到大杀，怕伤身；鬼爻入了用爻，就主死。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A passage of the chapter on illness on reading life and death: Wealth is the salary and fate and fears falling Void, and if it lies hidden the life line must be guarded from control and harm; first take the ten fatal hexagrams as ill-omened, the worst being a Subject line that is absent or Void, while what can be saved is when the Output place supports it with force; in spring avoid Xu and Meng, in summer dislike Guan and Gu, in autumn fear Bo and Jie, in winter dread Lu and Lin; with the fate line following the Pressure line, rescue is hard, and with the Self and the Body entering the tomb, treatment is hard; Pressure meeting a great killing, beware harm to the Body; a ghost entering the Subject line means death.

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·十一、疾病论》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9777–9779 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 11: on illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 9777–9779*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财为禄命怕空亡， | 财爻是禄命，怕落空亡； | Wealth is the salary and fate, and fears falling Void; |
| 伏是命爻防克害。 | 伏藏的是命爻，要防克害。 | if it lies hidden, the life line must be guarded from control and harm. |
| 先将十死卦为凶， | 先把十死卦当作凶， | First take the ten fatal hexagrams as ill-omened, |
| 莫比一般爻是吉。 | 不要拿它们和一般爻的吉去比。 | and do not weigh them against the usual auspicious lines. |
| 最凶者， | 最凶险的情形是， | The worst is |
| 用爻不见与空亡； | 用爻不上卦，和落空亡； | when the Subject line is absent, or Void; |
| 有救者， | 有救的情形是， | what can be saved is |
| 福位扶持生得力。 | 福位扶持、生助得力。 | when the Output place supports and strengthens it. |
| 春忌需蒙， | 春天忌需、蒙， | In spring avoid Xu and Meng, |
| 夏嫌观蛊， | 夏天嫌观、蛊， | in summer dislike Guan and Gu, |
| 秋忧剥节， | 秋天忧剥、节， | in autumn fear Bo and Jie, |
| 冬畏旅临。 | 冬天怕旅、临。 | in winter dread Lu and Lin. |
| 禄命随官难救， | 禄命跟着官鬼，难救； | With the fate line following the Pressure line, rescue is hard; |
| 世身入墓难医。 | 世爻和身入了墓，难医。 | with the Self and the Body entering the tomb, treatment is hard. |
| 官逢大杀忌伤身， | 官鬼遇到大杀，怕伤到身； | Pressure meeting a great killing, beware of harm to the Body; |
| 鬼入用爻当主死。 | 鬼爻入了用爻，就主死。 | a ghost entering the Subject line means death. |

> **打架**:哪几个卦占病最凶，几本书的名单不同。这一段按季节给：春需、蒙，夏观、蛊，秋剥、节，冬旅、临；阐奥歌章第三首（LY.ILL.CA.03）是丰、蛊、困、明夷、夬、同人一例，加冬旅、春需、夏观、秋剥，和这一段在旅、需、观、剥四卦的季节上一致；卜筮元龟一段（LY.ILL.DYTJ.15）是丰、观、需、剥、节、旅、贲、明夷、蛊、夬、同人「十死分明」；天玄赋（LY.ILL.TXF.06）是明夷、观、贲、需、临（世身入墓）和大畜、丰、同、蛊、夬（财鬼俱兴）。蒙只有这一段有，临只有这一段和天玄赋有；另外这一段点名的只有八个卦，却称「十死卦」，名目和数目对不上。书里没有裁决。周易尚占和文王金钱课按卦名断病，和这一段点名的几个卦有出入：蒙，周易尚占说「却宜占病」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.06）；观，周易尚占说「财聚病痊」，文王金钱课说「占病即愈」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.02、WWJQ.03）；节，文王金钱课说「疾病口舌消除安」（LY.ILL.WWJQ.03）；临，周易尚占说「病犯崇侵」，文王金钱课补录部分说「疾病即痊」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.07、WWJQ.05）；蛊，周易尚占说病凶，和这一段一致（LY.ILL.ZSZ.05）。
>
> **Where they disagree:** The books differ on which hexagrams are the worst for illness. This passage gives them by season: Xu and Meng in spring, Guan and Gu in summer, Bo and Jie in autumn, Lu and Lin in winter. The Chan'ao Gezhang's third poem (LY.ILL.CA.03) gives Feng, Gu, Kun, Mingyi, Guai and Tongren together, plus Lu in winter, Xu in spring, Guan in summer and Bo in autumn, agreeing with this passage on the seasons of Lu, Xu, Guan and Bo; the Bushi Yuangui passage (LY.ILL.DYTJ.15) gives Feng, Guan, Xu, Bo, Jie, Lu, Bi, Mingyi, Gu, Guai and Tongren, 'the ten deaths are clear'; the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.06) gives Mingyi, Guan, Bi, Xu and Lin (the Self and Body entering the tomb) and Daxu, Feng, Tong, Gu and Guai (Wealth and Pressure both rising). Meng is found only in this passage, and Lin only in this passage and the Tianxuan Fu; moreover this passage names only eight hexagrams yet calls them 'the ten fatal hexagrams', so the name and the number do not match. The books do not decide. The Zhouyi Shangzhan and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke judge illness by the name of the hexagram, and differ from several hexagrams named in this passage: for Meng (Youthful Folly) the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'on the contrary fit for divining illness' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.06); for Guan the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'wealth gathers and the illness is cured' and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'divining illness, it is cured at once' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.02, WWJQ.03); for Jie the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'illness and quarrels are removed and all is at peace' (LY.ILL.WWJQ.03); for Lin the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the illness offends a curse that attacks' and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke, in its supplementary part, says 'the illness is cured at once' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.07, WWJQ.05); for Gu the Zhouyi Shangzhan says the illness is ominous, which agrees with this passage (LY.ILL.ZSZ.05).

> **按**:这一段连着讲了三个层次：先是用神的安危（财为禄命、用爻不见与空亡、福位扶持），然后是十死卦按季节的忌，最后是墓和大杀（世身入墓、官逢大杀、鬼入用爻）。「禄命随官」是禄命爻随着官鬼（入墓）的意思，断易天机里有「随官入墓」一节可对照（LY.ILL.DYTJ.27）。「用爻」就是用神。
>
> **Note:** This passage runs through three layers: first the safety of the Subject (Wealth as salary and fate, the Subject line absent or Void, the Output place supporting it), then the seasonal taboos of the ten fatal hexagrams, and last the tomb and the great killing (the Self and Body entering the tomb, Pressure meeting a great killing, a ghost entering the Subject line). 'The salary-fate following the Pressure line' means the salary-fate line following the Pressure line (into the tomb), and the section on 'following Pressure into the tomb' in the Duanyi Tianji can be compared (LY.ILL.DYTJ.27). The 'Subject line' is the Subject.

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.27、LY.ILL.DYTJ.15、LY.ILL.CA.03、LY.ILL.ZSZ.02、LY.ILL.ZSZ.03、LY.ILL.ZSZ.04、LY.ILL.ZSZ.05、LY.ILL.WWJQ.03、LY.ILL.ZSZ.07、LY.ILL.WWJQ.05、LY.ILL.ZSZ.06

## LY.ILL.BSQS.36 疾病论·死生之兆(二)：催尸之杀怕临病者之爻、春辰夏未秋戌冬丑兴还死；天转杀地转杀遇日辰旺相便言死处；春忌卯夏嫌午秋酉冬子；不论少年老年一命见阎君

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:疾病论里接着讲死的征兆：要忌催尸杀，怕它落在病人的爻上；春天的辰、夏天的未最难当，秋天的戌、冬天的丑发动还是死；又嫌天转杀、地转杀，是最凶的神，再遇到日辰旺相，就可以说出死的地方；春天忌卯爻卯日，夏天嫌午位午爻，秋天的金爻、酉日最忌，冬天的子爻遇上子日；不论是白面少年，还是雪白鬓发的老人，家产再多，也总要拿一条命去见阎王。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The chapter on illness goes on with the signs of death: one must avoid the Urging-the-Corpse killing and fear it falling on the sick person's line; Chen in spring and Wei in summer are the hardest to bear, and Xu in autumn and Chou in winter, rising, still mean death; also dislike the Heaven-Turning and Earth-Turning killings, the most ill-omened spirits, and if they meet a strong Day one may name the place of death; in spring avoid a Mao line and a Mao day, in summer a Wu place and Wu line, in autumn the Metal line and the You day, in winter a Zi line meeting a Zi day; whether a fair-faced youth or an old person with snow-white temples, however much wealth a family has, one must still go with one's life to see Lord Yama.

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·十一、疾病论》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9780–9782 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 11: on illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 9780–9782*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 须忌催尸之杀， | 要忌讳催尸杀， | One must avoid the Urging-the-Corpse killing, |
| 怕临病者之爻。 | 怕它落在病人的爻上。 | and fear it falling on the sick person's line. |
| 春辰夏未最难当， | 春天的辰、夏天的未最难当， | Chen in spring and Wei in summer are the hardest to bear, |
| 秋戌冬丑兴还死。 | 秋天的戌、冬天的丑，发动还是死。 | and Xu in autumn and Chou in winter, rising, still mean death. |
| 又嫌天地转杀， | 又嫌天转杀、地转杀， | Also dislike the Heaven-Turning and Earth-Turning killings, |
| 最是凶神； | 它们是最凶的神； | the most ill-omened spirits; |
| 乃遇日辰旺相， | 如果遇到日辰旺相， | if they meet a strong Day, |
| 便言死处。 | 就可以说出死的地方。 | one may then name the place of death. |
| 春忌卯爻卯日， | 春天忌卯爻、卯日， | In spring avoid a Mao line and a Mao day, |
| 夏嫌午位午爻， | 夏天嫌午位、午爻， | in summer dislike a Wu place and Wu line, |
| 秋金酉日最忌凶， | 秋天的金爻、酉日最忌凶， | in autumn the Metal line and the You day are the most to be feared, |
| 冬子爻中逢子日。 | 冬天子爻遇上子日。 | in winter a Zi line meeting a Zi day. |
| 不论白面少年人， | 不论是白面的少年人， | Never mind a fair-faced youth, |
| 可惜雪鬓老年容。 | 还是可惜的雪白鬓发的老人； | nor, sadly, an old person with snow-white temples; |
| 纵有家资过百斗， | 纵然家产超过百斗， | even if the family's wealth exceeds a hundred measures of grain, |
| 也须一命见阎君。 | 也总得拿一条命去见阎王。 | one must still go with one's life to see Lord Yama. |

> **按**:「催尸杀」「天转杀」「地转杀」是神煞名：催尸杀按春辰夏未秋戌冬丑取（和下面的句子对得上），天转杀、地转杀的位置这里没有给。「便言死处」的「死处」按字面是死的地方，也可能是死的时间，书里没有说。「秋金酉日」是秋天的金爻和酉日，两者都算。卜筮全书神煞歌例里另有「丧车杀」（LY.ILL.BSQS.53）和「沐浴杀」，位置也是春辰夏未秋戌冬丑一类的取法，可对照。
>
> **Note:** The 'Urging-the-Corpse', 'Heaven-Turning' and 'Earth-Turning' killings are spirit-stars: the Urging-the-Corpse killing is taken as Chen in spring, Wei in summer, Xu in autumn and Chou in winter (which matches the lines that follow), and the positions of the Heaven-Turning and Earth-Turning killings are not given here. 'The place of death' is literally where death happens, and may mean when; the book does not say. 'Autumn Metal You day' means the Metal line in autumn and the You day, and both count. The spirit-star verses of the Bushi Quanshu also have a 'Funeral Carriage killing' (LY.ILL.BSQS.53) and a 'Bathing killing', placed in the same way (Chen in spring, Wei in summer, Xu in autumn, Chou in winter), and can be compared.

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.27、LY.ILL.DYTJ.31、LY.ILL.DYTJ.32、LY.ILL.ZSZ.01

## LY.ILL.BSQS.53 神煞歌例·占病见死的煞：墓门杀病人不死也伤财；丧车杀人来占病无他断、教君作急买棺材；沐浴杀发动病人亡

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:神煞歌例里三个主病人死亡的煞：墓门杀，甲乙见金、丙丁见水、戊己见木、庚辛见火、壬癸见土，病人不死也伤财；丧车杀，春鸡夏鼠秋兔冬马，有人来占病，没有别的可断，劝你赶紧去买棺材；沐浴杀，春辰夏未秋戌冬丑，发动，病人死亡。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Three killings in the verses on spirit-stars that mean a sick person's death: the Tomb Gate killing, Jia and Yi meeting Metal, Bing and Ding Water, Wu and Ji Wood, Geng and Xin Fire, Ren and Gui Earth: the sick person, if he does not die, at least loses money; the Mourning Cart killing, the rooster in spring, the rat in summer, the rabbit in autumn and the horse in winter: a person who comes to divine illness has nothing else to be judged, and I advise you to buy a coffin at once; the Bathing killing, Chen in spring, Wei in summer, Xu in autumn and Chou in winter: if it moves, the sick person dies.

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·十九、神煞歌例·凶神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 10090–10092 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 19: verses on spirit-stars, the ominous stars", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 10090–10092*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 甲乙见金墓门开， | 甲、乙见金，墓门打开； | Jia and Yi meeting Metal, the gate of the tomb opens; |
| 丙丁见水哭哀哀， | 丙、丁见水，哭得哀哀； | Bing and Ding meeting Water, there is mournful weeping; |
| 戊己见木须防厄， | 戊、己见木，要防灾厄； | Wu and Ji meeting Wood, beware of calamity; |
| 庚辛见火孝服来， | 庚、辛见火，丧服要来； | Geng and Xin meeting Fire, mourning dress is coming; |
| 壬癸见土难回避， | 壬、癸见土，难以回避， | Ren and Gui meeting Earth, it is hard to avoid, |
| 病人不死也伤财。 | 病人就是不死，也要伤财。 | and the sick person, if he does not die, at least loses money. |

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·十九、神煞歌例·凶神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 10096 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 19: verses on spirit-stars, the ominous stars", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 10096*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 丧车春鸡夏鼠来， | 丧车：春天是鸡、夏天来的是鼠， | The Mourning Cart: in spring the rooster, in summer the rat comes, |
| 秋兔冬马好安排； | 秋天是兔、冬天是马，好安排； | in autumn the rabbit, in winter the horse, which is well arranged; |
| 人来占病无他断， | 有人来占病，没有别的可断， | a person who comes to divine illness, there is nothing else to be judged, |
| 教君作急买棺材。 | 劝你赶紧去买棺材。 | I advise you to hurry and buy a coffin. |

**《卜筮全书·阐幽精要·十九、神煞歌例·凶神》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 10100 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Chanyou Jingyao, no. 19: verses on spirit-stars, the ominous stars", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 10100*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 沐浴杀难当， | 沐浴杀难当， | The Bathing killing is hard to bear; |
| 春辰夏未殃， | 春天辰、夏天未是祸殃， | Chen in spring and Wei in summer bring harm, |
| 秋戌冬丑是， | 秋天戌、冬天丑就是它； | Xu in autumn and Chou in winter are it; |
| 发动病人亡。 | 发动，病人就死亡。 | if it moves, the sick person dies. |

> **打架**:丧车杀的四季位置，两本不一样。卜筮全书这一首是「春鸡夏鼠秋兔冬马」；断易天机（LY.ILL.DYTJ.32）是「春鸡夏鼠秋马冬羊」；春、夏两季一致，秋、冬不同。另外断易天机的旌旗杀（LY.ILL.DYTJ.33）是「春兔夏鼠秋鸡冬马」，和卜筮全书这一首的丧车只差春、秋两季的鸡兔互换。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** The seasonal positions of the Mourning Cart differ between the two books. This verse in the Bushi Quanshu has 'the rooster in spring, the rat in summer, the rabbit in autumn, the horse in winter'; the Duanyi Tianji (LY.ILL.DYTJ.32) has 'the rooster in spring, the rat in summer, the horse in autumn, the sheep in winter'; spring and summer agree, autumn and winter do not. Moreover the Banner killing of the Duanyi Tianji (LY.ILL.DYTJ.33) has 'the rabbit in spring, the rat in summer, the rooster in autumn, the horse in winter', which differs from the Mourning Cart of this verse only in the rooster and rabbit swapped between spring and autumn. The books do not decide.

> **按**:墓门杀的五句，每句的前一个字组是日干（甲乙、丙丁、戊己、庚辛、壬癸），后一个字是五行，对着的正好是克那一组日干的五行（木逢金、火逢水、土逢木、金逢火、水逢土），也就是各日干的官鬼；歌里没有这样说明，这是从字面排出来的。「哭哀哀」「孝服」是丧事的征兆。丧车杀的位置，按地支对冲推：春（卯）冲酉（鸡）、夏（午）冲子（鼠）、秋（酉）冲卯（兔）、冬（子）冲午（马），卜筮全书这一首正好是各季旺支的对冲，断易天机的秋冬两处不是；这是按地支推出来的，书里没有这样讲。「教君作急买棺材」是语气很重的一句，原文如此。沐浴杀的位置（春辰夏未秋戌冬丑）和本篇疾病论里催尸杀的位置一样（LY.ILL.BSQS.36），歌里没有说它们是不是同一个煞。
>
> **Note:** In each of the five lines on the Tomb Gate killing, the first group of characters is a pair of day stems (Jia and Yi, Bing and Ding, Wu and Ji, Geng and Xin, Ren and Gui) and the second is an element, and the element is exactly the one that controls that pair of stems (Wood meeting Metal, Fire meeting Water, Earth meeting Wood, Metal meeting Fire, Water meeting Earth), that is, the Pressure of each day stem; the verse does not say so and this is laid out from the letter. 'Mournful weeping' and 'mourning dress' are signs of a funeral. The positions of the Mourning Cart, reckoned by the clashes of the branches: spring (Mao) clashes You (the rooster), summer (Wu) clashes Zi (the rat), autumn (You) clashes Mao (the rabbit), winter (Zi) clashes Wu (the horse); this verse in the Bushi Quanshu is exactly the clash of each season's ruling branch, and the Duanyi Tianji's autumn and winter are not; this is reckoned from the branches and the book does not say it. 'I advise you to buy a coffin at once' is a very heavy sentence, as the original has it. The positions of the Bathing killing (Chen in spring, Wei in summer, Xu in autumn, Chou in winter) are the same as those of the Urging-the-Corpse killing in the chapter on illness in this book (LY.ILL.BSQS.36), and the verses do not say whether they are the same killing.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 352 行:甲乙见金墓门开;同文 第 352 行:丙丁见水哭哀哀;同文 第 352 行:戊己见木须防厄;同文 第 352 行:庚辛见火孝服来;异文(相似 0.86) 第 352 行:壬癸会土难回避
- 《断易天机》:同文 第 294 行:丧车春鸡夏鼠来
- 《易冒》:同文 第 720 行:丧车春鸡夏鼠来

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.32、LY.ILL.DYTJ.33、LY.ILL.BSQS.36、LY.ILL.ZSZ.01

## LY.ILL.BSYG.05 日六神内动·白虎内惊忧疾病：白虎是金神；落在水爻上，水为白虎入墓占疾大凶；两重入墓必见死亡；秋申酉爻上动旺相尤凶，休囚吉

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:日六神内动的歌说：白虎在内卦发动，忧惊、疾病。自注说：白虎是金神；白虎也可以落在水爻上，这时水爻就是白虎，入了墓，占病大凶；例如庚、辛日得乾卦，甲子水爻动，变辛丑土，水墓在辰，白虎又是金神，叫两重入墓，必见死亡；白虎在秋天申、酉爻上动，旺相，更凶，休囚则吉。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The song on the spirits of the Day moving within says: when the White Tiger moves in the inner trigram, there is worry, fright and illness. Its own note says: the White Tiger is the Metal spirit; it can also come on a Water line, and then the Water line is the White Tiger; if it enters the tomb, illness is greatly ominous; for example, on a Geng or Xin day, casting Qian, the Water line Jiazi moves and turns into Xinchou Earth, the Water tomb is at Chen, and since the White Tiger is also the Metal spirit, it is called entering the tomb twice, and death is sure to be seen; when the White Tiger moves on a Shen or You line in autumn it is thriving and strong and still more ominous, and when resting or trapped it is auspicious.

**《卜筮元龟·六神门·日六神内动》原文** — 《大易断例卜筮元龟目》第 434 行 · *Bushi Yuangui (The Great Tortoise of Divination), "The Six Spirits: Spirits of the Day Moving in the Inner Trigram", base text; Dayi Duanli Bushi Yuangui Mu, line 434*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 白虎内惊忧疾病， | 白虎在内卦发动，忧惊、疾病， | when the White Tiger moves within, worry, fright and illness, |

**《大易断例卜筮元龟目》括号里的自注(没标「虎易注」的)** — 《大易断例卜筮元龟目》第 434 行 · *The Dayi Duanli's own bracketed notes (those not marked as the modern editor's); Dayi Duanli Bushi Yuangui Mu, line 434*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 白虎，金神。 | 白虎是金神。 | The White Tiger is the Metal spirit. |
| 或在水爻上来，是水为白虎，入墓，占疾大凶。 | 也可以落在水爻上，这时水爻就是白虎，入了墓，占病大凶。 | It may also come on a Water line; then Water is the White Tiger, and if it enters the tomb, illness is greatly ominous. |
| 如庚辛日卜得乾卦，甲子水动，是水为白虎，化为辛丑土，内见巽，水墓在辰，又曰白虎金神，名两重入墓，必见死亡。 | 例如庚、辛日卜得乾卦，甲子水爻动，这时水就是白虎，变成辛丑土，里面见到巽，水的墓在辰，又说白虎是金神，叫两重入墓，必然见到死亡。 | For example, on a Geng or Xin day, casting Qian, the Water line Jiazi moves, so Water is the White Tiger, and turns into Xinchou Earth; within is seen Xun, and the Water tomb is at Chen; and since the White Tiger is also called the Metal spirit, it is called entering the tomb twice, and death is sure to be seen. |
| 他皆仿此。 | 别的都照这个类推。 | The rest follow this. |
| 秋申酉爻上动，为旺相尤凶，休囚吉。 | 在秋天申、酉爻上动，是旺相，尤其凶；休囚就吉。 | Moving on a Shen or You line in autumn, it is thriving and strong and especially ominous; resting or trapped, it is auspicious. |

> **按**:这是这部书自注里最长的一个病例。「是水为白虎」是说白虎这个神附在水爻上；白虎本来是金神，所以又叫「两重入墓」——按字面，是金神白虎、和它所附的水爻，两个都入了墓（水墓在辰）。例子里「内见巽」的「巽」书里没有解释，译文照字面。「秋申酉爻上动，为旺相尤凶，休囚吉」是说白虎旺的时候凶得更重，衰了反而吉；这是白虎自己的旺衰，不是病人的。断易天机里同一句歌诀（LY.ILL.DYTJ.38）后面只有今人的注号（1），没有这段自注。
>
> **Note:** This is the longest case on illness in this book's own notes. 'Water is the White Tiger' says that the spirit of the White Tiger is attached to a Water line; the White Tiger is by nature the Metal spirit, so it is also called 'entering the tomb twice' — literally, both the Metal spirit White Tiger and the Water line it is attached to enter the tomb (the Water tomb is at Chen). The 'Xun' of 'within is seen Xun' in the example is not explained in the book and is translated literally. 'Moving on a Shen or You line in autumn, thriving and strong and especially ominous; resting or trapped, auspicious' says that the White Tiger is more ominous when it is strong and, when it is weak, on the contrary auspicious; this is the strength of the White Tiger itself and not of the sick person. The same line of the song in the Duanyi Tianji (LY.ILL.DYTJ.38) has after it only a modern note number (1) and not this note.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 469 行:白虎内惊忧疾病

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.38、LY.ILL.BSYG.03、LY.ILL.BSYG.04、LY.ILL.TXF.04、LY.ILL.HZL.08、LY.ILL.ZSZ.07、LY.ILL.GLJ.13

## LY.ILL.BSYG.10 推占来情·世应日月破并刑，官灾盗贼死亡惊：世应居月破月刑、又来刑本日，定有官司盗贼死亡惊恐；洞林曰世应三刑并杀阴，十死一生

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:推占来情的歌说：世、应碰上日、月的破和刑，有官灾、盗贼、死亡、惊恐；如果不是和人争讼的事，就有死亡、盗贼和哭泣的声音。自注说：凡是世、应落在月破、月刑上、又来刑到本日的，一定有官司、盗贼、死亡、惊恐；再看有没有刑杀。《洞林》说，世应三刑又并着杀阴，是十死一生。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The verses on reading the purpose of the asker say: when the Self and the Other meet the break and punishment of the Day and Month, there is official calamity, thieves, death and fright; if it is not a matter of quarrelling with others, there are death, thieves and the sound of weeping. Its own note says: whenever the Self and Other sit in the Month-break or Month-punishment and also come to punish the day itself, there are surely lawsuits, thieves, death and fright; then look also for punishment and killing. The Dongling says: when the Self and Other meet the three punishments together with the Yin Killing, it is ten deaths to one life.

**《卜筮元龟·六神门·推占来情休旺吉凶要决章》原文** — 《大易断例卜筮元龟目》第 470 行 · *Bushi Yuangui (The Great Tortoise of Divination), "The Six Spirits: Essential Verses on Reading the Purpose of the Asker and Strength and Weakness", base text; Dayi Duanli Bushi Yuangui Mu, line 470*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世应日月破并刑， | 世、应碰上日破、月破和刑， | When the Self and Other meet the Day-break, the Month-break and punishment, |
| 官灾盗贼死亡惊， | 有官灾、盗贼、死亡、惊恐； | there are official calamity, thieves, death and fright; |
| 若不人与相争事， | 如果不是和人争讼的事， | if it is not a matter of quarrelling with others, |
| 却有亡贼哭泣声。 | 就有死亡、盗贼、哭泣的声音。 | there are death, thieves and the sound of weeping. |

**《大易断例卜筮元龟目》括号里的自注(没标「虎易注」的)** — 《大易断例卜筮元龟目》第 470 行 · *The Dayi Duanli's own bracketed notes (those not marked as the modern editor's); Dayi Duanli Bushi Yuangui Mu, line 470*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡世应居月破月刑，来刑本日者，定有官司、盗贼、死亡、惊恐， | 凡是世、应落在月破、月刑上，又来刑到本日的，一定有官司、盗贼、死亡、惊恐， | Whenever the Self and Other sit in the Month-break or Month-punishment and also come to punish the day itself, there are surely lawsuits, thieves, death and fright, |
| 故也。 | 就是这个缘故。 | for this reason. |
| 或公讼喧争事， | 或者是打官司、喧闹争吵的事， | or matters of public litigation and noisy dispute, |
| 或失财、孝服、悲泣声。 | 或者是失财、孝服、悲哭的声音。 | or loss of wealth, mourning dress, the sound of grief. |
| 更看有无刑杀， | 还要看有没有刑和杀， | Look further for punishment and killing. |
| 洞林曰： | 《洞林》上说： | The Dongling says: |
| 世应三刑并杀阴， | 世、应遇三刑、又并着杀阴， | when the Self and Other meet the three punishments together with the Yin Killing, |
| 十死一生也。 | 是十死一生。 | it is ten deaths to one life. |

> **按**:这一联讲的是世、应碰到凶的时候会有什么事，死亡只是其中一项，并不专指病；收在疾病域是因为它给了「十死一生」这个最重的断语，而这个断语在占病里最常被引。「洞林曰」引的是《洞林》（书名，书里没有说是哪个人的《洞林》）；「杀阴」大概就是前面（第 437 行自注）按月排的杀阴（正午、二巳、三辰……逐月下），这里书里没有指明。「月刑」是和月建相刑的地支。「亡贼哭泣声」的「亡贼」读作「死亡、盗贼」。「若不人与相争事」的「人与」是别人，书里字序如此，按字面。
>
> **Note:** This couplet is about what happens when the Self and Other meet ill omens, and death is only one item among them, not particular to illness; it is taken in the illness domain because it gives the heaviest verdict 'ten deaths to one life', which is most often quoted in divining illness. 'The Dongling says' quotes the Dongling (a book title; the book does not say whose Dongling); the 'Yin Killing' is probably the Yin Killing set by month spoken of earlier (the note at line 437: Wu in the first month, Si in the second, Chen in the third, going down by months), which the book does not specify here. 'Month-punishment' is the branch that punishes the Month branch. The 'death and thieves' of 'the sound of death and thieves and weeping' is read as 'death, thieves'. The 'person with' of 'if it is not a matter of quarrelling with others' is 'others', in the character order of the book, and is literal.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 502 行:世应日月破并刑;同文 第 502 行:官灾盗贼死亡惊;异文(相似 0.86) 第 502 行:却有亡财哭泣声

相关:LY.ILL.BSYG.11、LY.ILL.BSQS.36、LY.ILL.TXF.06

## LY.ILL.BSZZ.36 何知章·病要死：卜筮正宗答用神无救又入墓；卜筮大全答身命世鬼入墓

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:怎么知道这家有人病得要死：卜筮正宗说是用神没有救护又入了墓；卜筮大全说是身、命、世、鬼入了墓，照这样推断。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** How to know that someone in a household is ill unto death: the Bushi Zhengzong says the Subject has no rescue and enters a tomb; the Bushi Daquan says the Body, the destiny, the Self and the ghost enter a tomb, and one judges by that.

**《卜筮正宗·赋文三篇·何知章》原文** — 《卜筮正宗》第 3570 行 · *Bushi Zhengzong (The Orthodox Line of Divination), "Three rhapsodies: the "how to know" rhyme", base text; Bushi Zhengzong, line 3570*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 何知人家病要死？ | 怎么知道这家有人病得要死？ | How can one know that someone in a household is ill unto death? |
| 用神无救又入墓； | 用神没有救护，又入了墓； | the Subject has no rescue and enters a tomb; |

**《卜筮正宗·赋文三篇·何知章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 9845 行 · *Bushi Zhengzong (The Orthodox Line of Divination), "Three rhapsodies: the "how to know" rhyme", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 9845*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 何知人家病要死？ | 怎么知道这家有人病得要死？ | How can one know that someone in a household is ill unto death? |
| 身命世鬼入墓推。 | 身、命、世、鬼入了墓，就这样推断。 | If the Body, the destiny, the Self and the ghost enter a tomb, judge it so. |

> **打架**:凭什么断病要死：卜筮正宗答「用神无救又入墓」，只看用神一爻；卜筮大全答「身命世鬼入墓推」，看身、命、世、鬼四样入墓。两本不一样，书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** What decides that the illness is unto death: the Bushi Zhengzong answers 'the Subject has no rescue and enters a tomb', looking only at the Subject line; the Bushi Daquan answers 'the Body, the destiny, the Self and the ghost enter a tomb', looking at four things entering a tomb. The two texts differ and the books do not decide.

> **按**:「何知章」一问一答，一共问了三四十件事（父母子孙的病、有没有子孙、讼事、财富、田地、屋宇、坟墓、六畜、口舌、盗贼……），都是一家的事；这里只收与病、死、鬼怪有关的。卜筮大全里它是「十六、何知章」，卜筮正宗里它在《赋文三篇》中；两本字句略有出入。「身命世鬼」的「身」按其他章的说法是卦身，「命」在这里没有再解释，按字面译作命。用神入墓的说法在增删卜易里有更多的讨论（LY.ILL.ZS.07：动墓绝、化墓绝须逢生旺）。
>
> **Note:** The 'how to know' rhyme asks and answers some thirty or forty things (illnesses of parents and children, whether there are children, lawsuits, wealth, land, houses, graves, livestock, quarrels, thieves and more), all affairs of a household; only those to do with illness, death and ghostly happenings are taken here. In the Bushi Daquan it is chapter 16, 'the how-to-know rhyme'; in the Bushi Zhengzong it is among the three rhapsodies; the two texts differ a little in wording. The 'Body' of 'Body, destiny, Self and ghost' is, by the other chapters' usage, the hexagram Body, and 'destiny' is not explained here and is rendered literally. The Subject entering a tomb is discussed at more length in the Zengshan Buyi (LY.ILL.ZS.07: moving into or changing into a tomb or extinction needs generation and strength).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 9845 行:何知人家病要死
- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 3570 行:何知人家病要死

相关:LY.ILL.BSZZ.35、LY.ILL.BSZZ.37、LY.ILL.ZS.07

## LY.ILL.CA.03 凶卦与化爻：丰蛊困明夷夬同人一例推（冬旅春需、夏观秋剥并倾危）；六爻安静尤难瘥、杀鬼交重便莫医；财旺身空身必丧、官生命死命应衰；两重官鬼因劳复；鬼化兄病不宜、化财凶有准、化子瘥无疑

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:丰、蛊、困、明夷，夬和同人，一样推断；冬天的旅、春天的需，都困重；夏天的观、秋天的剥，都倾危。六爻安静，尤其难痊；杀和鬼交重，就不要医。财旺而身空，身一定丧；官生而命死，命应衰。两重官鬼，是因劳累复发；鬼化为兄，病不宜；鬼化为财，凶有准；鬼爻化子，痊愈无疑。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Feng together with Gu, Kun (Confinement) and Mingyi, and Guai and Tongren: reason the same for all; in winter Lü, in spring Xu: all are trapped and heavy; in summer Guan, in autumn Bo: all topple and are in danger. With the six lines still it is still harder to recover; with killing and ghost crossing heavily one cannot treat it. Wealth strong and the Body Void: the body will surely be lost; the Pressure line alive and the natal line dead: life is surely weak. Two Pressure lines: a relapse from overwork; a ghost changing into Peer: the illness is not favourable; a ghost changing into Wealth: ill is certain; a Pressure line changing into Output: recovery is beyond doubt.

**《阐奥歌章·阐奥歌章·二十二、疾病章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3986 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Chan'ao Gezhang, chapter 22: illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3986*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 丰兼蛊困及明夷， | 丰，加上蛊、困和明夷， | Feng together with Gu, Kun (Confinement) and Mingyi, |
| 夬与同人一例推。 | 夬和同人，一样推断。 | and Guai and Tongren: reason the same for all. |
| 冬旅春需都困重， | 冬天的旅、春天的需，都困重； | in winter Lü, in spring Xu: all are trapped and heavy; |
| 夏观秋剥并倾危。 | 夏天的观、秋天的剥，都倾危。 | in summer Guan, in autumn Bo: all topple and are in danger. |
| 六爻安静尤难瘥， | 六爻安静，尤其难痊； | With the six lines still it is still harder to recover; |
| 杀鬼交重便莫医。 | 杀和鬼交重，就不要医了。 | with killing and ghost crossing heavily one cannot treat it. |
| 财旺身空身必丧， | 财旺而身空，身一定丧； | Wealth strong and the Body Void: the body will surely be lost; |
| 官生命死命应衰。 | 官生而命死，命应衰弱。 | the Pressure line alive and the natal line dead: life is surely weak. |
| 两重官鬼因劳复， | 两重官鬼，是因劳累而复发； | Two Pressure lines: a relapse from overwork; |
| 鬼化为兄病不宜。 | 鬼化为兄，病不适宜； | a ghost changing into Peer: the illness is not favourable; |
| 鬼化为财凶有准。 | 鬼化为财，凶是有准的； | a ghost changing into Wealth: ill is certain; |
| 鬼爻化子瘥无疑。 | 鬼爻化为子，痊愈无疑。 | a Pressure line changing into Output: recovery is beyond doubt. |

> **打架**:哪几个卦占病最凶，几本书的名单不同。阐奥歌章这一首（LY.ILL.CA.03）是丰、蛊、困、明夷、夬、同人一例，冬旅、春需、夏观、秋剥；第四首（LY.ILL.CA.06）是明夷、蛊、夬、剥、丰、同，第五首是丰、观、需、节、旅、贲、临、明夷、蛊、夬、同人；天玄赋（LY.ILL.TXF.06）是明夷、观、贲、需、临（世身入墓）和大畜、丰、同、蛊、夬（财鬼俱兴）；断易天机的卜筮元龟一段（LY.ILL.DYTJ.15）是丰、观、需、剥、节、旅、贲、明夷、蛊、夬、同人「十死分明」。名单大同小异：丰、蛊、夬、同人、明夷几乎处处都在，观、需、贲、旅、剥、节、临、大畜则有的有、有的没有。书里没有裁决。周易尚占（LY.ILL.ZSZ.02 至 ZSZ.05）和文王金钱课（LY.ILL.WWJQ.03、WWJQ.05）也按卦名断病，和这个名单有出入：观，周易尚占说「财聚病痊」，文王金钱课说「占病即愈」「疾病皆除」；丰，文王金钱课说「疾病渐好」，周易尚占说「病人沉滞」；夬，周易尚占说「病讼迟延」，文王金钱课补录部分说「疾病除根」；蛊、困、明夷三卦，周易尚占也说病凶或「件件遭伤」，和这个名单一致（文王金钱课对这三卦没有讲病）。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Which hexagrams are worst in divining illness is listed differently by several books. This poem of the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.03) has Feng, Gu, Kun, Mingyi, Guai and Tongren alike, with Lü in winter, Xu in spring, Guan in summer and Bo in autumn; its fourth poem (LY.ILL.CA.06) has Mingyi, Gu, Guai, Bo, Feng and Tong, and its fifth Feng, Guan, Xu, Jie, Lü, Bi, Lin, Mingyi, Gu, Guai and Tongren; the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.06) has Mingyi, Guan, Bi, Xu and Lin (the Self and Body entering a tomb) and Daxu, Feng, Tong, Gu and Guai (Wealth and the ghost both rising); the Bushi Yuangui passage in the Duanyi Tianji (LY.ILL.DYTJ.15) has Feng, Guan, Xu, Bo, Jie, Lü, Bi, Mingyi, Gu, Guai and Tongren, 'the ten deaths plain'. The lists are much alike with small differences: Feng, Gu, Guai, Tongren and Mingyi are almost everywhere, and Guan, Xu, Bi, Lü, Bo, Jie, Lin and Daxu are in some and not in others. The books do not settle it. The Zhouyi Shangzhan (LY.ILL.ZSZ.02 to ZSZ.05) and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke (LY.ILL.WWJQ.03, WWJQ.05) also judge illness by the name of the hexagram, and differ from this list: for Guan (Viewing) the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'wealth gathers and the illness is cured' and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'divining illness, it is cured at once' and 'illness all removed'; for Feng (Abundance) the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'the illness gradually gets better' and the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the sick are held fast'; for Guai (Breakthrough) the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'illness and lawsuit are delayed' and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke, in its supplementary part, says 'the illness is rooted out'; for Gu (Decay), Kun (Confinement) and Mingyi (Brightness Wounded) the Zhouyi Shangzhan also says the illness is ominous or 'every matter meets injury', which agrees with this list (the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says nothing of illness for these three).

> **按**:这是第一首的末六联。「困」是困卦，按「Kun （Confinement）」译；「旅」「需」「观」「剥」是卦名，前面加季节：冬旅、春需、夏观、秋剥，是说这几个卦在这个季节占病特别凶（按季节各有一个卦）。「官生命死命应衰」文字费解，按字面译：官（鬼）在长生、命在死。「两重官鬼因劳复」的「劳复」是病愈后因劳累而复发（中医词）。「六爻安静尤难瘥」和天玄赋「卦爻安静，重为济世之人」（LY.ILL.TXF.14）、野鹤「六爻安静、卦吉可以调理」（LY.ILL.ZS.22）正好相反：这里说安静更难愈——三家说法不同，书里没有裁决。周易尚占的疾厄部（第 787–790 行）里这一段有两处字不同：「六爻安静尤难瘥」写作「六爻安静犹难瘥」（「犹」，不是「尤」，意思差不多，都是还更难愈）；「鬼化为财凶有准」写作「官化为财凶有定」（官和鬼在这里是同一个爻；「有定」「有准」都是说一定）。
>
> **Note:** These are the last six couplets of the first poem. 'Kun' is the hexagram Kun (Confinement); 'Lü', 'Xu', 'Guan' and 'Bo' are hexagram names, each given a season: Lü in winter, Xu in spring, Guan in summer, Bo in autumn, meaning that these hexagrams are especially ominous in divining illness in that season (one hexagram for each season). 'The Pressure line alive and the natal line dead' is hard to understand and is rendered literally: the Pressure line at Birth and the natal line at Death. The 'relapse' of 'two Pressure lines: a relapse from overwork' is illness returning through exertion after recovery (a term of Chinese medicine). 'With the six lines still it is still harder to recover' is exactly the opposite of the Tianxuan Fu's 'the hexagram and lines still, weighty is he who saves the world' (LY.ILL.TXF.14) and the Old Man of Yehe's 'six lines still and the hexagram auspicious, it can be treated by nursing' (LY.ILL.ZS.22): here it says that stillness makes recovery harder — the three differ, and the books do not settle it. In the Zhouyi Shangzhan's section on illness and affliction (lines 787–790) this passage differs in two places: 'with the six lines still it is still harder to recover' is written with a different word for 'still' (the sense is about the same: harder still to recover); and 'a ghost changing into Wealth: ill is certain' is written 'Pressure changing into Wealth: ill is fixed' (Pressure and ghost are the same line here; 'fixed' and 'certain' both say it is sure).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《周易尚占》:同文 第 787 行:丰兼蛊困及明夷;同文 第 787 行:夬与同人一例推;同文 第 788 行:冬旅春需都困重;同文 第 788 行:夏观秋剥并倾危;异文(相似 0.86) 第 788 行:六爻安静犹难瘥;同文 第 788 行:杀鬼交重便莫医;同文 第 789 行:财旺身空身必丧;同文 第 789 行:官生命死命应衰;同文 第 789 行:两重官鬼因劳复;同文 第 789 行:鬼化为兄病不宜;同文 第 790 行:鬼爻化子瘥无疑
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1360 行:丰兼蛊困及明夷;同文 第 1360 行:夬与同人一例推;同文 第 1360 行:冬旅春需都困重;同文 第 1360 行:夏观秋剥并倾危;同文 第 1360 行:六爻安静尤难瘥;同文 第 1360 行:杀鬼交重便莫医;同文 第 1360 行:财旺身空身必丧;同文 第 1360 行:官生命死命应衰;同文 第 1360 行:两重官鬼因劳复;同文 第 1360 行:鬼化为兄病不宜;同文 第 1360 行:鬼化为财凶有准;同文 第 1360 行:鬼爻化子瘥无疑

相关:LY.ILL.CA.02、LY.ILL.CA.04、LY.ILL.TXF.06、LY.ILL.TXF.14、LY.ILL.ZS.22、LY.ILL.YY.06、LY.ILL.ZSZ.02、LY.ILL.ZSZ.03、LY.ILL.ZSZ.04、LY.ILL.ZSZ.05、LY.ILL.WWJQ.03、LY.ILL.WWJQ.05

## LY.ILL.CA.06 占病凶卦二首：明夷蛊夬剥丰同六卦哪堪占病，财鬼二爻俱发动丧门吊客闹丛丛；丰观需节旅贲临明夷蛊夬及同人占病若逢此卦归家急急办前程

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:明夷、蛊、夬、剥、丰、同（人）这六个卦，怎么经得起占病的凶；财、鬼两爻都发动，丧门、吊客热闹地聚在一起。丰、观、需、节、旅、贲、临，明夷、蛊、夬和同人，占病如果遇到这些卦，赶紧回家去办后事。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Mingyi, Gu, Guai, Bo, Feng and Tong(ren): how can these six hexagrams bear the ill of divining illness; with Wealth and the ghost both moving, Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence crowd noisily together. Feng, Guan, Xu, Jie, Lü, Bi and Lin, Mingyi, Gu, Guai and Tongren: if in divining illness one meets these hexagrams, hurry home and make ready for what lies ahead.

**《阐奥歌章·阐奥歌章·二十二、疾病章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3998 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Chan'ao Gezhang, chapter 22: illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3998*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 明夷蛊夬剥丰同， | 明夷、蛊、夬、剥、丰、同（人）， | Mingyi, Gu, Guai, Bo, Feng and Tong(ren): |
| 六卦哪堪占病凶。 | 这六个卦怎么经得起占病的凶； | how can these six hexagrams bear the ill of divining illness; |
| 财鬼二爻俱发动， | 财、鬼两爻都发动， | with Wealth and the ghost both moving, |
| 丧门吊客闹丛丛。 | 丧门、吊客热闹地聚在一起。 | Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence crowd noisily together. |

**《阐奥歌章·阐奥歌章·二十二、疾病章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 4001 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Chan'ao Gezhang, chapter 22: illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4001*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 丰观需节旅贲临， | 丰、观、需、节、旅、贲、临， | Feng, Guan, Xu, Jie, Lü, Bi (Adornment), Lin, |
| 明夷蛊夬及同人。 | 明夷、蛊、夬和同人； | Mingyi, Gu, Guai and Tongren: |
| 占病若还逢此卦， | 占病如果遇到这些卦， | if in divining illness one meets these hexagrams, |
| 归家急急办前程。 | 赶紧回家去办后事。 | hurry home and make ready for what lies ahead. |

> **打架**:周易尚占和文王金钱课按卦名断病，和第五首这个名单有出入：观，周易尚占说「财聚病痊」，文王金钱课说「占病即愈」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.02、WWJQ.03）；贲，周易尚占说「病安」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.03）；节，文王金钱课说「疾病口舌消除安」（LY.ILL.WWJQ.03）；丰，文王金钱课说「疾病渐好」，周易尚占说「病人沉滞」（LY.ILL.WWJQ.03、ZSZ.04）；夬，周易尚占说「病讼迟延」，文王金钱课补录部分说「疾病除根」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.04、WWJQ.05）；临，周易尚占说「病犯崇侵」，文王金钱课补录部分说「疾病即痊」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.07、WWJQ.05）；蛊、明夷，周易尚占不说吉，和名单一致（LY.ILL.ZSZ.05）；文王金钱课对这两卦没有讲病。
>
> **Where they disagree:** The Zhouyi Shangzhan and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke judge illness by the name of the hexagram and differ from the list of the fifth poem: for Guan the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'wealth gathers and the illness is cured' and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'divining illness, it is cured at once' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.02, WWJQ.03); for Bi (Adornment) the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the illness is settled' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.03); for Jie (Restraint) the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'illness and quarrels are removed and all is at peace' (LY.ILL.WWJQ.03); for Feng the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'the illness gradually gets better' and the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the sick are held fast' (LY.ILL.WWJQ.03, ZSZ.04); for Guai the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'illness and lawsuit are delayed' and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke, in its supplementary part, says 'the illness is rooted out' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.04, WWJQ.05); for Lin (Approach) the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the illness offends a curse that attacks' and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke, in its supplementary part, says 'the illness is cured at once' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.07, WWJQ.05); for Gu and Mingyi the Zhouyi Shangzhan speaks of no good fortune, which agrees with the list (LY.ILL.ZSZ.05), and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says nothing of illness for these two.

> **按**:这是第四、五首。两首列的凶卦名单和第一首（LY.ILL.CA.03）、天玄赋（LY.ILL.TXF.06）不完全一样，名单的比较见 LY.ILL.CA.03 的「打架」一栏。「同」「节」「旅」「贲」「丰」等是卦名，「同」按上下文是同人。「丧门吊客闹丛丛」的「丛丛」是热闹聚在一起的样子。「归家急急办前程」的「办前程」按字面译作「make ready for what lies ahead」，意思是赶紧回家料理后事。
>
> **Note:** These are the fourth and fifth poems. The lists of ominous hexagrams in the two poems are not exactly the same as in the first poem (LY.ILL.CA.03) and the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.06); the comparison of the lists is in the 'dispute' field of LY.ILL.CA.03. 'Tong', 'Jie', 'Lü', 'Bi', 'Feng' and so on are hexagram names, and 'Tong' is Tongren from the context. The 'cong cong' of 'Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence crowd noisily' is a crowd gathering noisily. 'Ban qian cheng' in 'go home in haste and make ready for what lies ahead' is rendered literally as 'make ready for what lies ahead', meaning to hurry home and see to the funeral. 

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 1595 行:财鬼二爻俱发动
- 《火珠林》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 1360 行:明夷蛊□剥丰同;同文 第 1360 行:六卦哪堪占病凶;同文 第 1360 行:财鬼二爻俱发动;同文 第 1360 行:丧门吊客闹丛丛
- 《断易天机》:同文 第 1686 行:明夷蛊夬及同人
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1360 行:丰观需节旅贲临;同文 第 1360 行:明夷蛊夬及同人;同文 第 1360 行:占病若还逢此卦;同文 第 1360 行:归家急急办前程

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.06 孙膑断疾病歌(五)：明夷蛊剥夬丰同六卦占病不可逢；财鬼二爻俱发动、丧门吊客闹匆匆；六重白虎临身恶；鬼爻并爻应发动、化蛇入库身终

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:明夷、蛊、剥、夬、丰、同这六个卦，占病时哪里愿意遇上；财、鬼两爻都发动，丧门、吊客闹哄哄地赶来；六重白虎临在身上凶恶，若在别的爻上也不好；鬼爻和应爻一起发动，化成螣蛇、入了墓库，身命就终了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Mingyi, Gu, Bo, Guai, Feng and Tong: who would wish to meet these six hexagrams in divining illness? With Wealth and the ghost both moving, Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence crowd in noisily and in haste; a six-fold White Tiger sitting on the Body is vicious, and if it is on another line it is also not good; the Pressure line and the Other line moving together, changing into the Coiling Snake and entering the tomb, the body's life ends.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·孙膑断疾病歌》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1595 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Sun Bin's song on judging illness", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 1595*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 明夷蛊剥夬丰同， | 明夷、蛊、剥、夬、丰、同（人）， | Mingyi, Gu, Bo, Guai, Feng and Tong(ren): |
| 六卦哪须占病逢， | 这六个卦，占病时哪里愿意遇上； | who would wish to meet these six hexagrams in divining illness; |
| 财鬼二爻俱发动， | 财、鬼两爻都发动， | with Wealth and the ghost both moving, |
| 丧门吊客闹匆匆。 | 丧门、吊客闹哄哄地赶来。 | Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence crowd in noisily and in haste. |
| 六重白虎临身恶， | 六重白虎临在身上，凶恶； | a six-fold White Tiger sitting on the Body is vicious; |
| 若在他爻亦不中， | 若在别的爻上，也不好； | if it is on another line it is also not good; |
| 鬼爻并爻应发动， | 鬼爻和应爻一起发动， | the Pressure line and the Other line moving together, |
| 化蛇入库的身终（2）。 | 化成螣蛇、入了墓库，身命就终了。 | changing into the Coiling Snake and entering the tomb, the body's life ends. |

> **按**:「同」是同人卦的简称。这六个卦和阐奥歌章、天玄赋、卜筮元龟的凶卦名单大同小异，见 LY.ILL.CA.03 的「打架」。「六卦哪须占病逢」的「哪须」，这里按「哪里愿意」译（意思是占病时不想遇上）；字面也可读成「何须」，书里没有说明。「六重白虎」字面译作 six-fold，「六重」是什么意思书里没有说。「鬼爻并爻应发动」的「并爻应」读不通顺，这里按「并应爻」译，即鬼爻和应爻一起动。「（2）」是今人的注号，那条注不收。「化蛇入库」是化成螣蛇、又入了墓库（辰戌丑未），原文没有说是什么爻。
>
> **Note:** 'Tong' is short for the Tongren hexagram. These six hexagrams are much like the lists of ominous hexagrams in the Chan'ao Gezhang, the Tianxuan Fu and the Bushi Yuangui, see the dispute in LY.ILL.CA.03. In 'who would wish to meet them in divining illness' the 'na xu' is rendered 'who would wish' (meaning one does not want to meet them in divining illness); it can also be read literally as 'why need', and the book does not explain it. 'Six-fold White Tiger' is literal, and the book does not say what 'six-fold' means. 'Gui yao bing yao ying fa dong' does not read smoothly in 'bing yao ying'; it is rendered 'bing ying yao', the Pressure line and the Other line moving together. The '(2)' is a modern annotation mark and that annotation is not quoted. 'Changing into the Snake and entering the tomb' is changing into the Coiling Snake and also entering a tomb (Chen, Xu, Chou, Wei), and the original does not say which line.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3998 行:财鬼二爻俱发动
- 《火珠林》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 1360 行:明夷蛊□剥丰同;同文 第 1360 行:财鬼二爻俱发动

相关:LY.ILL.CA.03、LY.ILL.TXF.06、LY.ILL.TXF.09、LY.ILL.DYTJ.15、LY.ILL.DYTJ.07、LY.ILL.HDY.11、LY.ILL.ZSZ.04、LY.ILL.ZSZ.05

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.07 孙膑断疾病歌(六)：卦变墓乡须丧命、不然亦主困蒙蒙；乾坎震巽为生魂在外不死内难存、离坤艮兑人为鬼在内定生在外死；勾陈爻动即开坟、乾兑须臾为孝子

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦变到墓乡，必定丧命，不然也主昏沉困顿。乾、坎、震、巽是生魂，在外不死，在内难活；离、坤、艮、兑是人变成鬼，在内一定活，在外就死。勾陈爻动，就要开坟了；乾、兑，片刻之间就有人做孝子。孙膑这个诀很难领会，只在于善于推算。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the hexagram changes into the tomb quarter life will surely be lost, and otherwise it also means a dull, hazy exhaustion. Qian, Kan, Zhen and Xun are the living soul: outside he does not die, inside he can hardly survive; Li, Kun, Gen and Dui are the person becoming a ghost: inside he surely lives, outside he dies. When the Hook Earth line moves the grave is opened; Qian and Dui: in a moment someone is a filial mourner. Sun Bin's secret here is hard to know and lies only in being good at working it out.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·孙膑断疾病歌》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1595 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Sun Bin's song on judging illness", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 1595*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦变墓乡须丧命， | 卦变到墓乡，必定丧命； | If the hexagram changes into the tomb quarter life will surely be lost; |
| 不然亦主困蒙蒙， | 不然也主昏沉困顿； | otherwise too it means a dull, hazy exhaustion; |
| 乾坎震巽为生魂， | 乾、坎、震、巽是生魂， | Qian, Kan, Zhen and Xun are the living soul: |
| 在外不死内难存。 | 在外不死，在内难活。 | outside he does not die, inside he can hardly survive. |
| 离坤艮兑人为鬼， | 离、坤、艮、兑，人成了鬼； | Li, Kun, Gen and Dui: the person becomes a ghost; |
| 在内定生在外死， | 在内一定活，在外就死； | inside he surely lives, outside he dies; |
| 勾陈爻动即开坟， | 勾陈爻动，就要开坟了； | when the Hook Earth line moves the grave is opened; |
| 乾兑须臾为孝子。 | 乾、兑，片刻之间就有人做孝子。 | Qian and Dui: in a moment someone is a filial mourner. |
| 孙膑此诀须难知， | 孙膑这个诀，很难领会； | Sun Bin's secret here is hard to know; |
| 惟在善推而已矣。 | 只在于善于推算罢了。 | it lies only in being good at working it out. |

> **按**:「生魂」「鬼」是这首歌自己对八个卦的分类：乾、坎、震、巽为一类，离、坤、艮、兑为另一类；「在外」「在内」指什么，书里没有说，可能指这个卦是落在外卦还是内卦，也可能指鬼所在的位置，这里按字面译，不作选择。这套分法别的书里没有，这里只有这一首。「乾兑须臾为孝子」的「孝子」是披麻戴孝的人，也就是有人死了。「须臾」是片刻。「困蒙蒙」按「昏沉困顿」译。这一首的尾两句是收束，说孙膑的诀难懂，要靠善于推算。
>
> **Note:** 'Living soul' and 'ghost' are this song's own sorting of the eight trigrams: Qian, Kan, Zhen and Xun in one class, Li, Kun, Gen and Dui in the other. The book does not say what 'outside' and 'inside' refer to; it may be whether the trigram falls in the outer or the inner trigram of the hexagram, or the position of the ghost, and it is rendered literally here without choosing. This way of sorting is not found in the other books, only in this song. The 'filial mourner' of 'Qian and Dui in a moment a filial mourner' is a person in mourning dress, that is, someone has died. 'Xu yu' is a moment. 'Kun meng meng' is rendered 'a dull, hazy exhaustion'. The last two lines close the song, saying that Sun Bin's secret is hard to know and depends on being good at working it out.

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.06、LY.ILL.TXF.06、LY.ILL.HDY.11、LY.ILL.DYTJ.15

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.15 卜筮元龟·占疾病凶卦：丰观需剥节旅贲明夷蛊夬及同人，不问四时及生旺，十死分明

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:丰、观、需、剥、节、旅、贲、明夷、蛊、夬，还有同人，不论四季、也不论生旺，「十死」是分明的，不必多说。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Feng, Guan, Xu, Bo, Jie, Lü, Bi, Mingyi, Gu, Guai and Tongren: regardless of the four seasons or of strength, the 'ten deaths' are plain and there is no need to say more.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·卜筮元龟》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1685–1686 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Bushi Yuangui", base text; Duanyi Tianji, lines 1685–1686*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占疾病凶卦云： | 占疾病的凶卦，歌诀说： | On the ominous hexagrams in divining illness it says: |
| 丰观需剥节旅贲， | 丰、观、需、剥、节、旅、贲， | Feng, Guan, Xu, Bo, Jie, Lü and Bi, |
| 明夷蛊夬及同人， | 明夷、蛊、夬，还有同人， | Mingyi, Gu, Guai and Tongren: |
| 不问四时及生旺， | 不论四季，也不论生旺， | regardless of the four seasons or of strength, |
| 十死分明不须陈。 | 「十死」是分明的，不必多说。 | the 'ten deaths' are plain and there is no need to say more. |

> **打架**:哪几个卦占病最凶，几本书的名单不同。这一块是丰、观、需、剥、节、旅、贲、明夷、蛊、夬、同人；阐奥歌章第五首（LY.ILL.CA.06）也是这十一个；孙膑断疾病歌（LY.ILL.DYTJ.06）只有明夷、蛊、剥、夬、丰、同六个；天玄赋（LY.ILL.TXF.06）是明夷、观、贲、需、临（世身入墓）和大畜、丰、同、蛊、夬（财鬼俱兴）；火珠林一段（LY.ILL.DYTJ.17）是同人、丰、夬、蛊、剥、明夷。丰、蛊、夬、同人、明夷几乎处处都在，观、需、贲、旅、剥、节、临、大畜则有的有、有的没有。书里没有裁决。周易尚占和文王金钱课按卦名断病，和这个名单有出入：观，周易尚占说「财聚病痊」，文王金钱课说「占病即愈」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.02、WWJQ.03）；贲，周易尚占说「病安」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.03）；节，文王金钱课说「疾病口舌消除安」（LY.ILL.WWJQ.03）；丰，文王金钱课说「疾病渐好」，周易尚占说「病人沉滞」（LY.ILL.WWJQ.03、ZSZ.04）；夬，周易尚占说「病讼迟延」，文王金钱课补录部分说「疾病除根」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.04、WWJQ.05）；蛊、明夷，周易尚占不说吉，和名单一致（LY.ILL.ZSZ.05）；文王金钱课对这两卦没有讲病。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Which hexagrams are worst in divining illness is listed differently by several books. This block has Feng, Guan, Xu, Bo, Jie, Lü, Bi, Mingyi, Gu, Guai and Tongren; the fifth poem of the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.06) has the same eleven; Sun Bin's song (LY.ILL.DYTJ.06) has only the six Mingyi, Gu, Bo, Guai, Feng and Tong; the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.06) has Mingyi, Guan, Bi, Xu and Lin (the Self and Body entering a tomb) and Daxu, Feng, Tong, Gu and Guai (Wealth and the ghost both rising); the Huozhu Lin passage (LY.ILL.DYTJ.17) has Tongren, Feng, Guai, Gu, Bo and Mingyi. Feng, Gu, Guai, Tongren and Mingyi are almost everywhere, and Guan, Xu, Bi, Lü, Bo, Jie, Lin and Daxu are in some and not in others. The books do not settle it. The Zhouyi Shangzhan and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke judge illness by the name of the hexagram and differ from this list: for Guan the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'wealth gathers and the illness is cured' and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'divining illness, it is cured at once' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.02, WWJQ.03); for Bi the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the illness is settled' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.03); for Jie the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'illness and quarrels are removed and all is at peace' (LY.ILL.WWJQ.03); for Feng the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'the illness gradually gets better' and the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the sick are held fast' (LY.ILL.WWJQ.03, ZSZ.04); for Guai the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'illness and lawsuit are delayed' and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke, in its supplementary part, says 'the illness is rooted out' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.04, WWJQ.05); for Gu and Mingyi the Zhouyi Shangzhan speaks of no good fortune, which agrees with the list (LY.ILL.ZSZ.05), and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says nothing of illness for these two.

> **按**:名单里有十一个卦（丰、观、需、剥、节、旅、贲、明夷、蛊、夬、同人），而说的是「十死」，「十死」按字面译作 ten deaths，可能是「十有九死」一类的说法，书里没有解释。「不问四时及生旺」是说不看季节、也不看这个卦的旺衰，遇上就是凶。这个名单和别处的凶卦名单大同小异，见 LY.ILL.CA.03 的「打架」。
>
> **Note:** The list has eleven hexagrams (Feng, Guan, Xu, Bo, Jie, Lü, Bi, Mingyi, Gu, Guai, Tongren) while it speaks of 'ten deaths'; 'ten deaths' is rendered literally and may be a saying like 'nine out of ten die', and the book does not explain it. 'Regardless of the four seasons or of strength' says that neither the season nor the strength or weakness of the hexagram is looked at, and meeting one is ominous. This list is much like the lists of ominous hexagrams elsewhere, see the dispute in LY.ILL.CA.03.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 4001 行:丰观需节旅贲;同文 第 4001 行:明夷蛊夬及同人
- 《火珠林》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 1360 行:丰观需节旅贲;同文 第 1360 行:明夷蛊夬及同人

相关:LY.ILL.CA.03、LY.ILL.CA.06、LY.ILL.DYTJ.06、LY.ILL.TXF.06、LY.ILL.DYTJ.17、LY.ILL.ZSZ.02、LY.ILL.ZSZ.03、LY.ILL.ZSZ.04、LY.ILL.ZSZ.05、LY.ILL.WWJQ.03、LY.ILL.WWJQ.05

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.17 火珠林(断易天机引)·疾病财爻鬼莫连：二爻俱发命危难；同人丰夬蛊剥明夷是死缘；杀世鬼身三入墓人将魂魄入黄泉；药爻在世难医疗；虎动占身病未痊；卦入墓乡并丧命

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占疾病，财爻和鬼不要连在一起，两爻都发动，命有危难；同人、丰、夬成了凶的一对，蛊、剥、明夷是死的缘由。杀、世、鬼、身，三样入了墓，人的魂魄就要入黄泉；药爻在世，还是难医；虎动、占住身，病没有痊愈；卦入墓乡，并且丧命，卧在床席上，病绵绵不断。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In illness the Wealth line and the ghost must not be linked, and if both move life is in danger; Tongren, Feng and Guai make an ominous set, and Gu, Bo and Mingyi are causes of death. The killing, the Self, the ghost and the Body, three of them entering the tomb, the person's spirit-souls are about to enter the Yellow Springs; the medicine line on the Self, hard to treat; the Tiger moving and taking hold of the Body, the illness is not cured; the hexagram entering the tomb quarter, life is lost, and lying on the bed-mat the illness drags on and on.

**《断易天机·占疾病第二十四·火珠林》原文** — 《断易天机》第 1720 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Section 24, on illness: Huozhu Lin", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 1720*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 疾病财爻鬼莫连， | 占疾病，财爻和鬼不要连在一起； | In illness the Wealth line and the ghost must not be linked; |
| 二爻俱发命危难， | 两爻都发动，命有危难； | if both move, life is in danger; |
| 同人丰夬成凶对， | 同人、丰、夬成了凶的一对， | Tongren, Feng and Guai make an ominous set, |
| 蛊剥明夷是死缘。 | 蛊、剥、明夷是死的缘由。 | and Gu, Bo and Mingyi are causes of death. |
| 杀世鬼身三入墓， | 杀、世、鬼、身，三样入了墓， | the killing, the Self, the ghost and the Body, three of them entering the tomb, |
| 人将魂魄入黄泉， | 人的魂魄就要入黄泉； | the person's spirit-souls are about to enter the Yellow Springs; |
| 药爻在世难医疗， | 药爻在世，难医疗； | the medicine line on the Self, hard to treat; |
| 虎动占身病未痊。 | 虎动，占住身，病没有痊愈； | the Tiger moving and taking hold of the Body, the illness is not cured; |
| 卦入墓乡并丧命， | 卦入墓乡，并且丧命， | the hexagram entering the tomb quarter, life is lost, |
| 卧于床席病绵绵。 | 卧在床席上，病绵绵不断。 | and lying on the bed-mat the illness drags on and on. |

> **按**:这一首在断易天机里题作《火珠林》，和火珠林本书的「占疾病」（LY.ILL.HZL.01 起）是不同的文字。「杀世鬼身三入墓」读不确定：是杀、世、鬼、身三样入墓，还是别的断法，原文没有点明，这里按字面译。「药爻在世难医疗」按字面译，上一句已经说入墓，这一句大概是说即使药爻（子孙）持世也难医，是读法，原文没有「即使」二字。这一首的凶卦名单是同人、丰、夬、蛊、剥、明夷，和别处的名单大同小异，见 LY.ILL.CA.03 的「打架」。
>
> **Note:** This poem is headed 'Huozhu Lin' in the Duanyi Tianji, and it is a different text from the 'divining illness' of the Huozhu Lin itself (from LY.ILL.HZL.01). 'Sha shi gui shen san ru mu' is not certain: whether it is the killing, the Self, the ghost and the Body, three of them entering the tomb, or some other way of judging, the original does not make plain, and it is rendered literally. 'The medicine line on the Self, hard to treat' is rendered literally; the line before has already said entering the tomb, and this line probably says that even with the medicine line (Output) holding the Self it is hard to treat, which is a reading, and the original has no word for 'even'. The list of ominous hexagrams of this poem is Tongren, Feng, Guai, Gu, Bo and Mingyi, much like the lists elsewhere, see the dispute in LY.ILL.CA.03.

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.15、LY.ILL.DYTJ.06、LY.ILL.CA.03、LY.ILL.HZL.01、LY.ILL.ZSZ.04、LY.ILL.ZSZ.05

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.23 论墓字如何取用：辰戌丑未五行之库、凶爻要入吉爻不可墓；人墓、事墓、鬼墓；占病得鬼墓为庙神、占病墓旺鬼难脱病难安多死、鬼是庙神墓旺乃太庙之神；墓要冲克破即解神

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:辰、戌、丑、未是五行的库（墓）：凶爻要入库，吉爻不可入墓，入墓就成了休囚。墓有三种：人墓、事墓、鬼墓。人墓：比如占兄弟的病，墓在辰，当日是戌，虽然合着入了墓，也不会死；人旺墓衰，不会死；兄弟旺、墓爻衰，不会死。鬼墓：占病得到鬼墓，鬼是庙里的神；占病而墓旺，鬼难脱身、病难安、多半死；占病时鬼是庙神，若墓旺，就是大庙的神，不是小小的神祠。墓这个字，要冲克破它，这是按墓爻的活法来论，也就是解神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei are the storehouses (tombs) of the five phases: a fierce line should enter one, a lucky line must not be in a tomb, and in a tomb it is resting or trapped. There are three tombs: the person's tomb, the matter's tomb and the ghost's tomb. The person's tomb: for example divining a brother's illness, the tomb is Chen and the day is Xu; though it combines and enters the tomb he does not die; if the person is strong and the tomb weak he does not die; if the brother is strong and the tomb line weak he does not die. The ghost's tomb: divining illness and obtaining the ghost's tomb, the ghost is a temple god; divining illness with the tomb strong, the ghost is hard to shake off, the illness hard to settle and mostly ends in death; where the ghost is a temple god in divining illness, if the tomb is strong it is a god of a great temple and not a small shrine. As for the word tomb, it must be clashed, controlled or broken; this is judged by the living method of the tomb line, and it is the resolving spirit.

**《断易天机·论墓字如何取用》原文** — 《断易天机》第 32 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "How to use the word tomb", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 32*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 辰戌丑未， | 辰、戌、丑、未， | Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei, |
| 五行之库， | 是五行的库（墓）； | are the storehouses (tombs) of the five phases: |
| 凶爻要入， | 凶爻要入库， | a fierce line should enter one, |
| 吉爻不可墓， | 吉爻不可入墓， | a lucky line must not be in a tomb, |
| 墓则为休囚。 | 入墓就成了休囚。 | and in a tomb it is resting or trapped. |
| 有人墓、事墓、鬼墓。 | 墓有人墓、事墓、鬼墓三种。 | There are the person's tomb, the matter's tomb and the ghost's tomb. |

**《断易天机·论墓字如何取用》原文** — 《断易天机》第 32 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "How to use the word tomb", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 32*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 何为人墓？ | 什么是人墓？ | What is the person's tomb? |
| 且如占兄弟病，辰为墓，当日是戌， | 比如占兄弟的病，墓在辰，当日是戌， | For example, divining a brother's illness, the tomb is Chen and the day is Xu; |
| 虽合入墓，不死； | 虽然合着入了墓，也不会死； | though it combines and enters the tomb he does not die; |
| 又人旺墓衰，不死； | 又人旺墓衰，不会死； | again, if the person is strong and the tomb weak he does not die; |
| 兄弟旺，墓爻衰，不死。 | 兄弟旺、墓爻衰，不会死。 | if the brother is strong and the tomb line weak he does not die. |

**《断易天机·论墓字如何取用》原文** — 《断易天机》第 32 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "How to use the word tomb", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 32*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 何为鬼墓？ | 什么是鬼墓？ | What is the ghost's tomb? |
| 占病得鬼墓，为庙神； | 占病得到鬼墓，鬼是庙里的神； | Divining illness and obtaining the ghost's tomb, the ghost is a temple god; |

**《断易天机·论墓字如何取用》原文** — 《断易天机》第 32 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "How to use the word tomb", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 32*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病墓旺，鬼难脱，病难安，多死。 | 占病而墓旺，鬼难脱身，病难安，多半死。 | Divining illness with the tomb strong, the ghost is hard to shake off, the illness hard to settle, and mostly it ends in death. |

**《断易天机·论墓字如何取用》原文** — 《断易天机》第 32 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "How to use the word tomb", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 32*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病鬼是庙神，如墓旺，乃太庙之神，非小小神祠。 | 占病时鬼是庙神，若墓旺，就是大庙的神，不是小小的神祠。 | Where the ghost is a temple god in divining illness, if the tomb is strong it is a god of a great temple and not a small shrine. |

**《断易天机·论墓字如何取用》原文** — 《断易天机》第 32 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "How to use the word tomb", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 32*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 墓之一字，要冲克破，此以墓爻活法论之，即解神也。 | 墓这个字，要冲克破它，这是按墓爻的活法来论，也就是解神。 | As for the word tomb, it must be clashed, controlled or broken; this is judged by the living method of the tomb line, and it is the resolving spirit. |

> **按**:这一段是断易天机「问曰：墓字如何取用」的答话，原来的问答一问一答是旺、墓、空三字各一段，这里只收墓字。原文说墓有三种（人墓、事墓、鬼墓），但只把人墓和鬼墓举了例，事墓只说了「所卜之事为难成费力」（占病用不到，不收）。「人墓」是人（用爻）入墓：占兄弟病，兄弟属土（或以辰为墓），当日是戌，虽戌辰相冲、合入墓，也不死；人旺墓衰、兄弟旺墓爻衰也不死。「鬼墓」里「占病得鬼墓，为庙神」是说这一病的鬼入了墓，这个鬼是庙里的神灵（鬼是神灵作祟）；「墓旺」是墓爻旺，鬼在墓里出不来（脱不了），所以病难安。这一节的「鬼墓」和火珠林的三墓（LY.ILL.HZL.01：宫墓、鬼墓、财墓，是世爻遇到某一墓）不是同一个分类：火珠林的鬼墓是鬼爻五行的墓，这里的鬼墓是鬼入墓；两处都说墓与鬼合在一起病重。「占贼」「占讼」「占失物」「占行人」「占财」的几句不在病的范围，不收。「解神」是把凶事解开的神，墓爻被冲克破，也就是解神。
>
> **Note:** This passage is the answer to 'someone asks: how is the word tomb to be used' in the Duanyi Tianji; the questions and answers originally take the three words strong, tomb and Void a passage each, and only the word tomb is taken here. The original says there are three tombs (the person's, the matter's and the ghost's) but gives examples only for the person's and the ghost's, and for the matter's tomb says only 'the thing divined is hard to accomplish and costs effort' (not used in illness, and not taken). The 'person's tomb' is the person (the Subject line) entering a tomb: divining a brother's illness, the tomb is Chen and the day is Xu, and though it combines and enters the tomb he does not die; and if the person is strong and the tomb weak, or the brother strong and the tomb line weak, he does not die. In the 'ghost's tomb', 'divining illness and obtaining the ghost's tomb, the ghost is a temple god' says that the ghost of this illness has entered a tomb, and this ghost is a spirit of a temple (a spirit is bringing the curse); 'the tomb strong' is the tomb line strong, and the ghost cannot get out of the tomb (cannot shake off), and so the illness is hard to settle. The 'ghost's tomb' of this passage and the three tombs of the Huozhu Lin (LY.ILL.HZL.01: the palace tomb, the ghost's tomb and the Wealth tomb, where the Self line meets one of the tombs) are not the same classification: the Huozhu Lin's ghost's tomb is the tomb of the phase of the ghost line, and this passage's ghost's tomb is the ghost entering a tomb; both say that a tomb joined with the ghost makes the illness heavy. The sentences about divining a thief, a lawsuit, lost things, a traveller and wealth are outside illness and are not taken. The 'resolving spirit' is a spirit that unties an ill matter, and a tomb line that is clashed, controlled or broken is the resolving spirit.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 5325 行:有：人墓、事墓、鬼墓

相关:LY.ILL.HZL.01、LY.ILL.TXF.06、LY.ILL.DYTJ.27、LY.ILL.HDY.11

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.25 绝处逢生·占病：用爻与吉神绝处逢生病将死而复活；鬼爻与忌神绝处逢生病欲安而复作

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:绝处逢生：比如申日占卜，卦里的用爻属木，木遇到申就是绝，这木爻就没有用了，和没有一样；如果有水爻发动，水在申长生，动来相生，木爻又重新有用，好比人在困窘的时候遇到贵人扶持，必有寒谷回春的样子。占病：用爻和吉神在绝处逢生，病将死而又活过来；鬼爻和忌神在绝处逢生，病快要好了却又发作。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Meeting life in a place of extinction: for example, on a Shen day the Subject line in the hexagram belongs to Wood; Wood meeting Shen is in Extinction and this Wood line is of no use, the same as absent; if a Water line moves, Water having its Long Life at Shen moves to generate it, and the Wood line is again of use, like a person in hard straits who meets a patron to support him, and there is surely the look of spring returning to a cold valley. Divining illness: if the Subject line and a lucky spirit meet life in a place of extinction, the illness about to end in death revives; if the Pressure line and the Adversary meet life in a place of extinction, the illness about to settle breaks out again.

**《断易天机·散见各章·论》原文** — 《断易天机》第 56 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Scattered sections: the discussions", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 56*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 且如申日占， | 比如申日占卜， | Take a divination on a Shen day: |
| 卦遇用爻属木， | 卦里的用爻属木， | the Subject line in the hexagram belongs to Wood; |
| 木见申则绝， | 木遇到申就是绝， | Wood meeting Shen is in Extinction, |
| 木爻无用矣。 | 这木爻就没有用了。 | and this Wood line is of no use. |
| 此同无也。 | 这等于没有。 | this is the same as having none. |
| 若得水爻发动， | 如果有水爻发动， | If a Water line moves, |
| 水长生动来相生， | 水在申长生，动来生木， | Water having its Long Life at Shen moves to generate it, |
| 木爻仍复有用。 | 这木爻又重新有用了。 | the Wood line is again of use. |
| 譬如人当困穷之际， | 好比人在困窘的时候， | like a person in hard straits |
| 得遇贵人扶持， | 遇到贵人扶持， | who meets a patron to support him, |
| 必有寒谷回春之象， | 必有寒谷回春的样子， | there is surely the look of spring returning to a cold valley; |
| 此乃是绝处逢生也。 | 这就是绝处逢生。 | this is meeting life in a place of extinction. |

**《断易天机·散见各章·论》原文** — 《断易天机》第 65 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Scattered sections: the discussions", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 65*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病： | 占病时： | Divining illness: |
| 用爻与吉神绝处逢生， | 用爻和吉神在绝处逢生， | if the Subject line and a lucky spirit meet life in a place of extinction, |
| 病将死而复活。 | 病将死而又活过来； | the illness about to end in death revives; |
| 鬼爻与忌神绝处逢生， | 鬼爻和忌神在绝处逢生， | if the Pressure line and the Adversary meet life in a place of extinction, |
| 病欲安而复作。 | 病快要好了却又发作。 | the illness about to settle breaks out again. |

> **按**:「绝处逢生」是断易天机「论」里的一章：一个爻在它的绝地，又逢生它的爻发动来相生，就像人在困境里遇到贵人。这一章占婚、占产、占官、占讼、求财、出行、行人、家宅各有一例（见 LY.MARRY.DYTJ 一带、LY.WEALTH.DYTJ 一带），这一条收占病那一句：用爻（病人）遇吉神绝处逢生，病将死而复活；鬼爻（病）遇忌神（生鬼的神）绝处逢生，病要好了又发作。「木见申则绝」是木在申（金）受克为绝；「水长生动来相生」是水在申长生。这两句和天玄赋「月解交重灾渐退，天医发动便回生」（LY.ILL.TXF.05）的「病将好又复发」是不同的：那里是吉神动，这里是绝地逢生。
>
> **Note:** 'Meeting life in a place of extinction' is a chapter in the 'discussions' of the Duanyi Tianji: a line in its place of extinction, with a line that generates it moving to generate it, like a person in hard straits meeting a patron. This chapter has one case each for marriage, childbirth, office, lawsuit, wealth, travel, a traveller and the house (see LY.MARRY.DYTJ and around, LY.WEALTH.DYTJ and around), and this entry takes the sentence on illness: the Subject line (the sick person) with a lucky spirit meeting life in a place of extinction, the illness about to end in death revives; the Pressure line (the illness) with the Adversary (the spirit that generates the ghost) meeting life in a place of extinction, the illness about to settle breaks out again. 'Wood meeting Shen is in Extinction' is Wood controlled at Shen (Metal) and so in Extinction; 'Water having its Long Life at Shen' is Water born long-lived at Shen. These two lines differ from the Tianxuan Fu's 'when the Month resolves and the Day moves heavily calamity fades, when the Heavenly Physician moves he recovers at once' (LY.ILL.TXF.05): there it is a lucky spirit moving, and here it is meeting life in extinction.

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.26 合处逢冲·占病：惟有占讼与病喜遇合处逢冲；合则事必成、逢冲灾必散、事将危而有救病欲死而复生；吉神合处不可冲、凶神合处喜逢冲

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:合处逢冲：比如占得雷地豫卦，世应相生，六爻相合，吉，没有不利的，是事事可以成的样子；却是在子日占得，子冲应上的午、害世上的未，这就是合处逢冲。只有占官司和占病，喜欢遇到合处逢冲：合，事就一定成；逢冲，灾就一定散；事将危而有救，病欲死而复生。其余照这样类推。又说：吉神合的地方不可以冲，凶神合的地方喜欢逢冲。大概可以照这个断。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A clash where there is a combination: for example, divining the hexagram Thunder over Earth, Yu: the Self and the Other generate each other, the six lines combine, lucky and nothing unfavourable, the image of every matter succeeding; but it is divined on a Zi day, and Zi clashes with the Wu on the Other line and harms the Wei on the Self line, which is meeting a clash where there is a combination. Only in divining a lawsuit and an illness is meeting a clash where there is a combination welcome: combining, the matter surely succeeds; meeting a clash, calamity surely scatters; a matter about to be in danger is rescued, an illness about to end in death revives. The rest follow this. It also says: where a lucky spirit combines it must not be clashed, and where a fierce spirit combines a clash is welcome. One may perhaps follow this judgement.

**《断易天机·散见各章·论》原文** — 《断易天机》第 68 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Scattered sections: the discussions", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 68*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 且如占得雷地豫卦。 | 比如占到雷地豫卦。 | Suppose the divination gets the Yu hexagram, Thunder over Earth. |
| 世应相生， | 世应相生， | The Self and the Other generate each other, |
| 六爻相合， | 六爻相合， | the six lines combine, |
| 吉无不利， | 吉，没有不利的， | lucky and nothing unfavourable, |
| 乃事事可成之象也。 | 是事事可以成的样子。 | the image of every matter succeeding. |
| 却在子日占得， | 却是在子日占到的， | But it is got on a Zi day, |
| 子冲应上午、害世上未， | 子冲应爻上的午、又害世爻上的未， | and Zi clashes the Wu on the Other line and harms the Wei on the Self line; |
| 此乃合处逢冲也。 | 这就是合处逢冲。 | this is 'meeting a clash where there was combination'. |

**《断易天机·散见各章·论》原文** — 《断易天机》第 73–74 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Scattered sections: the discussions", base text; Duanyi Tianji, lines 73–74*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 惟有占讼与病， | 只有占官司和占病， | Only in divining a lawsuit and an illness |
| 喜遇合处逢冲。 | 喜欢遇到合处逢冲。 | is meeting a clash where there is a combination welcome. |
| 合则事必成， | 合，事就一定成； | Combining, the matter surely succeeds; |
| 逢冲灾必散。 | 逢冲，灾就一定散； | meeting a clash, calamity surely scatters; |
| 事将危而有救、病欲死而复生。 | 事将危而有救，病欲死而复生。 | a matter about to be in danger is rescued, an illness about to end in death revives. |
| 其余仿此。 | 其余照这样类推。 | The rest follow this. |
| 又云： | 书里又有一首说： | The book also has another verse, which says: |
| 吉神合处不可冲， | 吉神合的地方不可以冲， | where a lucky spirit combines it must not be clashed, |
| 凶神合处喜逢冲也。 | 凶神合的地方喜欢逢冲。 | and where a fierce spirit combines a clash is welcome. |
| 庶几可遵此断。 | 大概可以照这个断。 | one may perhaps follow this judgement. |

> **按**:「合处逢冲」一章的总句是：吉事逢合本来好，若在合的地方遇冲，事情将成而破。占婚、占官、占财、谋事都是这样，只有占官司和占病是例外：那两样要的是灾散，逢冲正好。这一条是占病那一句：病快要死了逢冲就活。「子冲应上午、害世上未」是子日冲午、又害未（子未相害）。这一句和这本书的「逢冲」一章（LY.ILL.DYTJ.28）一起，是冲在病里的两面：合处逢冲救病，静爻遇日冲叫暗动，吉凶都有。另外，天玄赋的「月解交重灾渐退」（LY.ILL.TXF.05）也是把灾冲散一类。
>
> **Note:** The general statement of the chapter 'a clash where there is a combination' is that a lucky matter meeting a combination is good, but if it meets a clash in the place of the combination, the matter about to succeed is broken. It is so in divining marriage, office, wealth and planning a matter, and only in divining a lawsuit and an illness is it the exception: those two want calamity to scatter, and meeting a clash is just right. This entry is the sentence on illness: an illness about to end in death lives on meeting a clash. 'Zi clashes with the Wu on the Other line and harms the Wei on the Self line' is the Zi day clashing with Wu and also harming Wei (Zi and Wei harm each other). This sentence together with the chapter of this book on 'meeting a clash' (LY.ILL.DYTJ.28) gives the two sides of a clash in illness: a clash where there is a combination saves the illness, and a still line meeting a day clash is called a hidden movement, with both luck and ill. The Tianxuan Fu's 'when the Month resolves and the Day moves heavily calamity fades' (LY.ILL.TXF.05) is also of the kind that scatters calamity.

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.27 随官入墓·占病：身随鬼入墓、世随鬼入墓、命随鬼入墓，不问占何事皆非吉兆；占病遇之十占九死

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:随官入墓有三种：身随鬼入墓、世随鬼入墓、命随鬼入墓。以上三种墓，不问占什么事，都不是吉兆。占病遇到它，十占里有九个死。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Following the ghost into a tomb has three kinds: the Body following the ghost into a tomb, the Self following the ghost into a tomb, and the natal line following the ghost into a tomb. These three tombs, whatever is divined, are none of them a lucky sign. Divining illness and meeting one, nine out of ten die.

**《断易天机·散见各章·论》原文** — 《断易天机》第 78 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Scattered sections: the discussions", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 78*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 随官入墓， | 跟着官鬼入墓， | Following Pressure into the tomb |
| 其目有三： | 它的名目有三种： | its kinds are three: |
| 有身随鬼入墓， | 有卦身跟着鬼入墓， | the Body following Pressure into the tomb, |
| 有世随鬼入墓， | 有世爻跟着鬼入墓， | the Self following Pressure into the tomb, |
| 有命随鬼入墓。 | 有本命跟着鬼入墓。 | and the natal line following Pressure into the tomb. |

**《断易天机·散见各章·论》原文** — 《断易天机》第 82 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Scattered sections: the discussions", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 82*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以上三墓， | 以上三种入墓， | Of these three tombs, |
| 不问占何事， | 不管占什么事， | whatever is being divined, |
| 皆非吉兆。 | 都不是吉兆。 | none is an auspicious sign. |

**《断易天机·散见各章·论》原文** — 《断易天机》第 93 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Scattered sections: the discussions", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 93*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病遇之， | 占病遇到它， | Divining illness and meeting it, |
| 十占九死。 | 十占里有九个死。 | nine out of ten die. |

> **按**:「随官入墓」的三种（身、世、命随鬼入墓）在这本书里各有一个卦例（丑日占得雷水解卦、未日占得山天大畜、未日寅日生人占得地雷复……），例不收。这一条只收总句和占病的一句：「占病遇之，十占九死」。天玄赋的「入墓卦」（LY.ILL.TXF.06）说的是「世身随鬼入墓决不瘥」，这里多了「命随鬼入墓」（病人的本命爻随鬼入墓）。「命」是病人的本命爻，这一点和孙膑断疾病歌里「先从卦中寻本命」（LY.ILL.DYTJ.02）是同一个「命」。其余各事（占身、占婚、占产、求官、占讼、求财、出行、行人、家宅、移居、风水）的一句不在病的范围，不收。
>
> **Note:** Each of the three kinds of 'following the ghost into a tomb' (the Body, the Self and the natal line following the ghost into a tomb) has an example hexagram in this book (a Chou day divining Thunder over Water, Jie; a Wei day divining Mountain over Heaven, Daxu; a Wei day and a person born on a Yin day divining Earth over Thunder, Fu ...), and the examples are not taken. This entry takes only the general statement and the sentence on illness: 'divining illness and meeting it, nine out of ten die'. The Tianxuan Fu's 'hexagrams that enter tombs' (LY.ILL.TXF.06) says 'the Self and the Body following the ghost into a tomb surely does not recover', and here there is in addition 'the natal line following the ghost into a tomb' (the sick person's natal line following the ghost into a tomb). The 'natal line' is the sick person's natal line, the same 'natal line' as in 'first look in the hexagram for the natal line' in Sun Bin's song (LY.ILL.DYTJ.02). The sentences for the other matters (the person, marriage, childbirth, seeking office, lawsuit, wealth, travel, a traveller, the house, moving house, geomancy) are outside illness and are not taken.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 2824 行:有身随鬼入墓;同文 第 2824 行:有世随鬼入墓;同文 第 2824 行:有命随鬼入墓
- 《增删卜易》:异文(相似 0.83) 第 4076 行:卦身随鬼入墓;异文(相似 0.83) 第 10706 行:衰世随鬼入墓;异文(相似 0.83) 第 4076 行:本命随鬼入墓
- 《易冒》:异文(相似 0.83) 第 1007 行:以世随鬼入墓
- 《易林补遗》:异文(相似 0.83) 第 2267 行:云：身随鬼入墓;异文(相似 0.83) 第 1302 行:之，命随鬼入墓
- 《易隐》:异文(相似 0.83) 第 1990 行:世身随鬼入墓;异文(相似 0.83) 第 1707 行:身世随鬼入墓
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 833 行:有身随鬼入墓;同文 第 833 行:有世随鬼入墓;同文 第 833 行:有命随鬼入墓
- 《黄金策》:异文(相似 0.83) 第 1263 行:看身随鬼入墓;异文(相似 0.83) 第 1263 行:墓，世随鬼入墓;异文(相似 0.83) 第 1263 行:墓，命随鬼入墓

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.06、LY.ILL.DYTJ.02、LY.ILL.DYTJ.23

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.31 三丘五墓杀：春丑夏辰秋未冬戌为三丘、却与五墓对宫取，病人作福也难留；此占病忌，或有吉神解救方免

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:春天是丑、夏天是辰、秋天是未、冬天逢戌，这是三丘；五墓要取三丘的对宫。病人就是作福（祈福）也难留住。这是占病所忌，只有得吉神来解救才能免去。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In spring Chou, in summer Chen, in autumn Wei and in winter meeting Xu: these are the Three Mounds; the Five Tombs are taken from the opposite palace. The sick person, though he makes offerings for fortune, can hardly be kept. This is a taboo in divining illness, and it is escaped only if a lucky spirit comes to rescue.

**《断易天机·论神煞·凶煞》原文** — 《断易天机》第 291 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "On spirits and killings: the fierce killings", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 291*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 春丑夏辰秋即未， | 春天是丑、夏天是辰、秋天就是未， | In spring Chou, in summer Chen, in autumn Wei, |
| 三冬逢戌是三丘， | 冬天逢戌，这是三丘； | in winter meeting Xu: these are the Three Mounds; |
| 却与五墓对宫取， | 五墓却要取三丘的对宫， | the Five Tombs are taken from the opposite palace; |
| 病人作福也难留。 | 病人就是作福也难留住。 | the sick person, though he makes offerings for fortune, can hardly be kept. |
| 此占病忌， | 这是占病所忌， | This is a taboo in divining illness, |
| 或有吉神解救方免。 | 或是有吉神来解救才能免去。 | and it is escaped only if a lucky spirit comes to rescue. |

> **按**:这首「三丘五墓杀」的头四句和卜筮大全、易林补遗（LY.ILL.EL.51）里的三丘五墓歌是同一首，后面两句「此占病忌，或有吉神解救方免」是这里加上的。三丘是春丑、夏辰、秋未、冬戌；五墓「对宫取」，也就是三丘的对冲：春未、夏戌、秋丑、冬辰（这个排法见 LY.ILL.EL.51）。「病人作福也难留」和天玄赋的「二杀俱动，病人作福也难留」（LY.ILL.TXF.08）是同一句。神煞月令表里也有「三丘」「五墓」两行（丑丑丑辰辰辰未未未戌戌戌；未未未戌戌戌丑丑丑辰辰辰），那两行的断语是「坟墓崩，隐为崇凶」「坟墓崩陷凶」，不是专说病，这里不收。
>
> **Note:** The first four lines of this 'Three Mounds and Five Tombs killing' are the same verse as the Three Mounds and Five Tombs verse in the Buyi Daquan and the Yilin Buyi (LY.ILL.EL.51), and the last two lines, 'this is a taboo in divining illness, and it is escaped only if a lucky spirit comes to rescue', are added here. The Three Mounds are Chou in spring, Chen in summer, Wei in autumn and Xu in winter; the Five Tombs are 'taken from the opposite palace', that is the clash of the Three Mounds: Wei in spring, Xu in summer, Chou in autumn and Chen in winter (for this layout see LY.ILL.EL.51). 'The sick person, though he makes offerings for fortune, can hardly be kept' is the same sentence as the Tianxuan Fu's 'when both killings move, even if the sick person makes offerings for fortune he can hardly be kept' (LY.ILL.TXF.08). The table of spirits and killings by month also has two rows 'Three Mounds' and 'Five Tombs' (Chou Chou Chou Chen Chen Chen Wei Wei Wei Xu Xu Xu; Wei Wei Wei Xu Xu Xu Chou Chou Chou Chen Chen Chen), and the judgments of those two rows are 'the tomb crumbles, hidden it is an ominous curse' and 'the tomb crumbles and sinks, ominous', which are not specially about illness and are not taken here.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 10086 行:春丑夏辰秋即未;同文 第 10086 行:三冬逢戌是三丘;同文 第 10086 行:却与五墓对宫取;同文 第 4997 行:病人作福也难留
- 《易林补遗》:同文 第 1479 行:春丑，夏辰，秋即未;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1479 行:三冬逢戍是三丘;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1479 行:却兴五墓对宫取;同文 第 1479 行:病人作福也难留
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1863 行:病人作福也难留

相关:LY.ILL.EL.51、LY.ILL.TXF.08、LY.ILL.DYTJ.32、LY.ILL.ZSZ.01

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.32 丧车杀：丧车春鸡夏鼠秋马冬羊、人来占病断兴去、讨钱火急买棺材

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:丧车：春天是鸡、夏天是鼠，秋天是马、冬天是羊，好安排。有人来占病，断他是起身离去（去世）了，赶紧去讨钱、火速买棺材。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Mourning Cart: in spring it is the rooster, in summer the rat, in autumn the horse and in winter the sheep, which is well arranged. A person who comes to divine illness is judged to be setting off to leave; hurry to ask for money and buy a coffin in all haste.

**《断易天机·论神煞·凶煞》原文** — 《断易天机》第 294 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "On spirits and killings: the fierce killings", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 294*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 丧车春鸡夏鼠来， | 丧车：春天来的是鸡、夏天来的是鼠， | The Mourning Cart: in spring it comes as the rooster, in summer as the rat, |
| 秋马冬羊好安排， | 秋天是马、冬天是羊，好安排； | in autumn the horse, in winter the sheep, which is well arranged; |
| 人来占病断兴去， | 有人来占病，断他是起身离去（去世）了， | a person who comes to divine illness is judged to be setting off to leave, |
| 讨钱火急买棺材。 | 赶紧去讨钱、火速买棺材。 | hurry to ask for money and buy a coffin in all haste. |

> **按**:丧车杀的四季排法是春酉（鸡）、夏子（鼠）、秋午（马）、冬未（羊）。「断兴去」的「兴去」读不确定，「兴」有起身的意思，这里按「起身离去」译，也就是去世；「讨钱火急买棺材」是说要赶紧去讨钱备棺材，是个凶极的断语。「好安排」按字面译，是说四季的排法好排。丧车这个名目也见天玄赋（「丧车」「丧服」「棺椁」，LY.ILL.HZL.03 的申为丧车）。
>
> **Note:** The four-season layout of the Mourning Cart killing is You (the rooster) in spring, Zi (the rat) in summer, Wu (the horse) in autumn and Wei (the sheep) in winter. The 'xing qu' of 'divined to be xing qu' is not certain; 'xing' has the sense of setting off, and it is rendered 'setting off to leave', that is, dying; 'hurry to ask for money and buy a coffin' says one must hurry to ask for money and prepare a coffin, an extremely ominous judgment. 'Well arranged' is rendered literally and says the layout by seasons is easy to arrange. The name Mourning Cart also appears in the Huozhu Lin (Shen as the mourning cart, LY.ILL.HZL.03).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 10096 行:丧车春鸡夏鼠来
- 《易冒》:同文 第 720 行:丧车春鸡夏鼠来

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.31、LY.ILL.HZL.03
