## LY.ILL.DYTJ.36 神煞月令表·凶神(死与瘟病)：事气病必死丧凶、吊客病死忧疾凶、哭杀时行瘟病凶

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:神煞月令表里凶神的三行：事气（正月起午、未、申、酉、戌、亥、子、丑、寅、卯、辰、巳），病必死，丧事凶；吊客（辰、丑、戌、未循环），病人死亡、忧愁、疾病，凶；哭杀（巳、午、未、申、酉、戌、亥、子、丑、寅、卯、辰），流行的瘟病，凶。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Three fierce rows of the table of spirits and killings by month: Affair Qi (from the first month Wu, Wei, Shen, You, Xu, Hai, Zi, Chou, Yin, Mao, Chen, Si): in illness surely dead, mourning is ominous; Guest of Condolence (Chen, Chou, Xu, Wei in a cycle): the sick person dies, worry and sickness, ominous; the Weeping Killing (Si, Wu, Wei, Shen, You, Xu, Hai, Zi, Chou, Yin, Mao, Chen): epidemic plague is ominous.

**《断易天机·神煞月令表》原文** — 《断易天机》第 398 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "The table of spirits and killings by month", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 398*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 事气 | 事气 | Affair Qi |
| 午未申酉戌亥子丑寅卯辰巳 | 午、未、申、酉、戌、亥、子、丑、寅、卯、辰、巳（正月到十二月依次） | Wu, Wei, Shen, You, Xu, Hai, Zi, Chou, Yin, Mao, Chen, Si (from the first month to the twelfth in turn) |
| 病必死，丧凶。 | 病必死，丧事凶。 | in illness surely dead, mourning is ominous. |

**《断易天机·神煞月令表》原文** — 《断易天机》第 400 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "The table of spirits and killings by month", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 400*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 吊客 | 吊客 | Guest of Condolence |
| 辰丑戌未辰丑戌未辰丑戌未 | 辰、丑、戌、未、辰、丑、戌、未、辰、丑、戌、未（正月到十二月依次） | Chen, Chou, Xu, Wei, Chen, Chou, Xu, Wei, Chen, Chou, Xu, Wei (from the first month to the twelfth in turn) |
| 病死忧疾凶。 | 病人死亡、忧愁、疾病，凶。 | the sick person dies, worry and sickness, ominous. |

**《断易天机·神煞月令表》原文** — 《断易天机》第 408 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "The table of spirits and killings by month", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 408*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 哭杀 | 哭杀 | Weeping Killing |
| 巳午未申酉戌亥子丑寅卯辰 | 巳、午、未、申、酉、戌、亥、子、丑、寅、卯、辰（正月到十二月依次） | Si, Wu, Wei, Shen, You, Xu, Hai, Zi, Chou, Yin, Mao, Chen (from the first month to the twelfth in turn) |
| 时行瘟病凶。 | 流行的瘟病，凶。 | epidemic plague is ominous. |

> **按**:这三行的断语都说到死或瘟病：事气「病必死，丧凶」是最重的一句，吊客是病人死亡和忧愁，哭杀是时行的瘟病。「吊客」这一名和天玄赋里「丧门」「吊客」并提的说法（LY.ILL.TXF.04：「若临丧门、吊客、死符、丧车，持克用爻者，决主不起」）是同一个吊客。「哭杀」在表里的排法（巳午未申酉戌亥子丑寅卯辰）和前面「天哭杀」歌里的排法（正五九羊、二六十猴……，LY.ILL.DYTJ.33）不同，是两个杀。表里另有「大杀」「飞廉」「天丧」「大忌」等几行说到死亡，但都没有说病，这里不收。
>
> **Note:** The judgments of these three rows all speak of death or plague: Affair Qi, 'in illness surely dead, mourning is ominous', is the heaviest sentence, Guest of Condolence is the death and worry of the sick person, and the Weeping Killing is the plague of the season. The name 'Guest of Condolence' is the same Guest of Condolence as in the Tianxuan Fu's pairing of 'Mourning Gate' and 'Guest of Condolence' (LY.ILL.TXF.04: 'if it sits on the Mourning Gate, the Guest of Condolence, the Death Talisman or the Mourning Cart and controls the Subject line, it surely means he does not rise'). The layout of the 'Weeping Killing' in the table (Si, Wu, Wei, Shen, You, Xu, Hai, Zi, Chou, Yin, Mao, Chen) differs from that in the song of the 'Heavenly Weeping killing' (first, fifth, ninth the sheep, second, sixth, tenth the monkey ..., LY.ILL.DYTJ.33), and they are two killings. The table has other rows, 'Great Killing', 'Feilian', 'Heavenly Mourning' and 'Great Taboo', that speak of death but do not say illness, and they are not taken here.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《周易尚占》:同文 第 369 行:午未申酉戌亥子丑寅卯辰巳
- 《大易断例卜筮元龟目》:异文(相似 0.92) 第 404 行:午未申酉戌亥子丑寅卯辰
- 《易隐》:同文 第 611 行:午未申酉戌亥子丑寅卯辰巳
- 《易隐》:异文(相似 0.92) 第 726–727 行:同丑戌未辰丑戌未辰丑戌未
- 《周易尚占》:同文 第 301 行:巳午未申酉戌亥子丑寅卯辰
- 《大易断例卜筮元龟目》:同文 第 404 行:巳午未申酉戌亥子丑寅卯辰
- 《易隐》:同文 第 489–500 行:巳午未申酉戌亥子丑寅卯辰

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.35、LY.ILL.TXF.04、LY.ILL.DYTJ.33

## LY.ILL.DYTJ.48 占居官有疾病否：卦变为囚死乡居官身病至衰亡、无气带杀终有祸更值空亡必有丧；鬼动若逢休废地此身退位终无位、胎没并之世自微更加刑杀为衰

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占在任的官有没有疾病：凡是卦变到囚、死的地方，在任的官身上有病，以至衰亡；无气又带杀，终究有祸，再遇上空亡，必定有丧。鬼动，若逢到休、废的地方，这个人退位，终究没有官位；胎没并起来，世自己就衰微，再加刑、杀，就是衰败了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** On whether an official in office has illness: whenever the hexagram changes into a place of trapped or dead, the official in office falls ill, even to decline and death; without qi and bearing a killing, in the end there is calamity, and meeting Void as well there is surely a death. The ghost moving, if it meets a place of resting or ruin, this person leaves office and in the end has no post; with Womb and Submerged joined, the Self itself is faint, and with punishment and killings added it is decline.

**《断易天机·占官·占居官有疾病否》原文** — 《断易天机》第 2682–2683 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Divining office: whether an official in office has illness", base text; Duanyi Tianji, lines 2682–2683*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占居官有疾病否云： | 占在任的官有没有疾病，歌诀说： | On whether an official in office has illness it says: |
| 凡卦变为囚死乡， | 凡是卦变到囚、死的地方， | Whenever the hexagram changes into a place of trapped or dead, |
| 居官身病至衰亡， | 在任的官身上有病，以至衰亡； | the official in office falls ill, even to decline and death; |
| 无气带杀终有祸， | 无气又带着杀，终究有祸； | without qi and bearing a killing, in the end there is calamity, |
| 更值空亡必有丧。 | 再遇上空亡，必定有丧。 | and meeting Void as well there is surely a death. |
| 鬼动若逢休废地， | 鬼动，若逢到休、废的地方， | The ghost moving, if it meets a place of resting or ruin, |
| 此身退位终无位， | 这个人退位，终究没有官位； | this person leaves office and in the end has no post; |
| 胎没并之世自微， | 胎没并起来，世自己就衰微， | with Womb and Submerged joined, the Self itself is faint, |
| 更加刑杀为衰耳。 | 再加刑、杀，就是衰败。 | and with punishment and killings added it is decline. |

> **按**:这首在占官一章的「占居官有疾病否」一题下，问的是在任的官会不会生病。「囚死乡」是囚、死两种状态所在的地方（旺相休囚死里的囚和死）；「卦变为囚死乡」是卦（或世爻）变到囚死的地方。「胎没」是什么书里没有说，「胎」和「没」是两个名目，这里字面译作 Womb and Submerged；「并之」是并在一起。这首歌前半是说身病，后半转到「退位无位」的官运上，所以放在一起收。这一首在同一书里出现两次（一次是歌诀，一次是今人译文里重复的标题），译文不收。前半的意思（状态衰败、又带杀、再遇空亡则死）和孙膑歌「卦变墓乡须丧命」（LY.ILL.DYTJ.07）、天玄赋的「入墓卦」（LY.ILL.TXF.06）是同一类。
>
> **Note:** This poem is under the heading 'divining whether an official in office has illness' in the chapter on divining office, and asks whether an official in office will fall ill. The 'place of trapped or dead' is the place where the two states of trapped and dead (of strong, helped, resting, trapped, dead) lie; 'the hexagram changing into a place of trapped or dead' is the hexagram (or the Self line) changing into a place of trapped or dead. What 'Womb and Submerged' is the book does not say, and 'tai' and 'mo' are two names, rendered literally as Womb and Submerged; 'bing zhi' is joined together. The first half of the poem speaks of illness of the body, and the second half turns to fortune in office, 'leaves office and has no post', and so they are taken together. This poem appears twice in the same book (once as the verse and once as a repeated heading in a modern translation), and the translation is not quoted. The sense of the first half (a state of decline, bearing a killing, and meeting Void as well means death) is of the same kind as Sun Bin's song, 'if the hexagram changes into the tomb quarter life will surely be lost' (LY.ILL.DYTJ.07), and the Tianxuan Fu's 'hexagrams that enter tombs' (LY.ILL.TXF.06).

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.07、LY.ILL.TXF.06

---

## LY.ILL.EL.11 用爻遇旬空：暴病逢空可救、久病逢空必死；虽空分衰旺——空而旺相久病无妨、空而休囚遇日冲亦不死；立时空又值旬空无不倾亡；不空而月破必伤身

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:再说用爻上卦正好遇旬空，要看病是近病还是久病：暴病遇空可救，久病遇空必死。虽然遇空亡，还要分衰旺：空的用爻如果旺相，纵然是久病也没有妨碍；空的用爻如果休囚，只要遇上日冲也不会死。倘若是立时空又遇上旬空，不论近病久病，没有不死的。用爻虽然不落空，如果临月破，必定伤身。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Again, when the Subject line is in the hexagram and falls exactly on Void, look at whether the illness is recent or long: a sudden illness meeting Void can be saved, a long illness meeting Void surely dies. Though it meets Void, one still divides strong from weak: if the Void line is strong, even a long illness is no hindrance; if the Void line is resting or trapped, meeting a Day clash it still does not die. If it is Void at the moment and also falls into the ten-day Void, then whether the illness is recent or long, none escapes collapse. Though the Subject line is not Void, if it sits in Month-break it will surely harm the body.

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1285 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1285*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又论用爻上卦正值旬空，却看病之远近，暴病逢空可救，久病逢空必死。 | 再说用爻上卦正好遇旬空，要看病是近病还是久病：暴病遇空可救，久病遇空必死。 | Again, when the Subject line is in the hexagram and falls exactly on Void, look at whether the illness is recent or long: a sudden illness meeting Void can be saved, a long illness meeting Void surely dies. |
| 虽值空亡，还分衰旺。 | 虽然遇空亡，还要分衰旺。 | Though it meets Void, one still divides strong from weak. |
| 空如旺相，纵然久病也无妨。 | 空的用爻如果旺相，纵然是久病也没有妨碍。 | If the Void line is strong, even a long illness is no hindrance. |
| 空若休囚，但遇日冲亦不死。 | 空的用爻如果休囚，只要遇上日冲也不会死。 | If the Void line is resting or trapped, meeting a Day clash it still does not die. |
| 倘若立时空又值旬空，不拘远近之病，无不倾亡。 | 倘若是立时空又遇上旬空，不论近病久病，没有不死的。 | If it is Void at the moment and also falls into the ten-day Void, then whether the illness is recent or long, none escapes collapse. |
| 用爻虽不落空，如临月破必致伤身。 | 用爻虽然不落空，如果临月破，必定伤身。 | Though the Subject line is not Void, if it sits in Month-break it will surely harm the body. |

> **打架**:用神落空要不要分近病久病：易林补遗分：暴病逢空可救、久病逢空必死，而且空了还要分衰旺——旺相的久病也无妨，休囚的遇日冲也不死；增删卜易的野鹤同样分近久，近病落空到冲空、实空的日子就好，久病用神落旬空月破则旺相也无法治（LY.ILL.ZS.04、ZS.05）；黄金策旧注和《海底眼》说主象落空占病大忌，要有动爻日辰冲克才有救，不分近久（LY.ILL.HJC.37）。易林补遗的「空旺相久病也无妨」和增删卜易的「久病用神空破，旺相也无法医治」在久病旺相的空爻上说法相反。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether a Void Subject calls for a division between recent and long illness: the Yilin Buyi divides them: a sudden illness meeting Void can be saved and a long one surely dies, and beyond that a Void line is divided by strength — a strong one is no hindrance even in a long illness, a resting or trapped one does not die if it meets a Day clash; the Old Man of Yehe in the Zengshan Buyi likewise divides recent from long, a recent illness with the Subject Void being well on the day the Void is clashed or filled, while for a long illness a Subject that is Void or in Month-break cannot be treated even if strong (LY.ILL.ZS.04, ZS.05); the old commentary on the Huangjin Ce and the Haidi Yan say that a Void chief image is a great taboo in divining illness and that a moving line or a clash or control by the Day is needed for rescue, without dividing recent from long (LY.ILL.HJC.37). On a strong Void line in a long illness the Yilin Buyi ('strong Void, even a long illness is no hindrance') and the Zengshan Buyi ('long illness, Subject Void or broken, even strong it cannot be treated') say opposite things.

> **按**:「立时空」原文没有解释，大概指当下、此时就落空（与旬空的空相对），读法没有把握，这里按字面译作「立时空」。「日冲」是日辰冲它：冲空则实。这一条在「暴病逢空可救、久病逢空必死」上与增删卜易同，与黄金策旧注不同；但在空而旺相的久病是否无妨上，与增删卜易相反（见上面的分歧）。
>
> **Note:** 'Void at the moment' (li shi kong) is not explained in the original; it probably means Void right now, as against the Void of the ten-day cycle, but the reading is uncertain and it is translated literally. A 'Day clash' is the Day clashing it: a clash fills a Void. This entry agrees with the Zengshan Buyi on 'a sudden illness meeting Void can be saved and a long illness surely dies' and differs from the old commentary on the Huangjin Ce, but on whether a strong Void line makes a long illness harmless it is opposed to the Zengshan Buyi (see the disagreement above).

相关:LY.ILL.EL.10、LY.ILL.EL.12、LY.ILL.ZS.04、LY.ILL.ZS.05、LY.ILL.HJC.37、LY.ILL.YM.10、LY.ILL.DYTJ.51

---

## LY.ILL.EL.14 空身空命皆非忌，无鬼无财岂足凭：甲子旬空戌亥不等于属猪属犬人皆命绝；病人无鬼必死之说不准，无鬼不过无神祟；无财只是眼下不思饮食，惟夫占妻、主占仆忌财空

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:空身、空命都不必忌讳，无鬼、无财哪里够得上凭据。注说：比如甲子旬占，空的是戌、亥，天下万万属猪、属狗的人，难道都命绝了？前人又说病人无鬼必死，难道没有验证过吗？《天玄赋》里虽然说占病无鬼，就没有叩告的门路，是天年命尽，这病不能好。可是比如哥哥占弟弟的病，鬼是忌神，怎么该在卦里？又如父亲占儿子的病，鬼是仇人，怎么可以用它？如果按道理论病，只取用爻的旺衰生克，就能断定存亡。凡是卦里无鬼，不过是没有神祟罢了，难道就是天年命尽？何况现在屡次试验没有官鬼的卦，也未必死。又辨别没有财的说法更加差错：财爻虽然是饮食，没有财，不过是眼下不吃东西，怎么能丧命？只有丈夫占妻子、主人占仆人来看，才忌讳财空，其余占的都不忌讳。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Void of the Body and the Void of the natal branch need not be feared, and no ghost or no Wealth is hardly enough to rely on. The commentary says: for example, when a divination is made in the Jiazi decade the Void branches are Xu and Hai, and of the innumerable people under heaven born in the year of the pig or the dog, are all to have their lives cut off? The men of old also said that a patient with no ghost must die, and was there no proof? Although the Tianxuan Fu says that in divining illness with no ghost there is no door to knock on and appeal, it is the end of one's allotted years, and the illness will not be cured. But for instance, if an elder brother divines a younger brother's illness, the ghost is the Adversary, and how should it be in the hexagram? Or if a father divines a son's illness, the ghost is an enemy, and how can it be used? If the illness is discussed on principle, only the strength and the generation and control of the Subject line are taken, and life or death is decided. A hexagram with no ghost only lacks a haunting, and is it therefore the end of one's allotted years? Moreover, no-Official hexagrams have been tried many times and the person did not necessarily die. Again the claim about lacking Wealth is even more mistaken: though the Wealth line is food and drink, lacking it only means that for the moment he does not eat, how can that cost his life? Only in a husband divining a wife or a master a servant is a Void Wealth line feared, and in all other divinations it is not feared.

**《易林补遗·疾病用神(章题缺,在是病非病之后)》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1288 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "The Subject in illness (chapter title missing; after the passage on real illness)", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1288*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 空身空命皆非忌，无鬼无财岂足凭。 | 空身、空命都不必忌讳，无鬼、无财哪里够得上凭据。 | The Void of the Body and the Void of the natal branch need not be feared, and no ghost or no Wealth is hardly enough to rely on. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1289 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1289*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 且如甲子旬占，空当戌亥，寰中万万属猪属犬生人，岂皆命绝？ | 比如甲子旬占，空的是戌、亥，天下万万属猪、属狗的人，难道都命绝了？ | For example, when a divination is made in the Jiazi decade the Void branches are Xu and Hai, and of the innumerable people under heaven born in the year of the pig or the dog, are all to have their lives cut off? |
| 前人又言病人无鬼必死，岂无验乎？ | 前人又说病人无鬼必死，难道没有验证过吗？ | The men of old also said that a patient with no ghost must die, and was there no proof? |
| 天玄赋中虽曰占病无鬼必无叩告之门，乃天年命尽也，其病不瘳。 | 《天玄赋》里虽然说占病无鬼，就没有叩告的门路，是天年命尽，这病不能好。 | Although the Tianxuan Fu says that in divining illness with no ghost there is no door to knock on and appeal, it is the end of one's allotted years, and the illness will not be cured. |
| 且如兄占弟病，鬼乃忌神，岂宜在卦？ | 可是比如哥哥占弟弟的病，鬼是忌神，怎么该在卦里？ | But for instance, if an elder brother divines a younger brother's illness, the ghost is the Adversary, and how should it be in the hexagram? |
| 又如父占子病，鬼作仇人，焉可用之？ | 又如父亲占儿子的病，鬼是仇人，怎么可以用它？ | Or if a father divines a son's illness, the ghost is an enemy, and how can it be used? |
| 若据理上论病只取用爻旺衰生克，便决存亡。 | 如果按道理论病，只取用爻的旺衰生克，就能断定存亡。 | If the illness is discussed on principle, only the strength and the generation and control of the Subject line are taken, and life or death is decided. |
| 凡卦无鬼不过无神祟耳，岂就作天年命尽乎？ | 凡是卦里无鬼，不过是没有神祟罢了，难道就是天年命尽？ | A hexagram with no ghost only lacks a haunting, and is it therefore the end of one's allotted years? |
| 况今屡试无官之卦，未必死也。 | 何况现在屡次试验没有官鬼的卦，也未必死。 | Moreover, hexagrams without a Pressure line have been tried many times and the person did not necessarily die. |
| 又辨无财者理更差误，财爻虽为饮食，无者不过目今饮食不食，焉能丧命？ | 又辨别没有财的说法更加差错：财爻虽然是饮食，没有财，不过是眼下不吃东西，怎么能丧命？ | Again the claim about lacking Wealth is even more mistaken: though the Wealth line is food and drink, lacking it only means that for the moment he does not eat, how can that cost his life? |
| 只有夫占妻、主占仆看，惟忌财空，其余占者皆不忌。 | 只有丈夫占妻子、主人占仆人来看，才忌讳财空，其余占的都不忌讳。 | Only in a husband divining a wife or a master a servant is a Void Wealth line feared, and in all other divinations it is not feared. |

> **打架**:病人卦里无鬼、无财是不是必死：易林补遗说无鬼不过是没有神祟，不等于天年命尽；无财不过眼下不思饮食，只有夫占妻、主占仆才忌财空；它批评的是《天玄赋》的「占病无鬼必无叩告之门，乃天年命尽」；易冒同样说无鬼无财、身空命空皆不死（LY.ILL.YM.11）；黄金策却说官鬼虽是恶煞不可无，不上卦要寻伏，伏又空则病必不能痊（LY.ILL.HJC.49），黄金策旧注、王洪绪对这句的范围也不一致；易隐里晁以道说用爻、命爻不上卦者死，男忌鬼空、女忌财空（LY.ILL.YY.10）；黄金策又说禄系妻财、寿属父母，财空无财则不思饮食（LY.ILL.HJC.38）。几家说法不同，书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether a patient's hexagram with no ghost or no Wealth means certain death: the Yilin Buyi says no ghost only lacks a haunting and does not mean the end of one's allotted years, and no Wealth only means he does not want to eat for the moment, a Void Wealth line being feared only in a husband divining a wife or a master a servant; what it criticises is the Tianxuan Fu's 'in divining illness with no ghost there is no door to knock on, it is the end of one's allotted years'; the Yi Mao likewise says that no ghost and no Wealth, Void of the Body or of the natal branch, do not mean death (LY.ILL.YM.11); the Huangjin Ce, however, says that although the Pressure line is a malign killer it cannot be absent, that when it is absent from the hexagram one must look for it hidden, and that if the hidden one is also Void the illness cannot be cured (LY.ILL.HJC.49), and the old commentary and Wang Hongxu differ on the scope of that saying; in the Yi Yin, Chao Yidao says that one whose Subject line or natal-branch line is absent from the hexagram dies, and that men fear Void ghosts and women Void Wealth (LY.ILL.YY.10); the Huangjin Ce also says that emolument belongs to Wealth and long life to the Resource line, and that a Void or absent Wealth means no appetite (LY.ILL.HJC.38). The books differ, and none gives a ruling.

> **按**:这一条把上一条歌诀的最后两句「空身空命皆非忌，无鬼无财岂足凭」拿出来单讲：空命（本命落旬空）、无鬼（卦无官鬼）、无财（卦无妻财）三种说法，评注者一个一个驳。「叩告之门」是可以向鬼神叩告的门路，出自《天玄赋》所说的「占病无鬼必无叩告之门」（黄金策也用了这句，见 LY.ILL.HJC.49）。「兄占弟病，鬼乃忌神」是说占兄弟的病，用神是兄弟，官鬼克兄弟，所以是忌神；「父占子病，鬼作仇人」是说占子孙的病，子孙的仇神是官鬼——所以这些卦里官鬼不该出现，何必因为无鬼就断死。
>
> **Note:** This entry takes the last two lines of the verse in the last entry, 'the Void of the Body and of the natal branch need not be feared, no ghost or no Wealth is hardly enough to rely on', and discusses them separately: three sayings, Void natal branch (the natal branch falling on the ten-day Void), no ghost (the hexagram lacks a Pressure line) and no Wealth (it lacks a Wealth line), and the commentator refutes them one by one. The 'door to knock on and appeal' is the way of appealing to ghosts and spirits, from the Tianxuan Fu's 'in divining illness with no ghost there is no door to knock on' (the Huangjin Ce uses this sentence too; see LY.ILL.HJC.49). 'An elder brother divining a younger brother's illness, the ghost is the Adversary' means that in divining a brother's illness the Subject is the Peer line, and the Pressure line controls the Peer, so it is the Adversary; 'a father divining a son's illness, the ghost is an enemy' means that for a son's illness the enemy of Output is the Pressure line; so in such hexagrams the Pressure line ought not to appear, and there is no reason to judge death because the ghost is absent.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.13、LY.ILL.EL.15、LY.ILL.YM.11、LY.ILL.HJC.38、LY.ILL.HJC.49、LY.ILL.YY.10、LY.ILL.ZS.01

---

## LY.ILL.EL.15 六冲分缓急：初灾遇六冲当全瘥、久病逢之命必伤，合处逢冲同此意，不凭主象弱和强；土爻临鬼动多凶少吉；近病逢冲则愈、重病逢冲则死；占尊长鬼为元神动则祈让可疗

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:只有六冲这一种，能分缓急，病的来源要自己斟酌衡量：刚起的灾遇上它应当全好，久病遇上它命必伤；合处逢冲也是这个意思，不看主象是弱还是强；再看土爻临鬼发动，凶多吉少，灾祸难以禳解。注说：病得六冲、六冲变冲、合处逢冲，都要审察病是远是近、是轻是重：暴病来占，朝夕之间就应当痊愈；如果久病来占，用象虽然旺相，也主身亡。又说近病逢冲就好，重病逢冲就死；卦纵然不冲，用爻也旺，凡是遇上财爻、鬼爻发动的，都可以断死；只有占尊长，鬼是元神，发动了就祈祷禳解可以治疗。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Only the Six-Clash tells slow from urgent, and the source of the illness must be weighed by oneself: a fresh calamity meeting it should be wholly cured, a long illness meeting it surely brings harm to life; a clash met in a combination has the same meaning, without regard to whether the chief image is weak or strong; and further, an Earth line on a moving ghost is mostly ominous and seldom auspicious, and the misfortune is hard to exorcise. The commentary says: when an illness has a six-clash, or a six-clash changing into a clash, or a clash met in a combination, one must examine whether it is near or far, light or heavy: a sudden illness divined should be cured by morning or evening; if a long illness is divined, though the Subject image is strong it still means death. It says also that a recent illness meeting a clash is cured and a heavy illness meeting a clash dies; even if the hexagram is not clashing and the Subject line is strong, wherever the Wealth or Pressure line moves one may say death; only in divining an elder, the ghost being the Support, moving it can be treated by prayer and exorcism.

**《易林补遗·疾病用神(章题缺,在是病非病之后)》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1290 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "The Subject in illness (chapter title missing; after the passage on real illness)", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1290*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又云惟有六冲分缓急，病源却要自斟量， | 只有六冲这一种，能分缓急，病的来源要自己斟酌衡量， | Only the Six-Clash tells slow from urgent, and the source of the illness must be weighed by oneself: |
| 初灾遇此当全瘥，久病逢之命必伤， | 刚起的灾遇上它应当全好，久病遇上它命必伤， | a fresh calamity meeting it should be wholly cured, a long illness meeting it surely brings harm to life; |
| 合处逢冲同此意，不凭主象弱和强， | 合处逢冲也是这个意思，不看主象是弱还是强， | a clash met in a combination has the same meaning, without regard to whether the chief image is weak or strong; |
| 更论土爻临鬼动，爻凶少吉祸难禳。 | 再看土爻临鬼发动，凶多吉少，灾祸难以禳解。 | and further, an Earth line on a moving ghost is mostly ominous and seldom auspicious, and the misfortune is hard to exorcise. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1292–1294 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 1292–1294*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 病得六冲化冲、合处逢冲，皆要审其远近重轻，如暴病来占，朝夕即当痊愈。 | 病得六冲、六冲变冲、合处逢冲，都要审察病是远是近、是轻是重：暴病来占，朝夕之间就应当痊愈。 | When an illness has a six-clash, or a six-clash changing into a clash, or a clash met in a combination, one must examine whether it is near or far, light or heavy: a sudden illness divined should be cured by morning or evening. |
| 若久病占之，用象虽然旺相，也主身亡。 | 如果久病来占，用象虽然旺相，也主身亡。 | If a long illness is divined, though the Subject image is strong it still means death. |
| 又论近病逢冲则愈，重病逢冲则死。 | 又说近病逢冲就好，重病逢冲就死。 | It says also that a recent illness meeting a clash is cured and a heavy illness meeting a clash dies. |
| 卦纵不冲，用爻亦旺，凡遇财鬼动者，万可言死。 | 卦纵然不冲，用爻也旺，凡是遇上财爻、鬼爻发动的，都可以断死。 | Even if the hexagram is not clashing and the Subject line is strong, wherever the Wealth or Pressure line moves one may say death. |
| 惟占尊长，鬼为元神，动则祈让可疗。 | 只有占尊长，鬼是元神，发动了就祈祷禳解可以治疗。 | Only in divining an elder, the ghost being the Support, moving it can be treated by prayer and exorcism. |

> **按**:原文「初灾遇此当全瘥」中的「瘥」是病愈；歌诀最后一句「爻凶少吉祸难禳」里的「爻凶」疑是「多凶」之误（注解处写作「多凶少吉」），这里按上下文译作「凶多吉少」。「六冲、六冲变冲、合处逢冲」是三种冲：卦是六冲卦、六冲卦变六冲卦、六合卦（或合爻）遇冲。这一条与增删卜易「六冲变冲久病难于调治」（LY.ILL.ZS.02）、「卦变绝克新疾亦主危亡」（LY.ILL.ZS.03）、易冒「卦属反伏墓绝亦不治；病未久三冲而愈、病已久三冲而亡」（LY.ILL.YM.10）、易隐（LY.ILL.YY.07）是同一件事，各家对久病遇冲的结论一致：久病逢冲必死；对近病遇冲则略有出入（此处近病逢冲则愈）。
>
> **Note:** 'Cured' (jiu) in 'a fresh calamity meeting it should be wholly cured' is recovery from illness; the 'yao xiong' (line ominous) in the last line of the verse looks like an error for 'duo xiong' (mostly ominous), which the commentary writes as 'mostly ominous and seldom auspicious', and it is translated so from the context. 'A six-clash, a six-clash changing into a clash, a clash met in a combination' are three kinds of clash: the hexagram being a Six-Clash hexagram, a Six-Clash hexagram changing into another, and a Six-Combine hexagram (or a combining line) meeting a clash. It is the same matter as the Zengshan Buyi's 'a Six-Clash changing into a clash makes a long illness hard to treat' (LY.ILL.ZS.02) and 'a change into Extinction or control also means danger in a fresh illness' (LY.ILL.ZS.03), the Yi Mao's 'a hexagram that is Reversed, Hidden, in a tomb or in Extinction is untreatable; a recent illness is cured by the three clashes, a long one dies' (LY.ILL.YM.10) and the Yi Yin (LY.ILL.YY.07); the books agree that a long illness meeting a clash surely dies, and differ slightly on a recent illness meeting a clash (here it is cured).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《易冒》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 1198 行:夏论土爻临鬼动，多凶少吉祸难禳

相关:LY.ILL.EL.14、LY.ILL.EL.16、LY.ILL.ZS.02、LY.ILL.ZS.03、LY.ILL.YM.10、LY.ILL.YY.07、LY.ILL.HJC.56

## LY.ILL.HDY.11 死气发时病讼凶、月破忧者散病者死：死气——占病、官司，动爻旺相克其世身；月破——忧者散、病者死、事不成、财无气，是用爻被月建冲

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:死气发动的时候，病和官司凶。死气，是占病、占官司时，动爻旺相，克了世和身。月破：忧愁的事散去，病的人死，事情不成，财没有气，这是用爻被月建冲的缘故。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the death-qi issues, illness and lawsuits are ominous. The death-qi: in divining an illness or a lawsuit, a moving line that is strong controls the Self and the body. Month-break: the one worried is dispersed, the sick person dies, the matter is not achieved and wealth has no qi, because the Subject line is clashed by the Month.

**《海底眼·生气死气日冲月破》原文** — 《海底眼》第 500 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Life-qi, death-qi, the Day clash and Month-break", base text; Haidi Yan, line 500*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 生气动者谋往吉， | 生气动的，谋事、出行吉； | When the life-qi moves, plans and going out are auspicious; |
| 死气发时病讼凶， | 死气发动的时候，病和官司凶； | when the death-qi issues, illness and lawsuits are ominous; |
| 日冲飞上伏爻出， | 日冲了飞爻，伏爻就出来； | the Day clashing the flying line, the hidden line comes out; |
| 月破爻冲争争空。 | 月破，爻被冲，争来争去落空。 | Month-break, the line clashed, contending and contending, it comes to Void. |

**《海底眼·生气死气日冲月破》原文** — 《海底眼》第 504 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Life-qi, death-qi, the Day clash and Month-break", base text; Haidi Yan, line 504*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 死气者： | 死气的说法： | The death-qi: |
| 如占病， | 比如占病， | for example in divining an illness, |
| 官司， | 占官司， | or a lawsuit, |
| 动爻旺相克其世身， | 动爻旺相，克了世和身， | a moving line that is strong controls the Self and the body, |
| 谓之死气。 | 叫做死气。 | is called the death-qi. |

**《海底眼·生气死气日冲月破》原文** — 《海底眼》第 508 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Life-qi, death-qi, the Day clash and Month-break", base text; Haidi Yan, line 508*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 月破者： | 月破的说法： | Month-break: |
| 忧者散， | 忧愁的事散去， | the one worried is dispersed, |
| 病者死， | 病的人死， | the sick person dies, |
| 事不成， | 事情不成， | the matter is not achieved, |
| 财无气。 | 财没有气。 | wealth has no qi; |
| 盖用爻被月建之冲也。 | 这是用爻被月建冲的缘故。 | for the Subject line is clashed by the Month. |

> **按**:这一节是「生气死气日冲月破」一段里和病有关的三句：歌诀「死气发时病讼凶」、解说「死气者：如占病，官司，动爻旺相克其世身」、解说「月破者：忧者散，病者死，事不成，财无气」。歌诀的其他三句（生气、日冲、日破）和眉批「日破为日虎神……」不专说病，没有收。「月破者：忧者散」的「忧者散」是「忧愁的事散去」还是「忧愁的人散去」，书里没有解释，这里译作前者；「病者死」是「病人死」，和黄金策、增删卜易的「用逢月破，久病难安」（LY.ILL.ZS.04）一致，不过那里说「月破看用神衰旺」，这里说得更绝对。「其世身」的「身」按字面译作 the body。 括号里「眉批：……」是书眉上的旁注，整理本排在正文里；这里分开收，说话人标为「海底眼眉批」。
>
> **Note:** This section quotes the three sentences about illness from the passage 'life-qi, death-qi, the Day clash and Month-break': the verse 'when the death-qi issues, illness and lawsuits are ominous', the explanation 'the death-qi: in divining an illness or lawsuit, a moving line strong controls its Self and body', and the explanation 'Month-break: the one worried is dispersed, the sick person dies, the matter is not achieved, wealth has no qi'. The other three lines of the verse (the life-qi, the Day clash, the Day break) and the marginal comment 'the Day break is the Day-tiger god ...' are not about illness in particular and are not quoted. Whether 'the one worried is dispersed' means 'the worry is dispersed' or 'the worrier is dispersed' the book does not explain, and it is rendered here as the former; 'the sick person dies' agrees with the Zengshan Buyi's 'the Subject meeting Month-break, a long illness is hard to settle' (LY.ILL.ZS.04), except that there 'Month-break depends on the strength of the Subject', and here it is put more absolutely. The 'body' of 'its Self and body' is rendered literally as the body. The bracketed 'marginal comment' is a note from the top margin that the edited text sets in the main text; it is quoted separately here, with the speaker marked as the marginal comment.

相关:LY.ILL.HDY.01、LY.ILL.ZS.04、LY.ILL.YM.10、LY.ILL.EL.11、LY.ILL.EL.17

## LY.ILL.HJC.37 主空无救须防中道而殂：主象落空占病大忌，要有动爻日辰冲克才有救（冲空则实、克空为用）；王洪绪说不独指空，墓绝空破有救者无妨、无救者必死，救指生扶拱合

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:主象落空（王洪绪：或入墓、绝、被破），占病是大忌；要有救才行，没救就会死在半路。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A main image that is Void (Wang Hongxu: or in a tomb, in extinction, or broken) is a great taboo in divining illness; it must have rescue, and without rescue the patient will die on the way.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6896 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6896*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 主空无救，须防中道而殂。 | 主象落空又没有救，要提防半路死掉。 | A main image that is Void with no rescue: beware of death half way. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6897 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6897*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 主象若空，占病大忌。 | 主象如果落空，占病是大忌。 | If the main image is Void, it is a great taboo in divining illness. |
| 必得动爻日辰冲克，谓之有救。 | 一定要有动爻、日辰来冲它、克它，才叫有救。 | It must be clashed or controlled by a moving line or the Day for it to be called rescued. |
| 盖冲空则实，克空为用，故也。 | 因为冲了落空的爻它就实了，克了落空的爻它就有了用。 | For clashing a Void line makes it real, and controlling a Void line puts it to use. |
| 否则必死。 | 不然一定死。 | Otherwise he will surely die. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2372 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2372*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 非独指空而言也。 | 这一句不光是指落空说的。 | This does not speak of Void alone. |
| 凡主象墓绝空破，有救者无妨，无救者必死。 | 凡是主象入墓、入绝、落空、被破，有救的没关系，没有救的一定死。 | Whenever the main image is in a tomb, in extinction, Void or broken, if there is rescue it does not matter, and if there is none he will surely die. |
| 救者，生扶拱合也。 | 所说的救，就是生、扶、拱、合。 | By rescue is meant generating, supporting, embracing and combining. |

> **打架**:怎样才算「有救」：旧注说主象落空，要动爻、日辰来冲它、克它才有救（冲空则实，克空为用），不然必死；王洪绪说不光是落空，入墓、绝、被破也算，救是生、扶、拱、合。一个用冲克把它叫起来，一个用生扶合去帮它。
>
> **Where they disagree:** What counts as 'rescue': the old commentary says a Void main image is rescued only when a moving line or the Day clashes or controls it (clashing a Void line makes it real, controlling a Void line puts it to use), otherwise he surely dies; Wang Hongxu says the line speaks not of Void alone but of tomb, extinction and being broken as well, and rescue is generating, supporting, embracing and combining. One wakes the line by clash and control, the other helps it by generation, support and combination.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2372 行:主空无救，须防中道而殂
- 《增删卜易》:异文(相似 0.8) 第 12637 行:主空无救，中道而殂
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1212 行:主空无救，须防中道而殂

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.36、LY.ILL.HJC.47、LY.ILL.HJC.41

## LY.ILL.HJC.38 禄系妻财、寿属父母：财是食禄、父母是寿命，卦中不可无、宜静不宜动；财空或无财不思饮食，父母动占病大忌（克制福神、官杀肆虐、服药不效、反促天年）；主象遇绝，财父入绝为禄绝、寿绝

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占病，妻财是食禄，父母是寿命，卦里不能没有，宜静不宜动。卦里没有财爻或者财爻落空，是不想吃东西；父母爻动是占病的大忌，因为它克制子孙（福神），官鬼这个凶煞就能肆意逞凶，吃药不见效、祷告不灵，所以说「反促天年」。旧注还说：主象遇到绝，或变到绝地，财爻遇到叫禄绝，父母爻遇到叫寿绝，整个卦象如果凶，这是死的兆头。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In divining illness, Wealth is the food-emolument and Resource is the span of life; neither may be missing from the hexagram, and both should be still and not moving. If there is no Wealth line or it is Void, he has no wish to eat; a moving Resource line is a great taboo, because it controls Output (the spirit of fortune) and lets the Pressure killer run wild, so medicine has no effect and prayer is not answered, hence 'it shortens the span of life instead'. The old commentary adds: if the main image meets extinction or changes into a place of extinction, then Wealth meeting it is the emolument cut off and Resource meeting it the life span cut off, and if the whole image is bad this is a sign of death.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6899 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6899*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 禄系妻财，空则不思饮食； | 食禄归妻财管，落空就不想吃东西； | The emolument belongs to Wealth: if it is Void, he has no wish to eat; |
| 寿属父母，动则反促天年。 | 寿命归父母管，一动反倒缩短寿数。 | the life span belongs to Resource: if it moves, it shortens the span of life instead. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6900–6901 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 6900–6901*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病，以妻财为食禄，父母为寿命。 | 占病时，拿妻财做食禄，拿父母做寿命。 | In divining illness, Wealth is taken as the food-emolument and Resource as the life span. |
| 卦中不可无，宜静不宜动。 | 卦里不能没有，宜静不宜动。 | Neither may be missing from the hexagram; they should be still, not moving. |
| 若主象遇绝，或化绝地，财遇之为禄绝，父母遇之为寿绝。 | 如果主象遇到绝，或者变到绝地，财爻遇到叫禄绝，父母爻遇到叫寿绝。 | If the main image meets extinction, or changes into a place of extinction, Wealth meeting it is the emolument cut off, and Resource meeting it is the life span cut off. |
| 大象若凶，此为死兆。 | 整个卦象如果凶，这就是死的兆头。 | If the whole image is bad, this is a sign of death. |
| 卦若无财或落空亡，乃是不思饮食。 | 卦里如果没有财爻，或者财爻落了空亡，就是不想吃东西。 | If the hexagram has no Wealth line, or the Wealth line falls into Void, he has no wish to eat. |
| 父母爻动，占病大忌。 | 父母爻发动，是占病的大忌。 | A moving Resource line is a great taboo in divining illness. |
| 以其克制福神，官杀能肆其虐，故也。 | 因为它克制子孙（福神），官鬼这个凶煞就能肆意逞凶。 | Because it controls and restrains Output (the spirit of fortune), and the Pressure killer can then run wild. |
| 主服药不效，祷神不灵。 | 主吃药不见效，祷告求神不灵。 | It means medicine has no effect and prayer to the spirits is not answered. |
| 故云反促天年。 | 所以说反而缩短寿数。 | Hence it is said that it shortens the span of life instead. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2374 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2374*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病以妻财为食禄，卦若无财或落空亡，乃是不思饮食。 | 占病拿妻财做食禄，卦里如果没有财爻或者落了空亡，就是不想吃东西。 | In divining illness Wealth is the food-emolument; if the hexagram has no Wealth line or it falls into Void, he has no wish to eat. |
| 父母爻动，占病所忌，以其克制福神，官煞能肆其虐故也，主服药无效，故云反促天年。 | 父母爻动，是占病所忌讳的，因为它克制子孙（福神），官鬼这个凶煞就能肆意逞凶，主吃药没有效果，所以说反而缩短寿数。 | A moving Resource line is what divining illness taboos, because it controls Output (the spirit of fortune) and the Pressure killer can then run wild; it means medicine has no effect, hence it is said to shorten the span of life instead. |
| 占兄弟病反宜动也。 | 占兄弟的病，反而宜于动。 | In divining the illness of a brother (the Peer line), moving is on the contrary right. |

> **按**:「占兄弟病反宜动也」这一句是王洪绪独有的，书里没有说道理。按六亲，父母生兄弟，占兄弟的病时父母爻是原神，它动起来反倒是帮忙，大概是这个缘故。「福神」是子孙爻的另一个叫法，「食禄」是口粮、吃的。旧注比王洪绪多讲了「禄绝」「寿绝」。
>
> **Note:** The sentence 'in divining a brother's illness moving is on the contrary right' is Wang Hongxu's alone, and the book gives no reason. By the six relations Resource generates Peer, so in divining a brother's illness the Resource line is the Support, and its moving is on the contrary a help; that is probably the reason. The 'spirit of fortune' is another name for the Output line, and 'food-emolument' is one's ration, what one eats. The old commentary speaks of the 'emolument cut off' and the 'life span cut off', which Wang Hongxu does not.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2374 行:禄系妻财，空则不思饮食;同文 第 2374 行:寿属父母，动则反促天年
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1215 行:禄系妻财，空则不思饮食;同文 第 1215 行:寿属父母，动则反促天年

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.21、LY.ILL.HJC.32、LY.ILL.HJC.37

## LY.ILL.HJC.41 世上临鬼不可随官入墓：世持官鬼本非吉兆，遇日辰带墓爻、本命爻随官入墓、世爻用爻化入鬼墓、带鬼动化入墓、持鬼墓发动，皆主大凶

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻持着官鬼，本来就不是吉兆；如果再遇到日辰带着墓爻，就叫「随官入墓」；本命爻随官入墓也凶。世爻、用爻变入鬼的墓，以及世爻、命爻带着鬼动、变入墓的，还有用爻、命爻、世爻持着鬼墓发动的，都主大凶。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Pressure line holding the Self line is in itself no lucky sign; if it also meets a tomb line carried by the Day, this is called 'following Pressure into the tomb'; a natal line following Pressure into the tomb is also unlucky. The Self or Subject line changing into the tomb of Pressure, the Self or natal line carrying Pressure, moving and changing into a tomb, and the Subject, natal or Self line holding the tomb of Pressure and moving, all mean great misfortune.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6910 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6910*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世上鬼临，不可随官入墓； | 世爻上临着鬼，不可以随着官鬼入墓； | Pressure sitting on the Self line: it must not follow Pressure into the tomb; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6911 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6911*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世持官鬼，固非吉兆。 | 世爻持着官鬼，本来就不是吉兆。 | A Pressure line holding the Self line is in itself no lucky sign. |
| 若遇日辰带墓爻，谓随官入墓； | 如果遇到日辰带着墓爻，叫随官入墓； | If it meets a tomb line carried by the Day, this is called following Pressure into the tomb; |
| 本命爻随官入墓，亦凶。 | 本命爻随着官鬼入墓，也凶。 | a natal line following Pressure into the tomb is also unlucky. |
| 若世爻用爻，化入鬼墓； | 如果世爻、用爻变入鬼的墓； | If the Self or Subject line changes into the tomb of Pressure; |
| 及世爻命爻带鬼动，化入墓者； | 以及世爻、命爻带着鬼动、变入墓的； | or the Self or natal line carries Pressure, moves and changes into a tomb; |
| 与用爻命爻世爻，持鬼墓发动者，皆主大凶。 | 还有用爻、命爻、世爻持着鬼墓发动的，都主大凶。 | and the Subject, natal or Self line that holds the tomb of Pressure and moves: all mean great misfortune. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2380 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2380*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡占自己病，若世上临鬼入墓于日辰，或化入墓库于爻，固非吉兆，世爻持鬼墓发动亦凶。 | 凡是占自己的病，如果世爻上临着鬼、在日辰上入墓，或者变入爻上的墓库，本来就不是吉兆，世爻持着鬼墓发动也凶。 | In divining one's own illness, if a Pressure line sits on the Self line and enters the tomb at the Day, or changes into the tomb of a line, it is in itself no lucky sign, and the Self line holding the tomb of Pressure and moving is also unlucky. |

> **按**:「命爻」「本命爻」是求占人的本命（出生年的地支）在卦里落的那一爻。「随官入墓」是官鬼入墓时，世爻或命爻跟着它一起入墓。王洪绪只讲占自己的病，旧注没有这个限定，而且列的情形多得多。
>
> **Note:** The 'natal line' is the line in the hexagram where the asker's own fate (the branch of his birth year) falls. 'Following Pressure into the tomb' is the Self or natal line entering the tomb together with the Pressure line when it does. Wang Hongxu speaks only of divining one's own illness, which the old commentary does not limit, and the old commentary lists many more cases.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2380 行:世上鬼临，不可随官入墓
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1224 行:世上鬼临，不可随官入墓

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.24、LY.ILL.HJC.54

## LY.ILL.HJC.44 用连鬼杀目前必见倾危：旧注是用爻发动又化出鬼爻（病人变鬼），无子孙日辰解救必死；王洪绪说「连」当作「变」，鬼煞是忌神，指用神化回头克，有日月动爻解救则不危，并驳「用神变官鬼必死」

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用神连着鬼煞，眼前一定看到危险：没有子孙、日辰、动爻来解救，危险就在眼前。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Subject is joined to the Pressure killer, danger is surely in view: with no Output, Day or moving line to relieve it, the danger is immediate.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6919 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6919*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用连鬼杀，目前必见倾危。 | 用神连着鬼煞，眼前一定看见危险。 | The Subject joined to the Pressure killer: danger is surely to be seen before one's eyes. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6920 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6920*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占病，不宜用爻发动，动则病体不安。 | 占病，用爻不宜发动，动了病人的身体就不安稳。 | In divining illness the Subject line should not move; if it moves, the patient's body is not at peace. |
| 若又化出鬼爻，谓病人变鬼。 | 如果又变出鬼爻，叫病人变成鬼。 | If it also changes out a Pressure line, this is called the patient turning into a ghost. |
| 卦中更无子孙日辰解救，必死无疑之断。 | 卦里再没有子孙、日辰来解救，就是一定死、没有疑问的断法。 | If in the hexagram there is no Output or Day to rescue him, the judgment is that he will surely die, without doubt. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2386 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2386*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| “连”字当作“变”字解。 | 「连」字应当当作「变」字来解。 | The character 'lian' (joined) should be read as 'bian' (change). |
| 今术家以用神变出官鬼者，断其病必死，是以词害义矣。 | 现在的术士把用神变出官鬼的，断成这病必死，这是拿字面害了本意。 | Today's practitioners judge that when the Subject changes out a Pressure line the illness is surely fatal; this is letting the words hurt the meaning. |
| 孰知鬼煞者是忌神也，用连鬼煞，即指用化回头克耳。 | 哪里知道鬼煞说的是忌神，用连鬼煞，就是指用神变出回头克罢了。 | Who knows that the 'Pressure killer' is the Adversary, and that 'the Subject joined to the Pressure killer' just means the Subject changing into a returning control. |
| 用神变回头克，而无日月动爻解救者，目前立见其危也。 | 用神变出回头克，又没有日月、动爻来解救的，眼前立刻就看到危险。 | When the Subject changes into a returning control and there is no Month, Day or moving line to relieve it, the danger is at once before one's eyes. |

> **打架**:「用连鬼杀」是什么意思：旧注读作用爻发动、又变出官鬼，叫病人变成鬼，没有子孙、日辰解救就一定死；王洪绪说「连」字应当读作「变」，「鬼煞」是忌神、不是官鬼，句子是说用神变出回头克，没有日月、动爻解救，眼前才危险，并且明说术家「用神变官鬼必死」的断法是拿字面害了本意。
>
> **Where they disagree:** What 'the Subject joined to the Pressure killer' means: the old commentary reads it as the Subject line moving and also changing out a Pressure line, called the patient turning into a ghost, and with no Output or Day to rescue him he surely dies; Wang Hongxu says the character 'lian' should be read as 'change', that the 'Pressure killer' is the Adversary and not the Pressure line, that the line means the Subject changing into a returning control, and only with no Month, Day or moving line to relieve it is the danger before one's eyes; and he says outright that the practitioners' judgment 'the Subject changing into Pressure is surely fatal' lets the words hurt the meaning.

> **按**:王洪绪这一段和他在本章最后一条（LY.ILL.HJC.57）里的话是一个意思：鬼煞的道理在忌神，不在官鬼。「回头克」是动爻变出来的爻反过来克它自己。
>
> **Note:** Wang Hongxu's passage says the same as his words in the last entry of this chapter (LY.ILL.HJC.57): the principle of the 'Pressure killer' lies in the Adversary and not in the Pressure line. A 'returning control' is when the line a moving line changes into controls that line in return.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.9) 第 2386 行:用连鬼煞，目前必见倾危
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1233 行:用连鬼杀，目前必见倾危

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.45、LY.ILL.HJC.57

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.47 用绝逢生危而有救：用爻不宜在死绝之地（一克则倒），卦中动爻生助叫绝处逢生，凶中回吉，病危而有救

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用爻不宜落在死绝的地方，因为一克它就倒。卦里有动爻来生它、帮它，叫绝处逢生，是凶里转吉的象，病虽危险也有救。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Subject line should not be in a place of death or extinction, because one control topples it. If a moving line in the hexagram generates and helps it, this is called meeting life in a place of extinction, an image of turning to luck out of misfortune: the illness is in danger, but there is rescue.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6928 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6928*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用绝逢生，危而有救； | 用爻在绝处遇到生，危险却有救； | The Subject meeting life in extinction: in danger, yet with rescue; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6929 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6929*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 大抵用爻，不宜死绝之地，以其一克则倒，故也。 | 大体上用爻不宜落在死绝的地方，因为一克它就倒了。 | In general the Subject line should not be in a place of death or extinction, because one control topples it. |
| 卦中动爻生助，谓绝处逢生，凶中回吉之象，病必将危有救。 | 卦里有动爻来生它、帮它，叫绝处逢生，是凶里转吉的象，病虽将危也有救。 | If a moving line in the hexagram generates and helps it, this is called meeting life in a place of extinction, an image of turning to luck out of misfortune: the illness is on the point of danger, but there is rescue. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2392 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2392*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡用爻逢绝，如得卦中动爻相生，谓之绝处逢生，凶中回吉之象，虽危有救。 | 凡是用爻遇到绝，如果得到卦里动爻来生它，叫绝处逢生，是凶里转吉的象，虽然危险也有救。 | Whenever the Subject line meets extinction, if a moving line in the hexagram generates it, this is called meeting life in a place of extinction, an image of turning to luck out of misfortune: though in danger, there is rescue. |

> **按**:王洪绪这一段的后两句（用神不宜太弱……），在他的下一条里又抄了一遍（LY.ILL.HJC.48），这里只收前一句。
>
> **Note:** The last two sentences of this paragraph in Wang Hongxu ('the Subject should not be too weak ...') are copied again in his next entry (LY.ILL.HJC.48), and only the first sentence is taken here.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2392 行:用绝逢生，危而有救
- 《增删卜易》:同文 第 12730 行:用绝逢生，危而有救
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1242 行:用绝逢生，危而有救

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.23、LY.ILL.HJC.37、LY.ILL.HJC.48

## LY.ILL.HJC.48 主衰得助重亦何妨：用爻不宜太旺也不宜太弱，弱则体虚力怯难痊；得日辰动爻生合扶助最吉，纵是十分重病亦不至死

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用爻虽然不宜太旺，也不宜太弱。太弱，病人就体虚力怯，一时难以痊愈。如果得到日辰、动爻来生、合、扶助，最吉，就算是十分重的病，也不至于死。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Though the Subject line should not be too strong, it should not be too weak either. If weak, the patient is frail and timid in strength and cannot quickly be healed. If it gets generation, combination and support from the Day and moving lines it is most lucky: even a most heavy illness will not lead to death.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6931 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6931*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 主衰得助，重亦何妨。 | 主象衰弱得到帮助，病再重又有什么妨碍。 | A weak main image that is helped: however heavy the illness, what does it matter? |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6932–6934 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 6932–6934*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用爻虽不宜太旺，亦不宜太弱。 | 用爻虽然不宜太旺，也不宜太弱。 | Though the Subject line should not be too strong, it should not be too weak either. |
| 弱则病人体虚力怯，卒难痊可。 | 太弱，病人就体虚力怯，一时难以痊愈。 | If weak, the patient is frail and timid in strength, and cannot quickly be healed. |
| 若得日辰动爻生合扶助最吉，纵是十分重病，亦不至死。 | 如果得到日辰、动爻来生、合、扶助，最吉，就算是十分重的病，也不至于死。 | If it gets generation, combination and support from the Day and moving lines it is most lucky: even a most heavy illness will not lead to death. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2394 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2394*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用神不宜太弱，弱则病人体虚力怯难痊，若得日辰动爻生合扶助，最吉，纵有十分重病，亦不致死。 | 用神不宜太弱，太弱病人就体虚力怯难痊，如果得到日辰、动爻来生合扶助，最吉，就算有十分重的病，也不致死。 | The Subject should not be too weak; if weak the patient is frail and timid in strength and hard to heal; if it gets generation, combination and support from the Day and moving lines it is most lucky, and even a most heavy illness will not lead to death. |

> **按**:旧注这里明说「虽不宜太旺，亦不宜太弱」，和 LY.ILL.HJC.36 的「太刚则折」是一对：两头都不行，要的是有气而不过头。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary says outright here 'though it should not be too strong, it should not be too weak', which is a pair with the 'too rigid, it snaps' of LY.ILL.HJC.36: neither extreme will do, and what is wanted is force without excess.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2394 行:主衰得助，重亦何妨
- 《增删卜易》:同文 第 12730 行:主衰得助，重亦何妨
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1244 行:主衰得助，重亦何妨

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.36、LY.ILL.HJC.47

## LY.ILL.HJC.49 鬼伏空亡早备衣冠防不测：官鬼是恶杀却不可无（得病根由、虚实、安危、鬼神情状都决于它），不上卦要寻伏，伏爻又空则病必不能痊；王洪绪说这两句只指占父母、官府、丈夫的病

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼这一爻虽然是恶煞，也不能没有：得病的原因、症候的虚实、疾病的安危、鬼神的情状，都由它来决定。它不上卦，就要去找它伏在哪里才能推测；如果伏着的爻又落空，这病一定好不了：就算有好医生，也找不到病源；就算有鬼神，也没有可以叩告的门路，是天年命尽。病轻的不在此论。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Pressure line is a malign killer, yet it cannot be missing: how the illness was got, whether it is deficient or excess, whether it is safe or dangerous, and the state of ghosts and spirits are all decided by it. If it is not in the hexagram one must look for where it lies hidden in order to reason it out; if the hidden line is also Void, the illness surely cannot be cured: even a good doctor cannot reach its source, even the spirits have no door to be petitioned, and the span of life is used up. A light illness is not counted here.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6936 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6936*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼伏空亡，早备衣冠防不测； | 鬼爻伏在空亡里，要早些备好衣冠，提防不测； | Pressure hidden in Void: prepare the robes and cap early and guard against the unforeseen; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6937–6938 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 6937–6938*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼一爻，固是恶杀，然亦不可无。 | 官鬼这一爻，本来是凶恶的煞，但也不可以没有。 | The Pressure line is a malign killer, yet it cannot be missing. |
| 盖凡得症因由，症候虚实，疾病安危，鬼神情状，皆决于此。 | 因为得病的原因、症候的虚实、疾病的安危、鬼神的情状，都由它来决定。 | For how the illness was got, whether the symptoms are deficient or excess, whether the illness is safe or dangerous, and the state of ghosts and spirits are all decided by it. |
| 若不上卦，即当寻伏，庶可推测。 | 如果不上卦，就要去找它伏在哪里，这样才可以推测。 | If it is not in the hexagram, one must look for where it is hidden, and only then can one reason it out. |
| 若伏爻又空，其病必不能痊。 | 如果伏着的爻又落空，这病一定好不了。 | If the hidden line is also Void, the illness surely cannot be cured. |
| 纵有良医，亦不能达其病源； | 就算有好医生，也找不到它的病源； | Even a good doctor cannot reach its source; |
| 纵有鬼神，亦无叩告之门，天年命尽。 | 就算有鬼神，也没有可以叩头祷告的门路，天年命数已经尽了。 | even if there are spirits, there is no door at which to petition them, and the span of life given by heaven is used up. |
| 若病轻不在此论。 | 如果病轻，不在这个说法之内。 | A light illness is not counted in this. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2396 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2396*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此两句唯言父母、官府、丈夫病，如遇官爻伏而又空者，须防不测。 | 这两句只是说父母、官府、丈夫的病，如果遇到官爻伏藏又落空，就要提防不测。 | These two lines speak only of the illness of a parent (the Resource line), an official or a husband: if the Pressure line is hidden and also Void, beware of the unforeseen. |

> **打架**:这两句用在哪些病上：旧注说官鬼是一切病的根由，不能没有，伏而又空则病必不能痊（病轻的除外）；王洪绪说这两句只讲父母、官府、丈夫的病，遇到官爻伏而又空才要提防不测。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Which illnesses these two lines apply to: the old commentary says the Pressure line is the root of every illness and cannot be missing, and if it is hidden and also Void the illness surely cannot be cured (a light illness excepted); Wang Hongxu says the two lines speak only of the illness of a parent, an official or a husband, and only when the Pressure line is hidden and Void must one beware of the unforeseen.

> **按**:「衣冠」按上下文是入殓的衣帽，「早备衣冠」是叫人早些准备后事。「伏」是官鬼不在卦里，藏在本宫首卦某一爻的下面（见 LY.ILL.HJC.06、HJC.07）。
>
> **Note:** 'Robes and cap' (yiguan) is, from the context, the clothes and cap of the burial, and 'prepare them early' tells one to get affairs ready in good time. 'Hidden' means that the Pressure line is not in the hexagram and lies under a line of the head hexagram of the home palace (see LY.ILL.HJC.06 and HJC.07).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2396 行:鬼伏空亡，早备衣冠防不测
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1247 行:鬼伏空亡，早备衣冠防不测

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.06、LY.ILL.HJC.07、LY.ILL.HJC.39、LY.ILL.DYTJ.51

## LY.ILL.HJC.53 身上飞伏双官膏肓之疾：卦身持鬼、本宫官鬼又伏世爻或用爻之下、世爻飞伏上下皆官鬼，或鬼夹在用爻卦身前后，叫双官夹身，大象不死也是沉困考终之疾

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦身上坐着鬼，而本宫的官鬼又伏在世爻下面；或者世爻持着官鬼，而本宫的官鬼伏在用爻下面；或者自己占，遇到世爻的飞爻、伏爻上下都是官鬼：总叫双官夹身，最不利。就算大局不到死，也是沉重缠困、拖到寿终的病。王洪绪补充：身指卦身和用神；鬼夹在身爻或用爻的前后，或者世爻上有鬼、身爻上又有鬼，都叫双官夹用夹身。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the hexagram Body holds Pressure while the Pressure of the home palace lies hidden under the Self line; or the Self line holds Pressure while the Pressure of the home palace lies hidden under the Subject line; or in a divination for oneself the Self line, flying and hidden, has Pressure both above and below: all this is called double Pressure clamping the body, and is most unfavorable. Even if the whole image does not show death, it is a heavy, lingering illness that lasts to the end of his days. Wang Hongxu adds: the 'body' means the hexagram Body and the Subject; Pressure lines flanking the body line or the Subject line before and after, or Pressure on the Self line and also on the body line, are all called double Pressure clamping the Subject and the body.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6951 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6951*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 身上飞伏双官，膏肓之疾； | 身上飞爻、伏爻都是官鬼，是膏肓之疾； | Pressure both flying and hidden at the body: an illness that has reached the gaohuang; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6952 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6952*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡遇卦身持鬼，而本宫官鬼爻又伏世下； | 凡是遇到卦身持着鬼，而本宫的官鬼爻又伏在世爻下面； | Whenever the hexagram Body holds Pressure while the Pressure line of the home palace also lies hidden beneath the Self line; |
| 或世持官鬼，而本宫官伏用爻之下； | 或者世爻持着官鬼，而本宫的官鬼伏在用爻下面； | or the Self line holds Pressure while the Pressure of the home palace lies hidden beneath the Subject line; |
| 或自占遇世爻飞伏，上下皆值官鬼； | 或者自己占，遇到世爻的飞爻、伏爻，上下都是官鬼； | or in a divination for oneself, the Self line with its flying and hidden lines has Pressure both above and below; |
| 总谓双官夹身，最为不利。 | 总叫双官夹身，最不利。 | All this is called double Pressure clamping the body, and is most unfavorable. |
| 若大象不死，亦是沉困之疾，考终之患也。 | 如果大局不到死，也是沉重缠困的病，是拖到寿终的忧患。 | If the whole image does not show death, it is still a heavy, lingering illness, a trouble that lasts until the end of one's days. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2404 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2404*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 身者卦身及用神也。 | 身指的是卦身和用神。 | The 'body' means the hexagram Body and the Subject. |
| 如身爻上已临官鬼，又他爻动而飞入身上来者，或身之前后夹有官鬼，或用爻前后夹有官鬼，或世上有鬼而身上又有鬼，皆谓之双官夹用夹身，大象不死，亦是沉困考终之疾也。 | 比如身爻上已经临着官鬼，又有别的爻动了飞到身上来的，或者身爻前后夹着官鬼，或者用爻前后夹着官鬼，或者世爻上有鬼而身爻上又有鬼，都叫双官夹用夹身，大局不死，也是沉重缠困、拖到寿终的病。 | If a Pressure line is already on the body line and another line moves and flies onto the body, or Pressure lines flank the body before and after, or Pressure lines flank the Subject line before and after, or the Self line carries Pressure and the body line also carries Pressure, all this is called double Pressure clamping the Subject and clamping the body; even if the whole image does not show death, it is a heavy, lingering illness that lasts to the end of his days. |
| 即如子病吉凶，得恒卦，三五爻皆是官爻，午火子孙居其四爻，鬼之中是也。 | 比如占儿子的病吉凶，得到恒卦，三爻、五爻都是官爻，午火子孙在第四爻，正在两个鬼的中间，就是这一种。 | For example, in divining the fortune of a son's illness one gets Heng: the third and fifth lines are both Pressure lines and the Wu Fire Output line stands at the fourth line, in the middle of the two Pressures; that is the case. |
| 余仿此。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest follow this. |

> **打架**:「身」指什么、哪些情形算双官夹身：旧注讲三种（卦身持鬼而官鬼伏世下，世持鬼而官鬼伏用爻下，自占世爻飞伏上下皆鬼）；王洪绪先说「身」是卦身和用神，又把鬼夹在身或用爻前后、世爻和身爻都有鬼也算进来，比旧注宽。
>
> **Where they disagree:** What the 'body' is and which cases count as double Pressure clamping the body: the old commentary gives three (the hexagram Body holds Pressure while Pressure is hidden under the Self line; the Self line holds Pressure while Pressure is hidden under the Subject line; in a divination for oneself the Self line flying and hidden has Pressure above and below); Wang Hongxu first says the 'body' is the hexagram Body and the Subject, and also counts Pressure flanking the body or the Subject before and after, and Pressure on both the Self line and the body line, which is wider than the old commentary.

> **按**:王洪绪的例子里，恒卦属震宫（木）：三爻辛酉、五爻庚申是金，金克木，是官鬼；四爻庚午是火，是子孙。子孙夹在两个官鬼中间。这是按纳甲排出来的，书里没有写。「膏肓」是心尖和横膈之间的部位，古人说药力到不了那里；「考终」是活到寿终。
>
> **Note:** In Wang Hongxu's example Heng belongs to the Zhen palace (Wood): the third line Xin You and the fifth line Geng Shen are Metal, and Metal controls Wood, so they are Pressure; the fourth line Geng Wu is Fire, which is Output. Output is flanked by two Pressure lines. This is worked out from the Najia assignment and the book does not write it. 'Gaohuang' is the place between the tip of the heart and the diaphragm, which the ancients said medicine cannot reach; 'kaozhong' is to live out one's span to its end.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2404 行:身上飞伏双官，膏肓之疾
- 《黄金策》:异文(相似 0.9) 第 1259 行:身上飞伏双官，膏盲之疾

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.06、LY.ILL.HJC.07、LY.ILL.HJC.54

## LY.ILL.HJC.54 命入幽明两墓泉世之人：身、命、世随鬼入墓，有卦墓、鬼墓、世墓、主墓；动出在卦中的是明墓、变入的是幽墓，不拘幽明皆非吉兆；大象凶又逢墓动、或世爻主象俱入墓者必死，动爻日辰冲破墓爻则无事

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:身、命、世随着鬼入墓，墓有卦墓、鬼墓、世墓、主墓几种。凡是动出来在卦里的，人人都看得见，那个墓叫明墓；变进墓里去的，人看不见，那个墓叫幽墓。不管幽的明的，都不是吉兆：大局就算不死，病也危险困顿；如果大局本来就凶，又遇到墓发动，或者世爻和主象都入了墓，一定死。得到动爻、日辰把墓爻冲破，才可能没事。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Body, natal and Self lines follow Pressure into a tomb: there are the tomb of the hexagram, the tomb of Pressure, the tomb of the Self and the tomb of the main image. A tomb that moves out in the hexagram, which everyone can see, is a bright tomb; one that a line changes into, which people cannot see, is a dark tomb. Whether dark or bright, neither is a lucky sign: even if the whole image does not show death, the illness is dangerous and hard; if the whole image is already bad and a tomb also moves, or the Self line and the main image both enter a tomb, he will surely die. If a moving line or the Day clashes and breaks the tomb line, there may be no harm.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6954 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6954*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 命入幽明两墓，泉世之人。 | 命入了幽墓和明墓两种墓，是黄泉和人世之间的人。 | A life entering both the dark and the bright tombs: a person of the springs and of the world. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6955–6959 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 6955–6959*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以日时看，身随鬼入墓，命随鬼入墓，世随鬼入墓； | 拿日和时来看，身随着鬼入墓，命随着鬼入墓，世随着鬼入墓； | Viewing by the day and hour: the Body following Pressure into the tomb, the natal line following Pressure into the tomb, the Self line following Pressure into the tomb; |
| 有卦墓、鬼墓、世墓、主墓。 | 墓分四种：卦墓、鬼墓、世墓、主墓。 | There are the tomb of the hexagram, the tomb of Pressure, the tomb of the Self and the tomb of the main image. |
| 凡遇动出卦中者，人皆见之，其墓为明； | 凡是动出来在卦里的，人人都看得见，这个墓就是明墓； | Whatever moves out in the hexagram is seen by everyone, and that tomb is a bright one; |
| 变入墓中者，人所不见，其墓为幽。 | 变进墓里去的，人看不见，这个墓就是幽墓。 | what changes into the tomb is not seen by people, and that tomb is a dark one. |
| 不拘幽明，皆非吉兆。 | 不管幽的明的，都不是吉兆。 | Whether dark or bright, none is a lucky sign. |
| 大象不死，病亦危困。 | 大局不到死，病也危险困顿。 | If the whole image does not show death, the illness is still dangerous and hard. |
| 若大象既凶，又逢墓动，或世爻主象俱入墓者，必死。 | 如果大局本来就凶，又遇到墓发动，或者世爻和主象都入了墓，一定死。 | If the whole image is already bad and a tomb also moves, or the Self line and the main image both enter a tomb, he will surely die. |
| 得动爻日辰冲破墓爻，庶几无事。 | 得到动爻、日辰把墓爻冲破，才差不多没事。 | If a moving line or the Day clashes and breaks the tomb line, there may be no harm. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2406 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2406*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以卦看有鬼墓，以世看有世墓，以用神看有主墓，凡遇此三墓出现卦中者，人皆见之，其墓为明； | 以卦来看有鬼墓，以世爻来看有世墓，以用神来看有主墓，凡是这三个墓出现在卦里的，人人都看得见，这个墓就是明墓； | Looking from the hexagram there is the tomb of Pressure, from the Self line the tomb of the Self, from the Subject the tomb of the main image; where any of these three tombs appears in the hexagram, everyone sees it, and that tomb is a bright one; |
| 变入墓中者，人所不见，其墓为幽。 | 变进墓里去的，人看不见，这个墓就是幽墓。 | what changes into the tomb is not seen by people, and that tomb is a dark one. |
| 不拘明幽，病主危困。 | 不管明的幽的，病主危险困顿。 | Whether bright or dark, the illness is dangerous and hard. |
| 或世爻用爻被官鬼两头夹之，或见有两重鬼墓夹身者，必死。 | 或者世爻、用爻被官鬼两头夹着，或者见到两重鬼墓夹住身爻的，一定死。 | If the Self or Subject line is clamped by Pressure lines on both sides, or two layers of Pressure tombs clamp the body, he will surely die. |
| 得日辰动爻冲破墓爻，庶几无事。 | 得到日辰、动爻把墓爻冲破，才差不多没事。 | If the Day or a moving line clashes and breaks the tomb line, there may be no harm. |

> **打架**:墓有几种、什么情形必死：旧注列四种墓（卦墓、鬼墓、世墓、主墓，「以日时看」），必死的条件是大象凶又逢墓动、或世爻主象俱入墓；王洪绪列三种（鬼墓、世墓、主墓，分别从卦、世爻、用神看），必死的条件是世爻用爻被官鬼两头夹着、或有两重鬼墓夹身。
>
> **Where they disagree:** How many kinds of tomb there are and what makes death certain: the old commentary lists four (the tomb of the hexagram, of Pressure, of the Self and of the main image, 'viewing by the day and hour') and makes death certain when the whole image is bad and a tomb moves, or when the Self line and the main image both enter a tomb; Wang Hongxu lists three (the tombs of Pressure, of the Self and of the main image, seen from the hexagram, the Self line and the Subject) and makes death certain when the Self or Subject line is clamped by Pressure lines on both sides or two layers of Pressure tombs clamp the body.

> **按**:「明墓」是墓爻自己动出来、摆在卦面上的；「幽墓」是动爻变进墓里去、卦面上看不见的。经文「泉世之人」四个字字面是「泉下和在世的人」，两家的注都没有解释。
>
> **Note:** A 'bright tomb' is one whose tomb line has itself moved out and lies on the face of the hexagram; a 'dark tomb' is one that a moving line changes into, which cannot be seen on the face of the hexagram. The four characters 'quan shi zhi ren' of the verse literally mean 'a person of the springs below and of the world', and neither commentary explains them.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.9) 第 2406 行:命入幽冥两墓，泉世之人
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1262 行:命入幽明两墓，泉世之人

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.24、LY.ILL.HJC.41、LY.ILL.HJC.53

## LY.ILL.HJC.56 鬼动而逢日破何妨见险之虞：官爻发动祸已成，得日辰动爻冲散则病虽凶必不至死；忌爻发动遇冲亦吉，不遇冲而被日辰动爻合住或制克，也是见险无虞

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼爻发动，灾祸就成了。如果得到日辰或动爻来冲它，叫冲散，病虽然凶险，一定不至于死。忌爻发动，遇到冲也吉；如果没有遇到冲，但得到日辰或动爻来合住它、或者制克它，也都是遇到危险而没有忧患的象。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When a Pressure line moves, the misfortune is made. If the Day or a moving line clashes it, this is called scattering it, and though the illness looks bad he will surely not die. A moving Adversary line that meets a clash is also lucky; if it meets no clash but is held by a combination or controlled and restrained by the Day or a moving line, this too is the image of meeting danger without harm.

**《黄金策·病体》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6964 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The patient's condition", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6964*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼动而逢日破，何妨见险之虞。 | 鬼爻发动，遇上日辰冲破，那么见到危险的忧虑也没有妨碍。 | Pressure moving but meeting a clash-break from the Day: what does it matter that one fears meeting danger? |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6965–6966 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 6965–6966*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官爻发动，其祸成矣。 | 官爻发动，灾祸就成了。 | When the Pressure line moves, the misfortune is made. |
| 若得日辰动爻冲之，谓冲散； | 如果得到日辰、动爻来冲它，叫冲散； | If the Day or a moving line clashes it, this is called scattering it; |
| 主其病虽见凶，必不至死。 | 主这病虽然见凶，一定不至于死。 | it means the illness, though it looks bad, will surely not lead to death. |
| 忌爻发动，遇冲亦吉。 | 忌爻发动，遇到冲也吉。 | A moving Adversary line that meets a clash is also lucky. |
| 若不遇冲，而得日辰动爻，或合住，或制克，皆是见险无虞之象。 | 如果没有遇到冲，但是日辰、动爻来合住它，或者制克它，都是见到危险而没有忧患的象。 | If it meets no clash but the Day or a moving line holds it by a combination or controls and restrains it, all this is the image of meeting danger without harm. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2410 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2410*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官爻发动，或忌神发动，其祸成矣。 | 官爻发动，或者忌神发动，灾祸就成了。 | When a Pressure line moves, or the Adversary moves, the misfortune is made. |
| 若得日辰动爻冲之，谓已冲散，主其病虽凶而不死。 | 如果得到日辰、动爻来冲它，叫已经冲散了，主这病虽凶却不会死。 | If the Day or a moving line clashes it, it is said to have been scattered, and the illness, though bad, will not lead to death. |

> **按**:「日破」是被日辰冲破，「见险」是碰到危险。旧注多讲了两条：忌爻动遇冲也吉，没遇冲时被合住或制克也无虞；王洪绪把官爻和忌神并成一句，没有收这两条。
>
> **Note:** 'Broken by the Day' is clashed and broken by the Day, and 'meeting danger' is running into danger. The old commentary adds two points: a moving Adversary line meeting a clash is also lucky, and if it meets no clash but is held by a combination or controlled there is also no harm; Wang Hongxu puts the Pressure line and the Adversary in one sentence and does not take these two.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 2410 行:鬼动而逢日破，何妨见险之虞
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1268 行:鬼动而逢日破，何妨见险之虞

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.44、LY.ILL.HJC.57
