## LY.ILL.HJC.74 主绝受伤卢医难救：主象在休囚死墓绝之地又被日辰动爻乘旺刑克，或变入死墓空绝，病必死，虽良医无效

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:主象处在休囚死墓绝的地方，又被日辰、动爻趁着旺势刑克，这病一定死；主象变成死、墓、空、绝的，也是这样。就算有好医生，也没有效果。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A main image in a place of rest, trap, death, tomb or extinction that is also punished or controlled by the Day or a moving line riding its strength: the patient will surely die; a main image that changes into death, tomb, Void or extinction is the same. Even with a good doctor there is no effect.

**《黄金策·医药》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7034 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Doctors and medicine", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7034*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 主绝受伤，虚医难救； | 主象在绝地又受了伤，虚医也难以救； | The main image in extinction and wounded: a physician of no substance can hardly save him; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7035 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7035*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 主象在休囚死墓绝之地，而被日辰动爻乘旺刑克者，其病必死。 | 主象处在休囚死墓绝的地方，又被日辰、动爻趁着旺势刑克的，这病一定死。 | A main image in a place of rest, trap, death, tomb or extinction that is also punished or controlled by the Day or a moving line riding its strength: the patient will surely die. |
| 虽有良医，亦无效。 | 就算有好医生，也没有效果。 | Even with a good doctor, it has no effect. |
| 主象变死墓空绝者，亦然。 | 主象变成死、墓、空、绝的，也是这样。 | A main image that changes into death, tomb, Void or extinction is the same. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 2450 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 2450*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 主象若遇休囚墓绝，或变入墓绝，再有克伤者，虽良医不能救也。 | 主象如果遇到休囚墓绝，或者变入墓绝，又有克伤它的，就算好医生也救不了。 | If the main image meets rest, trap, tomb or extinction, or changes into tomb or extinction, and something also controls and harms it, even a good doctor cannot save him. |

> **按**:经文写「虚医难救」，王洪绪引作「卢医难救」。卢医就是和扁鹊齐名的古代名医，和下一条的「扁鹊无功」相对；「虚医」大概是「卢医」之误，译文按经文字面译。
>
> **Note:** The verse reads 'an empty physician can hardly save him' where Wang Hongxu quotes it as 'Lu the physician can hardly save him'. Lu Yi is the ancient healer named together with Bian Que, and matches the 'Bian Que has no success' of the next entry; 'empty physician' is probably a slip for 'Lu Yi', and the translation follows the letter of the verse.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.88) 第 2450 行:主绝受伤，卢医难救
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1322 行:主绝受伤，虚医难救

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.37、LY.ILL.HJC.47、LY.ILL.HJC.75

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.123 身命章·随官入墓未为有子有孙、助鬼伤身不免多灾多病（占婴孩：世身随官入墓必死、鬼伤克世身刑冲主象必多病、财助鬼大凶、鬼持世临身亦多病）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《黄金策》身命章占婴孩的一联说：「随官入墓，不算有子有孙；助鬼伤身，免不了多灾多病。」旧注：世爻和身随着官鬼入墓，一定死；鬼伤克世、身，刑冲主象，一定多病，财爻再帮它，是大凶；鬼持世、临身，也主多病。王洪绪：子孙入墓或化官入墓、化官鬼，一定死；鬼伤克兄弟爻、使子孙爻没有根，一定多病难养；财动助鬼克兄，或鬼持世临身，也主多病。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A couplet in the Huangjin Ce's chapter on the person and fate, on divining an infant, says: 'Following Pressure into the tomb, one cannot be said to have sons and grandsons; helping the ghost to harm the Body, one cannot escape many calamities and illnesses.' The old commentary: if the Self and Body follow Pressure into the tomb, one is sure to die; if a ghost harms and controls the Self or Body, punishing or clashing the principal image, there is sure to be much illness, and still worse if Wealth helps it; a ghost holding the Self line or sitting on the Body also means much illness. Wang Hongxu: Output entering the tomb, or turning into an officer and entering the tomb, or turning into Pressure, is sure death; a ghost harming and controlling Peer so that Output has no root means much illness and a child hard to raise; Wealth moving to help the ghost control Peer, or a ghost holding the Self line or sitting on the Body, also means much illness.

**《黄金策·身命》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6207 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Person and fate", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6207*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 随官入墓，未为有子有孙； | 随着官鬼入墓，不算有子有孙； | Following Pressure into the tomb, one cannot be said to have sons and grandsons; |
| 助鬼伤身，不免多灾多病。 | 帮着鬼来伤身，免不了多灾多病。 | helping the ghost to harm the Body, one cannot escape many calamities and illnesses. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6208 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6208*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦中若见世身随官入墓者，必死。 | 卦里如果看到世爻、身随着官鬼入墓，一定死。 | If in the hexagram the Self and the Body are seen following Pressure into the tomb, one is sure to die. |
| 故曰：未为有子有孙。 | 所以说：不算有子有孙。 | Hence it says: 'one cannot be said to have sons and grandsons'. |
| 若遇鬼伤克世身，刑冲主象者，必然多病。 | 如果遇到鬼爻伤克世爻、身，刑冲主象，一定多病。 | If a ghost harms and controls the Self or the Body, punishing or clashing the principal image, there is sure to be much illness. |
| 更逢财爻助之，大凶之兆。 | 再遇到财爻帮它，是大凶的征兆。 | If a Wealth line helps it as well, it is a sign of great ill. |
| 若鬼持世临身，亦主多病。 | 如果鬼爻持世、临在身上，也主多病。 | If a ghost holds the Self line or sits on the Body, it also means much illness. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1771 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1771*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若见子孙入墓，或化官入墓，或化官鬼，必死。 | 如果看到子孙爻入墓，或者化出官爻入墓，或者化成官鬼，一定死。 | If the Output line is seen entering the tomb, or turning into an officer and entering the tomb, or turning into Pressure, one is sure to die. |
| 故曰“未为有子有孙”。 | 所以说「不算有子有孙」。 | Hence it says 'one cannot be said to have sons and grandsons'. |
| 若遇鬼伤克兄弟爻，致子孙爻无根，必然多病难养。 | 如果遇到鬼爻伤克兄弟爻，使子孙爻没有根，一定多病、难养。 | If a ghost harms and controls the Peer line so that the Output line has no root, there is sure to be much illness and the child is hard to raise. |
| 财动助鬼克兄，或鬼持世临身，亦主多病。 | 财爻发动帮助鬼爻去克兄，或者鬼持世、临身，也主多病。 | If Wealth moves to help the ghost control Peer, or a ghost holds the Self line or sits on the Body, it also means much illness. |

> **按**:这一联在身命章里是占婴孩（小孩的造化）的一段，「有子有孙」是说这个孩子立得住、活得成。「随官入墓」「助鬼伤身」和通玄妙论里同名的两节是同一个说法：随官入墓见 LY.ILL.DYTJ.27，助鬼伤身见 LY.ILL.DYTJ.29（那两节讲的是占病）。旧注「刑冲主象」的主象是所占的那一爻，占婴孩时是子孙爻。「世身」是世爻和身。
>
> **Note:** This couplet is in a passage of the chapter on the person and fate that divines an infant (a child's fate), and 'having sons and grandsons' says that the child will stand and live. 'Following Pressure into the tomb' and 'helping the ghost to harm the Body' are the same sayings as the two sections of the same names in the Tongxuan Miaolun: following Pressure into the tomb is in LY.ILL.DYTJ.27 and helping the ghost to harm the Body in LY.ILL.DYTJ.29 (those two sections are about divining illness). The 'principal image' of the old commentary is the line being divined, which in divining an infant is the Output line. 'The Self and the Body' are the Self line and the Body.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1771 行:随官入墓，未为有子有孙;同文 第 1771 行:助鬼伤身，不免多灾多病
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 650 行:随官入墓，未为有子有孙;同文 第 650 行:助鬼伤身，不免多灾多病

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.27、LY.ILL.DYTJ.29、LY.ILL.HJC.124

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.124 身命章·遇令星如风摇干、逢绝地似雨倾花（占婴孩：子孙得地健旺凶神克战无事，死绝之地决难生养）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《黄金策》身命章占婴孩时说：「遇到令星，像风摇树干；逢到绝地，像雨打落花。」旧注：凡遇凶神来克战，子孙爻得地、健旺，一定无事，即使见一点小小的悔，也像微风摇树干，伤不了它；如果在死绝之地，一定难以生养，一有克战，就像暴雨打花，很少不残败的。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Huangjin Ce's chapter on the person and fate says, on divining an infant: 'Meeting the ruling star is like wind shaking a trunk; meeting a place of extinction is like rain beating down flowers.' The old commentary: whenever an ominous spirit attacks, if the Output line holds its ground and is strong, nothing will happen, and even with a slight regret it is like a light breeze shaking a trunk and cannot harm it; if it is in a place of death or extinction, it can hardly be reared, and once attacked it is like a sudden rain beating down flowers, and few escape ruin.

**《黄金策·身命》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6216 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Person and fate", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6216*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 遇令星，如风摇干； | 遇到令星，像风摇树干； | Meeting the ruling star is like wind shaking a trunk; |
| 逢绝地，似雨倾花。 | 逢到绝地，像雨打落花。 | meeting a place of extinction is like rain beating down flowers. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6217 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6217*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡遇凶神克战，若得子孙得地健旺，则必无事； | 凡是遇到凶神来克战，如果子孙爻得地、健旺，就一定没事； | Whenever an ominous spirit attacks, if the Output line holds its ground and is strong, nothing will happen; |
| 虽见小悔，犹微风摇干，必不能伤。 | 即使见到一点小小的悔，也像微风摇动树干，一定伤不了它。 | even if a slight regret appears, it is like a light breeze shaking a trunk and surely cannot harm it. |
| 若在死绝之地，决难生养； | 如果在死绝的地方，一定难以生养； | If it is in a place of death or extinction, it can hardly be reared; |
| 一有克战，则如骤雨倾花，鲜不残败。 | 一旦有克战，就像暴雨打花，很少不残败的。 | once it is attacked, it is like a sudden rain beating down flowers, and few escape ruin. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1777 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1777*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡父母动克子，若得子孙值日辰月建，虽见小晦，犹微风摇干无妨； | 凡是父母爻发动克子孙，如果子孙爻值着日辰、月建，即使见到一点小小的晦暗，也像微风摇动树干，没有妨碍； | Whenever the Resource line moves to control Output, if Output holds the Day or the Month branch, even with a slight dimming it is like a light breeze shaking a trunk and does no harm; |
| 若逢墓绝有克战，如骤雨倾花有损。 | 如果遇到墓绝又有克战，就像暴雨打花，有损伤。 | if it meets tomb and extinction with attack besides, it is like a sudden rain beating down flowers, and there is damage. |

> **按**:「令星」是月建（当令的那个支），所以「遇令星」是子孙爻值月建，得时。「风摇干」「雨倾花」是比喻：前者树干不动，后者花被打落。这一段在身命章占婴孩的几联之间，讲的是孩子养不养得活，所以放在疾病域的生死里。王洪绪的注加了一个条件：是父母发动克子孙的时候。
>
> **Note:** The 'ruling star' is the Month branch (the branch that rules the month), so 'meeting the ruling star' is the Output line standing on the Month branch, in season. 'Wind shaking a trunk' and 'rain beating down flowers' are comparisons: in the first the trunk does not move, in the second the flowers are beaten down. The passage stands among the couplets on divining an infant in the chapter on the person and fate and is about whether the child can be raised, which is why it is placed under life and death in the illness domain. Wang Hongxu's note adds a condition: it is when Resource moves to control Output.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1777 行:遇令星如风摇干;同文 第 1777 行:逢绝地似雨倾花
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 659 行:遇令星，如风摇干;同文 第 659 行:逢绝地，似雨倾花

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.123、LY.ILL.HJC.125

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.125 身命章·子孙化鬼，孝殇十月入冥途；禄贵临爻，拜住童年登相位（旧注：子化出鬼乃九死一生之兆，贵人禄马旺相则异日必贵）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《黄金策》身命章占婴孩时说：「子孙化成鬼，像汉殇帝才十个月就走进了冥途；禄、贵人临在爻上，像元朝的拜住童年就登上相位。」旧注：子孙爻化出鬼爻，是九死一生的征兆；如果贵人、禄马一起临上、子孙又旺相，将来必定显贵；子孙遇凶而化鬼，是说孩子自己化成了鬼，所以凶，遇吉而化官，是化成了官，所以吉。王洪绪：子孙休囚，化鬼或化父母，都是死的征兆。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Huangjin Ce's chapter on the person and fate says, on divining an infant: 'Output turning into a ghost: Emperor Shang of Han went to the underworld at ten months; Salary and the Noble on the line: Baizhu rose to minister in childhood.' The old commentary: the Output line turning into a ghost line is a sign of nine deaths to one life; if the Noble, Salary and Horse all sit on it and Output is strong, he will surely be eminent one day; when Output meets ill and turns into a ghost, the child himself has turned into a ghost, hence ill, and when it meets good and turns into an officer, he has turned into an officer, hence good. Wang Hongxu: Output resting or confined and turning into a ghost or into Resource is a sign of death.

**《黄金策·身命》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 6219 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Person and fate", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6219*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙化鬼，孝殇十月入冥途； | 子孙化成鬼，像汉殇帝才十个月就走进了冥途； | Output turning into a ghost: Emperor Shang of Han went down the road to the underworld at ten months; |
| 禄贵临爻，拜住童年登相位。 | 禄、贵人临在爻上，像元朝的拜住，童年就登上相位。 | Salary and the Noble on the line: Baizhu of the Yuan rose to be minister in childhood. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 6220 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 6220*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子化出鬼，乃九死一生之兆。 | 子孙爻化出鬼爻，是九死一生的征兆。 | The Output line turning into a ghost line is a sign of nine deaths to one life. |
| 如汉殇帝，生才十月即死也。 | 比如汉殇帝，生下来才十个月就死了。 | As Emperor Shang of Han, who died when barely ten months old. |
| 子化出鬼，而贵人禄马交临，子又旺相，异日必贵。 | 子孙化出鬼爻，但是贵人、禄、马一同临上，子孙又旺相，将来一定显贵。 | If Output turns into a ghost line but the Noble, Salary and Horse all sit on it and Output is strong, he will surely become eminent. |
| 如元拜住，年十四即为相也。 | 比如元朝的拜住，十四岁就做了宰相。 | As Baizhu of the Yuan, who became minister at fourteen. |
| 盖子逢凶而化鬼，谓身化为鬼，故凶； | 因为子孙遇凶而化成鬼，是说孩子自己化成了鬼，所以凶； | For when Output meets ill and turns into a ghost, it means the child himself has turned into a ghost, hence ill; |
| 子逢吉而化官，谓身化为官，故吉。 | 子孙遇吉而化成官，是说自己化成了官，所以吉。 | when Output meets good and turns into an officer, it means he himself has turned into an officer, hence good. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1779 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1779*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙休囚化鬼化父，皆死之兆，似汉殇帝生才十月即亡； | 子孙爻休囚，又化成鬼爻或父母爻，都是死的征兆，像汉殇帝才生十个月就死了； | Output resting or confined and turning into a ghost or into Resource, all are signs of death, like Emperor Shang of Han, who died when barely ten months old; |
| 若临贵人禄马旺相，如元拜住年十四即为相。 | 如果临着贵人、禄、马又旺相，就像元朝的拜住，十四岁就做了宰相。 | if it sits on the Noble, Salary and Horse and is strong, it is like Baizhu of the Yuan, who became minister at fourteen. |

> **按**:汉殇帝（刘隆）生后一百多天即位，不满一岁就死；拜住是元朝人，十四岁做了宰相。「九死一生」是九分死、一分生，不是必死；旧注接着讲了活的条件（贵人禄马交临、子孙旺相）。王洪绪的版本把「休囚」和「化父」也算进去：子孙休囚、化鬼或化父母，都是死的征兆；旺相又带贵人禄马才像拜住。两处说的是同一件事，条件讲得略有不同。
>
> **Note:** Emperor Shang of Han (Liu Long) took the throne a little over a hundred days after birth and died before he was a year old; Baizhu was a man of the Yuan who became minister at fourteen. 'Nine deaths to one life' is nine parts death and one part life, not certain death; the old commentary goes on to give the conditions for life (the Noble, Salary and Horse all on it, and Output strong). Wang Hongxu's version counts 'resting or confined' and 'turning into Resource' as well: Output resting or confined and turning into a ghost or into Resource are signs of death, and only when it is strong and carries the Noble, Salary and Horse is it like Baizhu. The two say the same thing with slightly different conditions.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1779 行:子孙化鬼，孝殇十月入冥途;同文 第 1779 行:禄贵临爻，拜住童年登相位
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 662 行:子孙化鬼，孝殇十月入冥途;同文 第 662 行:禄贵临爻，拜住童年登相位

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.123、LY.ILL.HJC.124

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.131 总断千金赋·疾病大宜天喜，若临凶杀必生悲（旧注：天喜吉星，占病遇之虽大象凶恶不以死断，临所忌之爻动则以为悲）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《黄金策》总断千金赋说：「疾病最适宜见天喜，如果天喜临在凶杀上，一定生出悲伤。」旧注：天喜是吉星，占病遇到它，即使整个卦象凶恶，也不按死来断，因为它是喜神；如果它临在所忌的爻上发动，就认为是悲，不认为是喜。王洪绪的说法一样，把「所忌的爻」写成「忌神」。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Huangjin Ce's Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements says: 'For illness the Tianxi star is greatly helpful, but if it sits on an ominous killing there is sure to be grief.' The old commentary: the Tianxi star is a lucky star, and in divining illness, meeting it, even if the whole image is ominous one does not judge death, because it is a spirit of joy; if it sits on a taboo line and moves, I take it as grief and not as joy. Wang Hongxu says the same, writing the 'taboo line' as the Adversary.

**《黄金策·总断千金赋》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5426 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5426*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 疾病大宜天喜，若临凶杀必生悲； | 疾病最适宜见天喜星，如果天喜临在凶杀上，一定生出悲伤； | Illness is greatly helped by the Tianxi star, but if it sits on an ominous killing there is sure to be grief; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5429 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5429*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又如天喜，吉星也。 | 再如天喜，是吉星。 | Again, the Tianxi star is a lucky star. |
| 占病遇之，虽大象凶恶，不以死断，喜神故也。 | 占病遇到它，即使整个卦象凶恶，也不按死来断，因为它是喜神。 | In divining illness, meeting it, even if the whole image is ominous, one does not judge it as death, because it is a spirit of joy. |
| 若临于所忌之爻动，吾必以为悲，而不以为喜。 | 如果它临在所忌的爻上发动，我一定认为是悲，不认为是喜。 | If it sits on a taboo line and moves, I will surely take it as grief and not as joy. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1282 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1282*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又如天喜吉星也，占病遇之虽大象凶恶，竟不以死断，因天喜故也，若临忌神，我必以为悲而不以为喜。 | 再如天喜是吉星，占病遇到它，即使整个卦象凶恶，竟然不按死来断，是因为有天喜；如果它临在忌神上，我一定认为是悲，不认为是喜。 | Again, the Tianxi star is a lucky star: in divining illness, meeting it, even if the whole image is ominous one does not judge death, because of the Tianxi star; but if it sits on the Adversary, I will surely take it as grief and not as joy. |

> **按**:这一句是总断千金赋里一连串「神煞要看它临在哪一爻」的例子之一：同一联接着讲往亡（出行最怕往亡，如系吉神终获利）。旧注、王洪绪借它说明「神煞之权轻而五行之权重」：神煞吉凶要看它临的是喜爻还是忌爻。所以天喜对占病是好东西，但天喜临在忌神（克用神的那一类）上发动，反而是悲。原文「凶杀」「凶煞」是同一个字的两种写法。「天喜」这颗星婚姻域也出现过（LY.MARRY.HJC.107 里的天喜星）。
>
> **Note:** This sentence is one of a run of examples in the Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements of 'a spirit-star must be looked at for the line it sits on': the same couplet goes on to the Fleeing Death star (for travel the Fleeing Death star is feared most, but if it is a lucky spirit one finally gains). The old commentary and Wang Hongxu use it to show that 'the power of spirit-stars is light and that of the five elements heavy': whether a spirit-star is good or ill depends on whether it sits on a line one welcomes or one that is a taboo. So the Tianxi star is good for illness, but when it sits on the Adversary (the kind of line that controls the Subject) and moves, it is grief instead. 'Ominous killing' and 'ominous killing' are two writings of the same word in the text. The Tianxi star also appeared in the marriage domain (the Tianxi star in LY.MARRY.HJC.107).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.92) 第 1280 行:疾病大宜天喜，若临凶煞必生悲
- 《易隐》:同文 第 40 行:疾病大宜天喜，若临凶杀必生悲
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 218 行:疾病大宜天喜，若临凶杀，必生悲

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.129、LY.ILL.HJC.156

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.147 总断千金赋·月建乃万卜之提纲（旧注：月建中有乃是真有；书中例：五月占小儿病得大过，卦中无子孙而月建午火正旺为主象，不断其死亡）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《黄金策》总断千金赋说：「月建是万卜的总纲，怎么可以帮着凶爻作恶。」旧注：月建是龙德之神，所以占卜以它为总纲；要详细看它有没有刑冲克害、生扶拱合，和世爻、身、主象有没有牵涉，就看出吉凶；月建里有的，才是真有，如果是坏事，就是真正的坏事。书中例：五月里占小孩的病，得到大过卦，卦里没有子孙爻，但月建是午火，正旺相，作为主象，所以不断他死亡。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Huangjin Ce's Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements says: 'The Month is the guiding thread of every divination: how could it help the tyrant do harm?' The old commentary: the Month is the spirit of Dragon Virtue, and so a divination takes it as its guiding thread; look closely at whether it has punishment, clash, control or harm, and generation, support, flanking or combination, and whether it bears on the Self, the Body or the principal image, and good and ill show; what the Month has is truly there, and if it is a bad matter it is truly a bad matter. A case in the book: in the fifth month a child's illness was divined and the Daguo hexagram obtained; the hexagram had no Output line, but the Month was Wu Fire, at its peak, serving as the principal image, and so death was not judged.

**《黄金策·总断千金赋》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5137 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5137*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 月建乃万卜之提纲，岂可助桀而为虐。 | 月建是万卜的总纲，怎么可以帮着凶爻作恶？ | The Month is the guiding thread of every divination: how could it help the tyrant do harm? |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5138 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5138*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 月建乃龙德之神，故卜卦以是为提纲。 | 月建是龙德之神，所以占卜以它为总纲。 | The Month is the spirit of Dragon Virtue, and so a divination takes it as its guiding thread. |
| 须详其有无刑冲克害，有无生扶拱合； | 要详细看它有没有刑、冲、克、害，有没有生、扶、拱、合； | One must look closely at whether it has punishment, clash, control or harm, and whether it has generation, support, flanking or combination; |
| 与世身主象，有无干涉，便见吉凶。 | 和世爻、身、主象有没有牵涉，就看出吉凶。 | and whether it bears on the Self, the Body or the principal image; then good and ill show. |
| 月建中有，乃是真有。 | 月建里有的，才是真有。 | What the Month has is truly there. |
| 如坏事，乃真正坏事也。 | 如果是坏事，就是真正的坏事。 | If it is a bad matter, it is truly a bad matter. |

**书中卦例** — 《卜筮大全》第 5140 行 · *Case from the book; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5140*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 且如五月内，占小儿病，得大过卦。 | 比如五月里，占小孩的病，得到大过卦。 | For example, in the fifth month, divining a child's illness, one obtains the Daguo hexagram. |
| 卦中无子孙爻，而月建午火，正旺为主象，不断其死亡。 | 卦里没有子孙爻，但月建是午火，正旺相，作为主象，不断他死亡。 | The hexagram has no Output line, but the Month is Wu Fire, at its peak, serving as the principal image, and one does not judge death. |
| 余仿此。 | 其余照这样类推。 | The rest follow this. |

> **按**:这条的意思是：卦里没有用神（占小孩的病，用神是子孙爻），可是当月的月建正是用神那个五行、又正旺，就算数，不能因为卦里没有子孙爻就断死。「月建中有，乃是真有」是这一条的总括。「龙德」是月建的别名（月建是龙德之神）。「助桀为虐」是帮着暴君作恶，这里指月建帮着凶爻，使凶更凶（旧注接着说「忌爻旺动而又生扶之，则是助桀为虐」）。书中例是旧注自己举的，没有写明后来的结果。
>
> **Note:** The sense of this entry: when the hexagram has no Subject (in divining a child's illness, the Subject is the Output line) but the Month branch of that month is exactly the Subject's element and at its peak, it counts, and one cannot judge death merely because the hexagram has no Output line. 'What the Month has is truly there' is the summing-up. 'Dragon Virtue' is another name for the Month (the Month is the spirit of Dragon Virtue). 'Help the tyrant do harm' is to help a tyrant do evil, and here means the Month helping an ominous line to make it more ominous (the old commentary goes on: 'if the Adversary is strong and moving and is also supported, that is helping the tyrant do harm'). The case is the old commentary's own, and the book does not say how it turned out.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.87) 第 1157 行:月建乃万卦之提纲，岂可助纣而为虐
- 《易隐》:异文(相似 0.93) 第 38 行:月建乃万卦之提纲，岂可助桀而为虐
- 《黄金策》:异文(相似 0.93) 第 23 行:月建乃万卜之提纲，岂可助桀为虐

相关:LY.ILL.DYTJ.39、LY.ILL.HJC.123

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.151 总断千金赋·无故勿空（旧注：身世主象无故自落空亡大凶，占病必死；王洪绪：「故」是受伤，无故之空不可当真空，旧注误解）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《黄金策》总断千金赋说「无故勿空」四个字，两家读法相反。旧注：旬里的空亡，有有缘故而空的、有没有缘故而空的；被日、月、动爻伤克而落在空亡，叫有缘故而空，避开它是可以的；如果没有刑冲克害、身世主象自己落了空亡，叫没有缘故而空，是大凶的兆头：占病必死，占事不成，占人有难，因为空了，即使有日辰、动爻也难以扶持救援。王洪绪：「故」是受伤的意思，「勿」要当「不」讲；旬空的爻若安静又被月建日辰克制，是有伤的空，到底是无用的空；若是没有被月建日辰克伤，或者发动、或得月日动爻生扶拱合冲起，这是无故之空，出旬、值日、得合的时候仍然能办事，不可当真空论；旧注把意思弄反了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Huangjin Ce's Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements has the four characters 'wu gu wu kong', which the two commentators read in opposite ways. The old commentary: of Void within the ten-day cycle some is Void with a cause and some Void without cause; a line hurt or controlled by the Day, the Month or a moving line and in Void is Void with a cause, and avoiding the harm in this way is acceptable; if there is no punishment, clash, control or harm and the Body, Self or principal image falls into Void by itself, it is Void without cause, a greatly ominous omen: in divining illness the sick will surely die, an affair does not come to completion, a person is in trouble, for when it is Void, even the Day and moving lines can hardly support or rescue. Wang Hongxu: 'cause' means being hurt, and 'do not' must be read as 'not'; a line in the ten-day Void that is still and controlled by the Month and Day is Void with injury, in the end a useless Void; one that has not been hurt by the Month or Day, or that moves, or is supported, flanked, combined or clashed into motion by the Month, Day or a moving line, is Void without cause, and when it comes out of the cycle, when the Day falls on it, or when it gets a combination, it can still do its work, and is not to be judged as truly Void; the old commentary has the sense backwards.

**《黄金策·总断千金赋》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5186 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5186*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 无故勿空。 | 无故勿空。 | Without cause, do not be Void. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5187 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5187*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 夫旬中空亡，有有故而空者，有无故而空者。 | 旬里的空亡，有有缘故而空的，有没有缘故而空的。 | Of Void within the ten-day cycle, some is Void with a cause and some Void without cause. |
| 凡遇日月动爻伤克，而在空亡，谓有故而空，避之可也。 | 凡是遇到日、月、动爻伤克，而落在空亡，叫有缘故而空，避开它是可以的。 | When a line is hurt or controlled by the Day, the Month or a moving line and is in Void, it is called Void with a cause, and avoiding the harm in this way is acceptable. |
| 若无刑冲克害，而身世主象自落空亡，此为无故而空。 | 如果没有刑、冲、克、害，而身、世、主象自己落了空亡，这叫没有缘故而空。 | If there is no punishment, clash, control or harm, and yet the Body, Self or principal image falls into Void by itself, this is Void without cause. |
| 大凶之兆。 | 是大凶的兆头。 | It is a greatly ominous omen. |
| 占病必死，占事不成，占人有难。 | 占病，病人一定会死；占事，事情办不成；占人，这个人要遇灾难。 | In divining illness the sick will surely die, an affair does not come to completion, in divining a person he is in trouble. |
| 盖空则虽有日辰动爻，难以扶持救援之故也。 | 因为空了，即使有日辰、动爻，也难以扶持救援。 | For when it is Void, even if there are the Day and moving lines, it is hard for them to support and rescue. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1179 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1179*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 故者，谓受伤的意思，“勿”字该当它“不”字解说。 | 「故」，是受伤的意思，「勿」字应当按「不」字来解。 | 'Cause' means being hurt, and the word 'do not' should be read as 'not'. |
| 大凡旬空之爻，安静又遇月建日辰克制，这是有过之空了，即使出旬值日，亦不能为吉为凶，这样旬空，到底无用之空矣。 | 大凡旬空的爻，安静又遇到月建、日辰克制，这是有伤的空，即使出旬、值日，也不能成吉成凶，这样的旬空，到底是无用的空。 | Generally a line in the ten-day Void that is still and meets control by the Month and the Day is Void with injury, and even when it comes out of the cycle or the Day falls on it, it can make neither good nor ill; such a Void is in the end a useless Void. |
| 若旬空之爻发动，或得月建日辰生扶拱合它，或日辰冲起它，或动爻生合它，这是无故之空，待其出旬值日得合之时，仍复能事，故曰无故之空爻，勿以为空也。 | 如果旬空的爻发动，或者得到月建、日辰生、扶、拱、合它，或者日辰冲起它，或者动爻生合它，这是没有缘故的空，等它出旬、值日、得到合的时候，仍然能办事，所以说，没有缘故的空爻，不要把它当作空。 | If a line in the ten-day Void moves, or is generated, supported, flanked or combined by the Month or the Day, or is clashed into motion by the Day, or is generated or combined by a moving line, this is Void without cause, and when it comes out of the cycle, when the Day falls on it, or when it gets a combination, it can still do its work; so it is said that a Void line without cause is not to be taken as Void. |
| 虽值旬空而没有受月建日辰克伤的，不可当它真空论。 | 虽然落在旬空、却没有被月建、日辰克伤的，不可以把它当作真空来论。 | A line that falls in the ten-day Void but is not hurt by the Month or the Day is not to be judged as truly Void. |
| 又如用神化回头克，又见会局来克，来克太过，岂不是有伤了？ | 又比如用神化出回头克，又见三合局来克，克得太过，难道不是有伤了吗？ | Again, if the Subject turns into a line that controls it back, and is controlled by a three-harmony frame as well, the control being excessive, is it not hurt? |
| 若是日月不伤它，用神一空则不受其克，亦称“无故”矣。 | 如果日月不伤它，用神一旦落空就不受它的克，也叫「无故」了。 | If the Day and Month do not hurt it, once the Subject falls Void it is not subject to that control, and this too is called 'without cause'. |
| 古有避凶之说，亦近乎无故之理。 | 古人有避凶的说法，也接近没有缘故（的空）的道理。 | The ancients' talk of avoiding ill is also close to the principle of Void without cause. |
| 旧注误以无伤克之爻不可空，日月二建克它又宜空，大失先天之妙旨，又失是篇之文理矣。 | 旧注错误地认为没有受伤克的爻不可以空、日月二建克它又适宜空，大大失去了先天的妙旨，又失去了这一篇的文理。 | The old commentary wrongly takes it that a line not hurt or controlled must not be Void and that one controlled by the Day and Month is rightly Void, which greatly loses the subtle sense of the Former Heaven and loses the sense of this passage too. |

> **打架**:「无故勿空」四个字怎么读，旧注和王洪绪相反。旧注：没有被刑冲克害、身世主象自己落空的，是「无故而空」，是大凶，占病必死；被日月动爻伤克而落空的，是「有故而空」，是避凶，可以。王洪绪：「故」是受伤的意思，没有被月建日辰克伤的旬空，才是「无故之空」，它不能当真空论，出旬、值日、得合时仍然有用；被克伤的旬空才是无用的空；他明说旧注把意思弄反了。书里没有裁决，也就是说：一个不受伤、自己落空的主象，旧注断凶（占病必死），王洪绪断不算真空。
>
> **Where they disagree:** The old commentary and Wang Hongxu read the four characters 'wu gu wu kong' in opposite ways. The old commentary: a principal image that is not hurt by punishment, clash, control or harm and falls Void by itself is 'Void without cause', greatly ominous, and in divining illness the sick will surely die; one hurt or controlled by the Day, the Month or a moving line and then falling Void is 'Void with a cause', which is avoiding ill, and acceptable. Wang Hongxu: 'cause' means being hurt; only a ten-day Void not hurt by the Month and Day is 'Void without cause', which is not to be judged as truly Void and is still useful when it comes out of the cycle, when the Day falls on it or when it gets a combination; it is the ten-day Void that is hurt that is the useless Void; and he says outright that the old commentary has the sense backwards. The books do not decide: a principal image not hurt and Void by itself the old commentary judges ominous (in illness, death), and Wang Hongxu judges it not truly Void.

> **按**:原文只有「无故勿空」四个字，没有标点以外的说明，所以两家的读法都说得通：旧注读成「没有缘故的空要不得」，王洪绪读成「没有缘故（没受伤）的空不算空」。经文翻成「Without cause， do not be Void」是照字面，保持两种读法都成立。「真空」「避凶而空」是旧注在紧接的「卦遇凶星，避之则吉」里的用语（LY.ILL.HJC.156）。「先天」指易的本来道理。「旬空」是起卦的那一旬里轮空的两个地支；三合局在王洪绪这里是来克的一方（会局克用神）。本库在疾病域收这一条，因为旧注给了「占病必死」这个断语；和天玄赋、易隐等讲「用空」的几条（LY.ILL.EL.11、ZS.04、ZS.05）互相照应。
>
> **Note:** The text has only the four characters 'wu gu wu kong' with no explanation, so both readings are possible: the old commentary reads it as 'Void without cause is not to be had', Wang Hongxu as 'Void without cause (not hurt) does not count as Void'. The line is translated literally as 'Without cause, do not be Void', so that both readings stand. 'True Void' and 'Void to avoid ill' are terms of the old commentary in the immediately following 'a hexagram that meets an ominous star: avoiding it is auspicious' (LY.ILL.HJC.156). 'The Former Heaven' means the original principle of the Changes. 'The ten-day Void' is the two branches left out of the ten-day cycle of the casting; in Wang Hongxu a three-harmony frame is the side that controls (a combined frame controlling the Subject). It is taken in the illness domain because the old commentary gives the verdict 'in divining illness the sick will surely die'; it answers the several entries that speak of the Subject Void (LY.ILL.EL.11, ZS.04, ZS.05).

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.156、LY.ILL.EL.11、LY.ILL.ZS.04、LY.ILL.ZS.05、LY.ILL.DYTJ.51

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.153 总断千金赋·墓多暗昧（旧注：凶爻要入墓，吉爻不要入墓；占病遇之九死一生；主象带刑动入墓者占病必死、占讼入狱）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《黄金策》总断千金赋说：「墓多暗昧。」旧注：大致上凶爻要入墓，吉爻不要入墓；《金锁玄关》有人墓、事墓、鬼墓的分别，《天玄赋》有身、世、命随着鬼入墓的说法，都是大凶的兆头；占病遇到，九死一生；捕获遇到，深深躲藏难找；人物遇到，愚昧不振作；失脱遇到，暗暗藏着看不见；因为墓是滞塞的意思，是暗昧不明的象；卦中主象带着刑、发动、入了墓的，占病必死，占官司入狱。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Huangjin Ce's Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements says: 'The tomb brings much obscurity.' The old commentary: in general an ominous line should enter the tomb and a lucky line should not; the Jinsuo Xuanguan distinguishes the person's tomb, the matter's tomb and the ghost's tomb, and the Tianxuan Fu has the saying of the Body, Self and natal line entering the tomb following the ghost, all greatly ominous omens; in divining illness, meeting it is nine deaths to one life; in a capture the quarry hides deep and is hard to find; in a person, he is dull and does not rouse himself; in a loss, it lies hidden and unseen; for the tomb means stagnation, the image of obscurity and unclarity; when the principal image in the hexagram carries punishment, moves and enters the tomb, in divining illness the sick will surely die and in a lawsuit one goes to prison.

**《黄金策·总断千金赋》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5323 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5323*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 墓多暗昧。 | 墓多暗昧。 | The tomb brings much obscurity. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5324–5327 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 5324–5327*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 大抵凶爻要入墓，吉爻不要入墓。 | 大致凶爻要入墓，吉爻不要入墓。 | In general an ominous line should enter the tomb and a lucky line should not. |
| 《金锁玄关》有：人墓、事墓、鬼墓之别。 | 《金锁玄关》有：人墓、事墓、鬼墓的分别。 | The Jinsuo Xuanguan distinguishes the person's tomb, the matter's tomb and the ghost's tomb. |
| 《天玄赋》有身、世、命，随鬼入墓之论。 | 《天玄赋》有身、世、命，随着鬼入墓的说法。 | The Tianxuan Fu has the saying of the Body, the Self and the natal line entering the tomb following the ghost. |
| 皆大凶之兆。 | 都是大凶的兆头。 | All are greatly ominous omens. |
| 占病遇之，九死一生； | 占病遇到它，九死一生； | In divining illness, meeting it is nine deaths to one life; |
| 捕获遇之，深遁难觅； | 捕获遇到它，深深躲藏难找； | in a capture, the quarry hides deep and is hard to find; |
| 人物遇之，愚蒙不振； | 人物遇到它，愚昧不振作； | in a person, he is dull and does not rouse himself; |
| 失脱遇之，暗藏不见。 | 失脱遇到它，暗暗藏着看不见。 | in a loss, it lies hidden and unseen. |
| 盖墓者，滞也。 | 因为墓，是滞塞的意思。 | For the tomb means stagnation. |
| 暗昧不明之象。 | 是暗昧不明的象。 | It is the image of obscurity and unclarity. |
| 卦中主象带刑动入墓者，占病必死，占讼入狱。 | 卦中主象带着刑、发动、入了墓的，占病必死，占官司入狱。 | When in the hexagram the principal image carries punishment, moves and enters the tomb, in divining illness the sick will surely die and in a lawsuit one goes to prison. |

> **按**:这里的「墓」是十二长生里的墓位（入墓）。「人墓、事墓、鬼墓」是《金锁玄关》的分别，书里没有展开；「身、世、命随鬼入墓」是天玄赋的说法（LY.ILL.TXF.06）和断易天机的「随官入墓」（LY.ILL.DYTJ.27）是同一件事：占病遇到，断易天机写「十占九死」，这里写「九死一生」，两处意思相同。这一条只有「占病」「占讼」两句是断语，「捕获」「人物」「失脱」讲的是别的事，为了保持这一整段完整一并收在这里。王洪绪对这一联的评语是十二长生里只有长生、墓、绝要紧（LY.ILL.HJC.129 收过），这里没有专门的王洪绪段。「愚蒙」按字面是愚昧蒙昧。
>
> **Note:** The 'tomb' here is the Tomb stage of the twelve stages of life (entering the tomb). 'The person's tomb, the matter's tomb, the ghost's tomb' is the Jinsuo Xuanguan's distinction, not expanded in the book; 'the Body, Self and natal line entering the tomb following the ghost' is the Tianxuan Fu's saying (LY.ILL.TXF.06) and is the same thing as 'following Pressure into the tomb' in the Duanyi Tianji (LY.ILL.DYTJ.27): for illness the Duanyi Tianji writes 'nine of ten divinations die', here it is 'nine deaths to one life', the same sense. Only the two sentences 'in divining illness' and 'in a lawsuit' are verdicts for our domain; 'a capture', 'a person' and 'a loss' are about other matters, and are kept here to keep the passage whole. Wang Hongxu's comment on this couplet is that of the twelve stages of life only Birth, Tomb and Extinction matter (taken in LY.ILL.HJC.129), and there is no separate Wang Hongxu passage here.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.152、LY.ILL.HJC.129、LY.ILL.DYTJ.27、LY.ILL.TXF.06

---

## LY.ILL.HJC.155 总断千金赋·死绝岂可持身（旧注：死绝二爻临持世身主象必不利，占人有难、占病无救、占医无效、占事不济，变动化入亦然）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:《黄金策》总断千金赋说：「死、绝，怎么可以持身？」旧注：死、绝两爻，临在、持着世、身、主象上，一定不利：占人有灾难，占病没有救，占医没有效，占事不成；变动化入的也是这样。王洪绪的这一条评语只有半句，「死、绝于日辰的爻，临持世、身、用神的，各种占都不利，变动化入」，原文到这里就断了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Huangjin Ce's Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements says: 'Death and Extinction: how can they hold the Body?' The old commentary: when the Death and Extinction lines sit on or hold the Self, the Body or the principal image, it is sure to be unfavourable: in divining a person he is in difficulty, in divining illness there is no rescue, in divining a doctor he is ineffective, an affair does not succeed; so too when they are moved or changed into. Wang Hongxu's comment on this has only half a sentence, 'a line in Death or Extinction by the Day branch that sits on or holds the Self, the Body or the Subject: no divination is favourable, in moving and changing into', where the text breaks off.

**《黄金策·总断千金赋》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5386 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "The Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5386*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 死绝岂可持身； | 死、绝，怎么可以持身？ | Death and Extinction: how can they hold the Body? |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5387 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5387*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 死绝二爻，临持世身主象者，必不利。 | 死、绝两爻，临在、持着世、身、主象上，一定不利。 | When the Death and Extinction lines sit on or hold the Self, the Body or the principal image, it is sure to be unfavourable. |
| 占人有难，占病无救，占医无效，占事不济。 | 占人有灾难，占病没有救，占医没有效，占事不成。 | In divining a person he is in difficulty, in divining illness there is no rescue, in divining a doctor he is ineffective, an affair does not succeed. |
| 变动化入者亦然。 | 变动化入的也是这样。 | So too when they are moved or changed into. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1253 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1253*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 死绝于日辰之爻临持世身用神者，诸占不利，变动化入 | 死、绝于日辰的爻，临持世、身、用神的，各种占都不利，变动化入 | A line in Death or Extinction by the Day branch that sits on or holds the Self, the Body or the Subject: no divination is favourable, in moving and changing into |

> **按**:这是「刑害不宜临用」那一联（LY.ILL.HJC.130）的下半句。「死绝」是十二长生里的死、绝两位，不是「死亡」。「占医无效」的「医」是请医生。王洪绪的评语在原书里印到「变动化入」就断了（是原书如此，不是本库截的），所以这里只有半句。「持身」的「身」书里连说「世、身、主象」，「身」应是卦身。
>
> **Note:** This is the second half of the couplet 'punishment and harm should not fall on the Subject' (LY.ILL.HJC.130). 'Death and Extinction' are two of the twelve stages of life and not 'death'. The 'doctor' of 'in divining a doctor' is calling in a physician. Wang Hongxu's comment is printed in the book up to 'in moving and changing into' and breaks off (this is how the book is, not cut by this library), so there is only half a sentence here. The 'Body' of 'hold the Body' is, as the text says 'the Self, the Body and the principal image' in a run, the hexagram's Body.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1253 行:死绝岂可持身
- 《增删卜易》:异文(相似 0.83) 第 5497 行:死绝岂可持
- 《易隐》:同文 第 40 行:死绝岂可持身
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 188 行:死绝岂可持身

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.130、LY.ILL.HJC.153

## LY.ILL.HZL.08 占医药·青龙临用爻或福德爻病重终可疗、青龙空亡卦无吉解病凶；白虎临父母当损、值财妻遭伤、子孙际遇终成否、兄弟逢之亦不昌、更并官爻临世上自身须忌灾殃

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:青龙临在用爻或福德爻上，病虽重，终究可治；青龙空亡、卦里又没有吉的化解，病凶。白虎临父母，父母当受损；若落在财上，妻子遭伤；遇上子孙，终究成否难定；兄弟遇上它，也不昌盛。再加官爻临在世上，自己就要忌灾殃。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With the Azure Dragon sitting on the Subject line or the Output line, the illness, though heavy, can in the end be treated; with the Azure Dragon Void and the hexagram having no lucky resolution, the illness is ominous. The White Tiger sitting on Resource harms it; if it falls on Wealth the wife is injured; meeting Output, success in the end is uncertain; and Peer meeting it does not flourish either. And with the Pressure line also sitting on the Self, one must guard against calamity for oneself.

**《火珠林·占医药》原文** — 《火珠林》第 468 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining doctors and medicine", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 468*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 青龙临用爻或福德爻， | 青龙临在用爻或福德爻上， | With the Azure Dragon sitting on the Subject line or the Output line, |
| 其病虽重终可疗。 | 病虽重，终究可治； | the illness, though heavy, can in the end be treated; |
| 青龙空亡卦无吉解， | 青龙空亡、卦里又没有吉的化解， | with the Azure Dragon Void and the hexagram having no lucky resolution, |
| 病凶。 | 病情凶险。 | the illness is ominous. |

**《火珠林·占医药》原文** — 《火珠林》第 469 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining doctors and medicine", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 469*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 白虎临父母当损， | 白虎临父母，父母当受损； | The White Tiger sitting on Resource harms it; |
| 若值财上妻遭伤。 | 若落在财上，妻子遭伤。 | if it falls on Wealth the wife is injured. |
| 子孙际遇终成否， | 子孙遇上它，终究成不成难定； | Output meeting it, success in the end is uncertain; |
| 兄弟逢之亦不昌。 | 兄弟遇上它，也不昌盛。 | and Peer meeting it does not flourish either. |
| 更并官爻临世上， | 再加官爻临在世上， | And with the Pressure line also sitting on the Self, |
| 自身须忌有灾殃。 | 自己就要忌灾殃。 | one must guard against calamity for oneself. |

> **按**:这一段是天玄赋的注里引的「苍屏云」那首诗（LY.ILL.TXF.04）的另一份：「青龙空亡并福德，病人平日无阴骘……白虎加临主哭丧，临克父爻当有咎，并财妻位必遭伤。子孙际会终成否，兄弟相逢更不昌，更值官爻持世上，己身须恐有灾殃」——这里「白虎临父母当损，若值财上妻遭伤，子孙际遇终成否，兄弟逢之亦不昌，更并官爻临世上，自身须忌有灾殃」几句几乎一样，只是没有「苍屏云」三个字，也没有那一首前面的「青龙」几句。「终成否」按「终究成不成（难定）」译，原文没有说结果。「福德爻」译作 Output line。
>
> **Note:** This passage is another copy of the poem quoted in the Tianxuan Fu's note as 'Cangping says' (LY.ILL.TXF.04): 'the Azure Dragon Void together with Output, the sick person has no hidden merit in ordinary days ... the White Tiger added means mourning, controlling the Resource line there will be harm, joining the Wealth position the wife will surely be injured; Output meeting it, success in the end is uncertain, Peer meeting it flourishes even less, and if in addition the Pressure line holds the Self, one must fear calamity for oneself'. Here 'the White Tiger sitting on Resource harms it, if it falls on Wealth the wife is injured, Output meeting it, success in the end is uncertain, Peer meeting it does not flourish either, and with the Pressure line also sitting on the Self, one must guard against calamity for oneself' is almost the same, only without the words 'Cangping says' and without the Azure Dragon lines before it in that poem. 'Zhong cheng fou' is rendered 'success in the end is uncertain', and the original does not say the outcome. 'Fu de yao' is rendered the Output line.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 4972 行:子孙际会终成否
- 《断易天机》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 1613 行:子孙际会终成否

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.04、LY.ILL.HZL.07、LY.ILL.HZL.09

---

## LY.ILL.HZL.18 妻财独发：生鬼伤父，问病难瘳、占亲无路；财动克父亦能生鬼，然财爻宜旺不宜空、宜静不宜动

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:妻财独发，生鬼、伤父；问病，难以痊愈，占亲人，没有出路。大抵财动克父，也能生鬼；但财爻要旺、不要空，要静、不要动。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Wealth moving alone generates the ghost and injures the father; asking about illness, it is hard to be cured, and divining a relative, there is no way out. Broadly, Wealth moving controls the father and can also generate the ghost; but the Wealth line should be strong and not Void, still and not moving.

**《火珠林·妻财独发》原文** — 《火珠林》第 196–197 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Wealth moving alone", base text; Huozhu Lin, lines 196–197*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 妻财独发，生鬼伤父；问病难瘳，占亲无路。 | 妻财独发，生鬼、伤父；问病，难以痊愈，占亲人，没有出路。 | Wealth moving alone generates the ghost and injures the father; asking about illness, it is hard to be cured, and divining a relative, there is no way out. |
| 大抵财动克父，亦能生鬼； | 大抵财动克父，也能生鬼； | Broadly, Wealth moving controls the father and can also generate the ghost; |
| 然财爻宜旺，不宜空；宜静、不宜动。 | 但财爻要旺、不要空，要静、不要动。 | but the Wealth line should be strong and not Void, still and not moving. |

> **按**:这一节的标题是「妻财独发」，头两句「妻财独发，生鬼伤父」说的是财爻一个人动：动了就克父母（伤父）、又生官鬼（鬼），所以问病难愈、占亲人无路。「大抵财动克父，亦能生鬼」是给这个道理的解释；「然财爻宜旺，不宜空；宜静、不宜动」一句是说财爻本身宜旺相、宜静，不是专门讲病的，这里连着收下来。下文讲占脱货、占婚姻、占讼的几句（惟占脱货要财爻发动……）不在病的范围，不收。「父」在这里是父母爻（Resource），「占亲」是占亲人。这和天玄赋「妻财独发」的说法，以及易冒等处的「财动克父」（LY.ILL.YM.42 一带）是同一类。
>
> **Note:** The heading of this section is 'Wealth moving alone', and its first two lines 'Wealth moving alone generates the ghost and injures the father' say that when the Wealth line alone moves it controls Resource (injuring the father) and also generates the Pressure line (the ghost), and so asking about illness it is hard to cure and divining a relative there is no way out. 'Broadly, Wealth moving controls the father and can also generate the ghost' is the explanation given for this; 'but the Wealth line should be strong and not Void, still and not moving' says the Wealth line itself should be strong and still, and is not about illness in particular, and is taken along here as it runs on. The following lines about divining the disposal of goods, marriage and lawsuits ('only in divining the disposal of goods must the Wealth line move ...') are outside illness and are not taken. The 'father' here is the Resource line, and 'divining a relative' is divining a relative. This is of the same kind as the Tianxuan Fu's saying on Wealth moving alone, and the 'Wealth moving controls the father' in the Yi Mao and elsewhere (around LY.ILL.YM.42).

相关:LY.ILL.HZL.17、LY.ILL.HZL.07

---

## LY.ILL.TXF.05 月解交重灾渐退、天医发动便回生：卦中得月解天医发动持世或持克用爻者虽重病不死；旺相最吉日下即愈、墓绝稍慢直待旺日方安；若值空亡其病难疗

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:月解交重，灾祸渐渐退去；天医发动，当下就回生。注说：月解、天医见后面的吉神章。卦里如果得到它们发动、持世，或者持着、克着用爻，即使重病也不会死；旺相最吉，当天就好；墓绝的稍慢，要等到旺的日子才安；如果落空亡，病就难治。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Month Release crossing heavily, calamity gradually retreats; Heavenly Doctor moving, one returns to life at once. The commentary says: Month Release and Heavenly Doctor are given in the later chapter on the lucky spirits. If the hexagram has them moving and holding the Self, or holding or controlling the Subject line, then even in a heavy illness the patient does not die; if strong it is most auspicious and the cure comes that very day; if in a tomb or Extinction it is a little slower, and one must wait for a strong day to be at peace; if it meets Void, the illness is hard to treat.

**《天玄赋·天玄赋·十七、疾病章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 4975 行 · *Tianxuan Fu (Rhapsody of the Heavenly Mystery), "Tianxuan Fu, chapter 17: illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4975*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 月解交重灾渐退， | 月解交重，灾祸渐渐退去； | Month Release crossing heavily, calamity gradually retreats; |
| 天医发动便回生。 | 天医发动，当下就回生。 | Heavenly Doctor moving, one returns to life at once. |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 4976 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4976*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 月解、天医， | 月解、天医， | Month Release and Heavenly Doctor: |
| 见后吉神章。 | 见后面的吉神章。 | see the later chapter on the lucky spirits. |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 4977 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4977*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦中若得发动持世， | 卦里如果得到它们发动持世， | If the hexagram has them moving and holding the Self, |
| 或持克用爻者， | 或者持着、克着用爻的， | or holding or controlling the Subject line, |
| 虽重病不死。 | 即使重病也不会死； | then even in a heavy illness the patient does not die; |
| 旺相最吉， | 旺相最吉， | if strong it is most auspicious, |
| 日下即愈； | 当天就好； | and the cure comes that very day; |
| 墓绝稍慢， | 墓绝的稍慢， | if in a tomb or Extinction it is a little slower, |
| 直待旺日方安。 | 要等到旺的日子才安； | and one must wait for a strong day to be at peace; |
| 若值空亡， | 如果落空亡， | if it meets Void, |
| 其病难疗。 | 病就难治。 | the illness is hard to treat. |

> **按**:月解、天医是吉神，天玄赋自己说「见后吉神章」——本库还没有收吉神章。这一条的判语是：月解或天医发动、持世，或持克用爻的，重病也不死；和易隐「生死·愈」（LY.ILL.YY.04）「用爻遇龙福月解天医天喜生合即愈」一致；和易林补遗（LY.ILL.EL.13）里「月解天医同发而用爻已遭伤仍死」的例子不冲突——那是说用爻被伤时，月解天医也救不回。 卜筮大全的这一章后面还有「虎易评注」（今人加的），本库不引。
>
> **Note:** Month Release and Heavenly Doctor are lucky spirits, and the Tianxuan Fu itself says 'see the later chapter on the lucky spirits'; this library has not yet quoted that chapter. The verdict of this entry is that with Month Release or Heavenly Doctor moving and holding the Self, or holding or controlling the Subject line, even a heavy illness does not kill, which agrees with the Yi Yin's 'life and death: recovery' (LY.ILL.YY.04), 'the Subject line meeting the Dragon, Fortune, Month Release, Heavenly Doctor or Heavenly Joy and being generated or combined, he recovers at once'; and it does not conflict with the example in the Yilin Buyi (LY.ILL.EL.13) in which 'Month Release and Heavenly Doctor move together but the Subject line is already wounded and he still dies' — that says that when the Subject line is wounded even Month Release and Heavenly Doctor cannot bring it back. After this chapter the Buyi Daquan also has 'Huyi's commentary' (added by a modern hand), which this library does not quote.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 1615 行:月解交重灾渐退;同文 第 1615 行:天医发动便回生
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1856 行:月解交重灾渐退;同文 第 1856 行:天医发动便回生

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.04、LY.ILL.TXF.06、LY.ILL.YY.04、LY.ILL.EL.13

## LY.ILL.TXF.06 入墓卦：明夷观贲需临切忌世身入墓、大畜丰同蛊夬莫逢财鬼俱兴；怕身世随鬼入墓决不瘥（观二爻乙巳鬼身亦在二爻、贲初爻己卯鬼世亦在初爻）；苍屏云八宫化入墓——乾化艮父丧、坤化巽母亡、震化坤长男厄、巽化坤长女当、坎化巽中男厄、离化乾中女卒、艮化巽少男倾、兑化艮少女卒，十人得病九人故；乾化艮子占父谓之应题大凶；财鬼不动未必丧亡、财鬼空亡虽病不死

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:明夷、观、贲、需、临，切忌世和身入墓；大畜、丰、同（人）、蛊、夬，不要遇到财和鬼一起兴动。注说：占病怕身、世跟着鬼入墓，一定治不好。比如风地观的二爻乙巳，鬼和身也在二爻，如果入了墓，就是身随鬼入墓；山火贲卦初爻己卯，鬼和世也在初爻，如果入了墓，就是世随鬼入墓；上面两卦举作例子，其余卦照这样仿。苍屏说：乾化入艮，父亲遭丧；坤化入巽，母亲忧亡；震化入坤，长男有厄；巽化入坤，长女当灾；坎化入巽，中男遭厄；离化入乾，中女暴卒；艮化入风（巽），少男倾倒；兑化入山（艮），少女暴卒；这是八宫化来入墓，十个人得病九个亡故。上面说的卦墓，不能单说乾化入艮墓只忧父亲的亡，而要看占病都不吉；假如儿子占父亲，遇到乾化艮，叫做应题，是大凶。大畜等五个卦，占病大忌；财和鬼不动，不一定死；财和鬼空亡，虽病也不死；必须把它的条理理清了，才可以断为全凶。上文所说的财鬼发动，也有吉凶的分别：比如雷火丰卦，鬼动忌占兄弟，财动忌占父母和自己本人；妻子占丈夫，终究没有凶；其余卦仿此。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Mingyi, Guan, Bi (Adornment), Xu and Lin: most to be avoided is the Self and the Body entering a tomb; Daxu, Feng, Tong(ren), Gu and Guai: do not meet Wealth and the ghost both rising. The commentary says: in divining illness one fears the Body and the Self following the ghost into a tomb, and then there is surely no recovery. For example, in Feng-Di Guan the second line Yisi has the ghost and the Body also on the second line; if it enters a tomb, that is the Body following the ghost into a tomb; in the hexagram Bi (Adornment), Mountain over Fire, the first line Jimao has the ghost and the Self also on the first line, and if it enters a tomb, that is the Self following the ghost into a tomb; these two hexagrams are given as examples and the others follow. Cangping says: Qian changing into Gen, the father is in mourning; Kun changing into Xun, the mother worried to death; Zhen changing into Kun, calamity for the eldest son; Xun changing into Kun, the eldest daughter takes it; Kan changing into Xun, calamity for the middle son; Li changing into Qian, the middle daughter dies suddenly; Gen changing into the wind (Xun), the youngest son topples; Dui changing into the mountain (Gen), the youngest daughter dies suddenly; these are the eight palaces changing into a tomb, and of ten who fall ill nine die. As for the tomb hexagrams above, one may not take Qian changing into the Gen tomb as only a worry for the father's death, but must look at illness in all of them as inauspicious; suppose a son divines about his father and meets Qian changing into Gen: it is called 'answering the topic' and is greatly ominous. The five hexagrams such as Daxu are greatly to be avoided in divining illness; if Wealth and the ghost do not move, death is not certain; if Wealth and the ghost are Void, though ill he will not die; one must grasp its threads before judging it wholly ominous. The Wealth and ghost moving mentioned above also have a division of good and ill: for example in the hexagram Feng, Thunder over Fire, the ghost moving is to be avoided in divining about brothers, Wealth moving in divining about parents and oneself; a wife divining about her husband has in the end no ill; the other hexagrams follow this.

**《天玄赋·天玄赋·十七、疾病章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 4979 行 · *Tianxuan Fu (Rhapsody of the Heavenly Mystery), "Tianxuan Fu, chapter 17: illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4979*

| 大畜、丰、同、蛊、夬， | 大畜、丰、同（人）、蛊、夬， | Daxu, Feng, Tong(ren), Gu and Guai: |
| 莫逢财鬼俱兴。 | 不要遇到财和鬼一起兴动。 | do not meet Wealth and the ghost both rising. |

| 占病大忌。 | 占病是大忌； | are greatly to be avoided in divining illness; |
| 财鬼不动， | 财和鬼不动， | if Wealth and the ghost do not move, |
| 未必丧亡； | 不一定死； | death is not certain; |
| 财鬼空亡， | 财和鬼空亡， | if Wealth and the ghost are Void, |
| 虽病不死。 | 虽然有病也不死； | though ill he will not die; |
| 必须把其条贯， | 必须把它的条理理清了， | one must grasp its threads, |
| 方可断曰全凶。 | 才可以断为全凶。 | and only then may one judge it wholly ominous. |

| 鬼动忌占兄弟， | 鬼动忌占兄弟， | the ghost moving is to be avoided in divining about brothers, |
| 财动忌占父母及本身； | 财动忌占父母和自己本人； | Wealth moving in divining about parents and oneself; |

| 余卦仿此。 | 其余的卦仿此。 | the other hexagrams follow this. |

> **打架**:周易尚占和文王金钱课按卦名断病，对这里点名的几个卦说得相反或轻重不同：观，周易尚占说「财聚病痊」，文王金钱课说「占病即愈」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.02、WWJQ.03）；贲，周易尚占说「病安」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.03）；大畜，周易尚占说「病脱」，文王金钱课说「疾病口舌渐渐磨」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.02、WWJQ.03）；丰，文王金钱课说「疾病渐好」，周易尚占说「病人沉滞」（LY.ILL.WWJQ.03、ZSZ.04）；夬，周易尚占说「病讼迟延」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.04）；临，周易尚占说「病犯崇侵」，文王金钱课补录部分说「疾病即痊」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.07、WWJQ.05）。不过天玄赋这里说的是有条件的：观、贲、需、临是世身入墓才忌，大畜、丰、同、蛊、夬是财鬼俱兴才忌，不是见到卦名就凶。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** The Zhouyi Shangzhan and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke judge illness by the name of the hexagram, and for several of the hexagrams named here they say the opposite, or a lighter thing: for Guan the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'wealth gathers and the illness is cured' and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'divining illness, it is cured at once' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.02, WWJQ.03); for Bi the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the illness is settled' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.03); for Daxu (Great Accumulation) the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the illness is shed' and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'illness and quarrels are gradually worn away' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.02, WWJQ.03); for Feng the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says 'the illness gradually gets better' and the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the sick are held fast' (LY.ILL.WWJQ.03, ZSZ.04); for Guai the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'illness and lawsuit are delayed' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.04); for Lin the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the illness offends a curse that attacks' and the Wenwang Jinqian Ke, in its supplementary part, says 'the illness is cured at once' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.07, WWJQ.05). The Tianxuan Fu here, however, speaks with a condition: Guan, Bi, Xu (Waiting) and Lin are to be feared only when the Self and the Body enter a tomb, and Daxu, Feng, Tongren, Gu and Guai only when Wealth and Pressure both rise, so that the name of the hexagram alone does not make it ominous. The books do not decide.

> **按**:这一节说的是「入墓卦」：两句赋点出十个卦（明夷、观、贲、需、临是「世身入墓」，大畜、丰、同、蛊、夬是「财鬼俱兴」），注里先举观、贲两例讲什么叫「身随鬼入墓」「世随鬼入墓」，再引苍屏的一首歌诀，讲八宫里各卦化入墓（乾化艮、坤化巽、震化坤、巽化坤、坎化巽、离化乾、艮化风、兑化山）对应家里的哪一位亲人有灾——「艮化风内」「兑化山中」的「风」是巽、「山」是艮。「应题」这里译作「answering the topic」，按字面，意思大概是卦象应了所问的人。「同」按上下文是同人。「条贯」是条理、头绪，「必须把其条贯」是说要把这些条件都对上了才可以断为全凶。这里用的是卦名代表一类：文字里提到的卦（观、贲、丰）是举例，「余卦仿此」。注末原文还有一段「虎易评注」，是今人加的，本库不引。 卜筮大全的这一章后面还有「虎易评注」（今人加的），本库不引。
>
> **Note:** This section is about the 'tomb hexagrams': two lines of verse name ten hexagrams (Mingyi, Guan, Bi, Xu and Lin as 'the Self and Body entering a tomb', and Daxu, Feng, Tong, Gu and Guai as 'Wealth and the ghost both rising'); the commentary first gives the examples of Guan and Bi to say what 'the Body following the ghost into a tomb' and 'the Self following the ghost into a tomb' mean, and then quotes a verse of Cangping's on which relative in the family is in calamity when each hexagram of the eight palaces changes into a tomb (Qian into Gen, Kun into Xun, Zhen into Kun, Xun into Kun, Kan into Xun, Li into Qian, Gen into the wind, Dui into the mountain) — the 'wind' of 'Gen changing into the wind' is Xun and the 'mountain' of 'Dui changing into the mountain' is Gen. 'Answering the topic' is translated literally, and probably means that the hexagram answers to the person asked about. 'Tong' is Tongren from the context. 'Tiao guan' is orderliness or thread, and 'one must grasp its threads' says that all these conditions must fit before it may be judged wholly ominous. The hexagram names stand for a kind here: the hexagrams mentioned in the text (Guan, Bi, Feng) are examples, and 'the other hexagrams follow this'. At the end of the commentary the original has a further passage, 'Huyi's commentary', added by a modern hand, which this library does not quote. After this chapter the Buyi Daquan also has 'Huyi's commentary' (added by a modern hand), which this library does not quote.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 1618 行:明夷、观、贲、需、临;同文 第 1618 行:切忌世身入墓;同文 第 1618 行:大畜、丰、同、蛊、夬;同文 第 1618 行:莫逢财鬼俱兴
- 《易冒》:异文(相似 0.83) 第 1194 行:大畜丰同人蛊夬
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1858 行:明夷、观、贲、需、临;同文 第 1858 行:切忌世身入墓;同文 第 1858 行:大畜、丰、同、蛊、夬;同文 第 1858 行:莫逢财鬼俱兴
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1119 行:明夷、观、贲、需、临;同文 第 1119 行:切忌世身入墓;异文(相似 0.83) 第 1119 行:大畜、丰、同人、蛊、夬;同文 第 1119 行:莫逢财鬼俱兴

## LY.ILL.TXF.07 男怕长生兼怕未来之节、女嫌沐浴最嫌过去之辰：男子怕鬼爻长生日得病，卦无子孙青龙天医发动则鬼爻墓绝日死；寅日遇火鬼、巳日遇金鬼、申日水土鬼、亥日木鬼谓之长生鬼；先看鬼爻衰旺再论节气（男怕未来节、近节必重、过节无伤）；女人怕鬼爻沐浴日得病（卯日火鬼、午日金鬼、酉日水土鬼、子日木鬼），过节后必重

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:男人怕长生，还怕未来的节气；女人嫌沐浴，最嫌过去的日子。注说：男子怕在鬼爻长生的日子得病，和鬼一起衰旺；卦里没有子孙、青龙、天医发动的，鬼爻墓绝的日子就死。又说：寅日遇火鬼、巳日遇金鬼、申日遇水土鬼、亥日遇木鬼，叫长生鬼；占的人遇上，大凶在后面，沉重才会来。凡决断安危，先按鬼爻的衰旺推断，再按节气来论：男人怕未来的节，临近节令必定加重，过了节就没有伤害；节前不重的，一定没有妨碍；如果是久病，应当以四季节候来论，比如木鬼忌秋天的节气，遇节不重，过节一定安。女人最忌在鬼爻沐浴的日子得病。又说：卯日遇火鬼、午日遇金鬼、酉日遇水土鬼、子日遇木鬼，叫沐浴鬼；遇上难痊愈，到后来反而加重；再看节后必定加重，不加重就没有伤害；女人极怕过后的节。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A man fears the Birth stage and also fears the coming solar term; a woman dislikes the Bath stage and most dislikes the term that has passed. The commentary says: a man fears falling ill on a day when the Pressure line is at Birth, and it rises and falls with the ghost; if the hexagram has no Output, Azure Dragon or Heavenly Doctor moving, he dies on a day when the Pressure line is in a tomb or at Extinction. It says again: a Fire ghost met on a Yin day, a Metal ghost on a Si day, a Water or Earth ghost on a Shen day, a Wood ghost on a Hai day, is called a Birth ghost; one who meets it will have great ill afterwards, and the heaviness only comes later. To decide danger and safety, first judge by the strength of the Pressure line, and then by the solar term: a man fears the coming term, near the term it is surely heavier, and past the term there is no harm; if it is not heavy before the term, there is surely no obstruction; if it is a long illness, one should reason by the seasonal terms of the four seasons, for example a Wood ghost fears the autumn terms, and if on meeting the term it is not heavy, after the term it will surely be at peace. A woman most fears falling ill on a day when the Pressure line is at Bath. It says again: a Fire ghost met on a Mao day, a Metal ghost on a Wu day, a Water or Earth ghost on a You day, a Wood ghost on a Zi day, is called a Bath ghost; met, it is hard to cure, and it becomes heavier afterwards; look next at the term after, when it is surely heavier, and if it is not heavy there is no harm; a woman greatly fears the term that has passed.

**《天玄赋·天玄赋·十七、疾病章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 4989 行 · *Tianxuan Fu (Rhapsody of the Heavenly Mystery), "Tianxuan Fu, chapter 17: illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4989*

| 凡决安危， | 凡是决断安危， | To decide danger and safety, |

| 凡男怕未来节， | 男人怕未来的节， | A man fears the coming term; |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 4993 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4993*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又云： | 又说道： | It says again: |
| 卯日遇火鬼， | 卯日遇火鬼， | a Fire ghost met on a Mao day, |
| 午日遇金鬼， | 午日遇金鬼， | a Metal ghost on a Wu day, |
| 酉日遇水土鬼， | 酉日遇水土鬼， | a Water or Earth ghost on a You day, |
| 子日遇木鬼， | 子日遇木鬼， | a Wood ghost on a Zi day, |
| 谓之沐浴鬼。 | 叫做沐浴鬼； | is called a Bath ghost; |
| 遇之难痊， | 遇上难痊愈， | met, it is hard to cure, |
| 至后反加沉重。 | 到后来反而加重。 | and it becomes heavier afterwards. |
| 次看节后必重， | 再看节后必定加重， | look next at the term after, when it is surely heavier, |
| 不重无伤。 | 不加重就没有伤害； | and if it is not heavy there is no harm; |
| 女人极怕过后之节。 | 女人极怕过后的节。 | a woman greatly fears the term that has passed. |

> **按**:这一节讲的是「长生鬼」和「沐浴鬼」：鬼爻长生的日子、沐浴的日子得病，加上节气的前后，判轻重。「长生」是十二长生的第一位（生），「沐浴」是第二位；寅日遇火鬼是火长生在寅，巳日遇金鬼是金长生在巳，申日遇水土鬼是水土长生在申，亥日遇木鬼是木长生在亥——所以叫长生鬼；沐浴鬼：卯日火鬼、午日金鬼、酉日水土鬼、子日木鬼，正是沐浴的位置。「未来之节」是还没到的节气，「过去之辰」是已过的日子/节气，男怕节前、女怕节后：男人在近节时必重、过节无伤；女人在节后必重。「四时节候」是二十四节气。这一段和易隐（LY.ILL.YY.10「男忌官鬼长生日得病，女忌官鬼沐浴日得病」）、黄金策（LY.ILL.HJC.05 一带）是同一说，只是这里多了节气的讲法。男女判轻重的方向是相反的，这是这本书的规定，没有解释。 卜筮大全的这一章后面还有「虎易评注」（今人加的），本库不引。
>
> **Note:** This section is about the 'Birth ghost' and the 'Bath ghost': falling ill on a day when the Pressure line is at Birth or at Bath, together with what comes before or after the solar term, decides light or heavy. 'Birth' is the first place of the twelve stages of growth, and 'Bath' the second; a Fire ghost on a Yin day is Fire's Birth at Yin, a Metal ghost on a Si day is Metal's Birth at Si, a Water or Earth ghost on a Shen day is Water and Earth's Birth at Shen, and a Wood ghost on a Hai day is Wood's Birth at Hai — hence 'Birth ghost'; the Bath ghost: Fire on Mao, Metal on Wu, Water and Earth on You, Wood on Zi, which are exactly the Bath places. 'The coming term' is the solar term not yet arrived, and 'the term that has passed' is the day or term that has gone by: a man fears before the term and a woman after: for a man near the term it is surely heavy and past the term there is no harm; for a woman after the term it is surely heavy. The 'seasonal terms of the four seasons' are the twenty-four solar terms. This passage says the same as the Yi Yin (LY.ILL.YY.10, 'a man should fear falling ill on the day the Pressure line is born, a woman on the day it is at Bath') and the Huangjin Ce (LY.ILL.HJC.05 and around), only with the added account of the solar terms. That the man's and the woman's directions of judging light and heavy are opposite is this book's rule and is not explained. After this chapter the Buyi Daquan also has 'Huyi's commentary' (added by a modern hand), which this library does not quote.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 1621 行:兼怕未来之节;同文 第 1621 行:最嫌过去之辰
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1860 行:兼怕未来之节;同文 第 1860 行:最嫌过去之辰

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.06、LY.ILL.TXF.08、LY.ILL.YY.10、LY.ILL.HJC.05

## LY.ILL.TXF.08 不宜丘墓同宫、安可雷风合卦：三丘五墓二杀俱动病人作福也难留；雷风是棺椁杀——震为棺、巽为椁、坤为墓、艮为冢，占病若全必死

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:不适宜丘墓在同一宫，怎么能雷风合成一卦？注说：三丘、五墓都见后面。这两个煞一起发动，病人做福也留不住；雷风是棺椁杀，震是棺，巽是椁，坤是墓，艮是冢，占病如果都齐了，必死。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** It is not fitting for the Mounds and Tombs to be in the same palace; how can Thunder and Wind combine in one hexagram? The commentary says: the Three Mounds and Five Tombs are all given later. When the two killings move together, even if the sick person performs blessing rites he can hardly be kept; Thunder and Wind are the Coffin-and-Outer-Coffin killing, Zhen being the coffin, Xun the outer coffin, Kun the grave and Gen the tomb mound; if in divining an illness all are present, he surely dies.

**《天玄赋·天玄赋·十七、疾病章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 4995 行 · *Tianxuan Fu (Rhapsody of the Heavenly Mystery), "Tianxuan Fu, chapter 17: illness", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4995*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 不宜丘墓同宫， | 不适宜丘墓在同一宫， | It is not fitting for the Mounds and Tombs to be in the same palace; |
| 安可雷风合卦。 | 怎么能让雷风合成一卦？ | how can Thunder and Wind combine in one hexagram? |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 4996 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4996*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 三丘五墓， | 三丘、五墓， | The Three Mounds and Five Tombs: |
| 俱见后。 | 都见后面。 | all are given later. |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 4997 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 4997*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 二杀俱动， | 这两个煞一起发动， | When the two killings move together, |
| 病人作福也难留。 | 病人做福也难留。 | even if the sick person performs blessing rites he can hardly be kept. |
| 雷风者， | 雷和风这两卦， | Thunder and Wind |
| 棺椁杀也。 | 是棺椁杀。 | are the Coffin-and-Outer-Coffin killing. |
| 震为棺， | 震卦是棺， | Zhen is the coffin, |
| 巽为椁， | 巽卦是椁， | Xun is the outer coffin, |
| 坤为墓， | 坤卦是墓， | Kun is the grave, |
| 艮为冢。 | 艮卦是冢。 | Gen is the tomb mound. |
| 占病若全必死。 | 占病如果都齐了，必死。 | If in divining an illness all are present, he surely dies. |

> **按**:「二杀」是三丘和五墓，书里说「俱见后」——后面的凶煞章。三丘五墓的排法（春丑夏辰秋未冬戌为三丘，对宫取五墓）见易林补遗「总论星煞值鬼诀」（LY.ILL.EL.51），「病人作福也难留」一句同是那首歌诀里的原话。「雷风」指震（雷）、巽（风）两卦：震为棺、巽为椁，和易冒、增删卜易里「风雷合卦、棺椁俱全」（LY.ILL.ZS.15）是同一个说法。「占病若全必死」的「全」是丘、墓、棺、椁几样都齐了。 卜筮大全的这一章后面还有「虎易评注」（今人加的），本库不引。
>
> **Note:** The 'two killings' are the Three Mounds and the Five Tombs, of which the book says 'all given later' — in the later chapter on malign killings. How the Three Mounds and Five Tombs are set (Chou in spring, Chen in summer, Wei in autumn and Xu in winter for the Three Mounds, the Five Tombs taken from the opposite palace) is in the Yilin Buyi's 'general discourse on ghosts by the stars and killings' (LY.ILL.EL.51), and 'even if the sick person performs blessing rites he can hardly be kept' is the very line of that verse. 'Thunder and Wind' are the Zhen (thunder) and Xun (wind) trigrams: Zhen is the coffin and Xun the outer coffin, the same saying as 'thunder and wind combining, coffin and outer coffin complete' in the Zengshan Buyi (LY.ILL.ZS.15). The 'all' of 'if in divining illness all are present' is that the mound, the tomb, the coffin and the outer coffin are all there. After this chapter the Buyi Daquan also has 'Huyi's commentary' (added by a modern hand), which this library does not quote.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 1624 行:不宜丘墓同宫;同文 第 1624 行:安可雷风合卦
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1862 行:不宜丘墓同宫;同文 第 1862 行:安可雷风合卦

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.07、LY.ILL.TXF.09、LY.ILL.EL.51、LY.ILL.ZS.15、LY.ILL.ZSZ.01、LY.ILL.HZL.01、LY.ILL.DYTJ.31

## LY.ILL.WWJQ.03 六十四卦课·占病逢乾、姤、观、节、革、丰、师、大畜、履、渐：病人痊愈、占病无妨、占病即愈、疾病皆除、疾病渐好、疾病大好、官司疾病皆无妨

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:文王金钱课的六十四卦课（原有的二十六卦）里，这十个卦的断辞说病会好：乾「病人痊愈」，姤「占病无妨」，观「占病即愈、疾病皆除」，节「疾病口舌消除安」，革「官司疾病皆无妨」，丰「疾病渐好」，师「疾病大好」，大畜「疾病口舌渐渐磨」，履「疾病皆除」，渐「官司疾病俱无妨」。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In the sixty-four hexagram readings of the Wenwang Jinqian Ke (the twenty-six that the book originally has), the verdicts of these ten hexagrams say that the illness will mend: Qian, 'the sick are cured'; Gou, 'divining illness: no harm'; Guan, 'divining illness: cured at once, all illness removed'; Jie, 'illness and quarrels are cleared and peace comes'; Ge, 'lawsuit and illness are both without harm'; Feng, 'illness gradually mends'; Shi, 'illness mends greatly'; Daxu, 'illness and quarrels are gradually worn away'; Lü, 'all illness is removed'; Jian, 'lawsuit and illness are both without harm'.

| 百般凑巧。 | 样样都凑得巧。 | all things fall out luckily. |

| 官司疾病俱无妨。 | 官司、疾病都没有妨碍。 | lawsuit and illness are both without harm. |

> **打架**:文王金钱课和周易尚占（LY.ILL.ZSZ.02 至 ZSZ.06）都是按卦名断病，可是同一个卦两本说的，有四个相反或相左：姤，周易尚占说「病者难安」，这里说「占病无妨」；丰，周易尚占说「病人沉滞」，这里说「疾病渐好」；师，周易尚占说「病人迟瘥」，这里说「疾病大好」；履，周易尚占说「弗占疾病」，这里说「疾病皆除」。乾、观、革、渐、大畜这五个两本一致说病会好；节，周易尚占没有讲病。另一方面，观、大畜在别的书的占病凶卦名单里（LY.ILL.CA.03、DYTJ.15、TXF.06 等），观卦两本都说病好，正好和那些名单相反（见 LY.ILL.ZSZ.02）。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** The Wenwang Jinqian Ke and the Zhouyi Shangzhan (LY.ILL.ZSZ.02 to ZSZ.06) both judge illness by the name of the hexagram, but for the same hexagram the two say things that are opposed or at odds in four cases: Gou, the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the sick will find it hard to be at peace' while here it says 'divining illness: no harm'; Feng, the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the sick one is weighed down and lingering' while here 'illness gradually mends'; Shi, the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'the sick recover late' while here 'illness mends greatly'; Lü, the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'do not divine illness' while here 'all illness is removed'. Qian, Guan, Ge, Jian and Daxu are five in which the two agree that the illness will mend; for Jie the Zhouyi Shangzhan does not speak of illness. On the other side, Guan and Daxu are in other books' lists of ominous hexagrams for illness (LY.ILL.CA.03, DYTJ.15, TXF.06 and so on), and for Guan both books say the illness mends, exactly against those lists (see LY.ILL.ZSZ.02). The books do not decide.

> **按**:这部书原文只有二十六卦（乾宫、坎宫、艮宫各八卦，震宫两卦），每卦有「象曰」（七言四句）、「诗曰」（四言四句）、「断曰」（四言四句）三项，讲病的话有的在断曰里，有的在诗曰或象曰里；这里把含病话的那一两行收在一起，每一段的卦名写在引文标签上。每卦的题目是「卦名（课名）」，如「天山 遁（浓云蔽日）」。几处原文的写法：「天风垢」的「垢」是姤的误写，「天泽覆」的「覆」是履的误写，「风鸣歧山」按字面应是「凤鸣岐山」，原文如此，不改。「和课」（革、大畜、节、坎等处）应是「合伙」（补录部分同一位置写作「合伙」），译文按「partnership」译。「旱苗得雨芪堂堂」的「芪」意思不明，译文按上下文读作苗长得挺拔。「交易得魁」的「魁」是第一名。「疾病口舌渐渐磨」的「磨」是磨掉、慢慢消磨。这些卦的断辞每句都是四个字的断语，没有说用神、世应、动变。
>
> **Note:** In the original this book has only twenty-six hexagrams (eight each for the Qian, Kan and Gen palaces and two for the Zhen palace), each with three items, 'the image verse' (four lines of seven characters), 'the poem' (four phrases of four characters) and 'the verdict' (four phrases of four characters); the words on illness are in the verdict in some, and in the poem or the image verse in others, and here the one or two lines containing words on illness are taken together, with the name of the hexagram written on each quotation label. The heading of each hexagram is 'name of hexagram (name of reading)', as in 'Heaven over Mountain, Dun (Thick Clouds Hide the Sun)'. For some original spellings: the 'gou' of 'Heaven over Wind, Gou' is a miswriting of Gou, the 'fu' of 'Heaven over Marsh, Fu' is a miswriting of Lü, and 'the Wind Cries on Mount Qi' should literally be 'the Phoenix Cries on Mount Qi'; all are as in the original and are not changed. 'He ke' (in Ge, Daxu, Jie, Kan and elsewhere) should be 'partnership' (the supplemented part writes 'partnership' in the same place), and the translation reads 'partnership'. The 'qi' of 'parched seedlings get rain, qi stately' is not clear in sense, and the translation reads it from the context as the seedlings standing tall. The 'kui' of 'trade takes first place' is the first place. The 'worn away' of 'illness and quarrels are gradually worn away' is rubbed off, slowly ground down. In these hexagrams every sentence of the verdict is a four-character phrase, and nothing is said of the Subject, the Self and Other, or moving lines.

相关:LY.ILL.WWJQ.04、LY.ILL.WWJQ.05、LY.ILL.ZSZ.02、LY.ILL.ZSZ.03、LY.ILL.ZSZ.04、LY.ILL.CA.03、LY.ILL.DYTJ.15

---

## LY.ILL.WWJQ.05 【补录】六十四卦课·占病逢解、恒、升、随、巽、小畜、颐、离、坤、临、大壮、夬、兑、萃、咸：疾病大好、有病就好、疾病口舌皆除根、疾病痊愈、灾消病散、疾病即痊、疾病安宁

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:文王金钱课文末补录的网上传本里，这十六个卦的卦辞或推断说病会好：解「疾病大好」，恒「有病就好」，升「疾病口舌皆除根」，随「疾病口舌保平安」，巽「疾病痊愈」，小畜「疾病口舌，交节安宁」，颐「疾病口舌可离身」，离「灾消病散」，坤「疾病见好」，临「疾病即痊」，大壮「疾病皆除」，夬「疾病除根」，兑「疾病痊愈」，萃「口舌疾病永无灾」，咸「疾病安宁」。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In the internet copy that the library appended at the end of the Wenwang Jinqian Ke, the hexagram verse or judgment of these fifteen hexagrams says that the illness will mend: Xie, 'illness mends greatly'; Heng, 'if there is illness, it mends'; Sheng, 'illness and quarrels are all uprooted'; Sui, 'illness and quarrels are kept safe and peaceful'; Xun, 'illness is cured'; Xiaoxu, 'illness and quarrels: after the turn of the season, peace'; Yi (Nourishment), 'illness and quarrels can leave the body'; Li, 'calamity vanishes and illness disperses'; Kun, 'illness shows improvement'; Lin, 'illness is cured at once'; Dazhuang, 'all illness is removed'; Guai, 'illness is uprooted'; Dui, 'illness is cured'; Cui, 'quarrels and illness bring no calamity ever'; Xian, 'illness is at ease'.

> **打架**:补录的这些卦里，有几个和周易尚占（LY.ILL.ZSZ.03 至 ZSZ.07）说的相反或相左：巽，周易尚占说「金神为崇」，这里说「疾病痊愈」；离，周易尚占说「病重财轻」，这里说「灾消病散」；临，周易尚占说「病犯崇侵」，这里说「疾病即痊」；夬，周易尚占说「病讼迟延」，这里说「疾病除根」；大壮，周易尚占说「休占讼病」，这里说「疾病皆除」；恒，周易尚占说「病须疾治」（要赶快治），这里说「有病就好」。解、随、颐、兑、萃、咸这六个两本一致说病好；升、小畜、坤周易尚占没有讲病。（这几个卦在补录部分里，而补录部分的来历不明，见下面的说明。）书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Several of these supplemented hexagrams are opposed to or at odds with what the Zhouyi Shangzhan says (LY.ILL.ZSZ.03 to ZSZ.07): Xun, the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'a Metal spirit is the curse' while here it says 'illness is cured'; Li, the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'illness is heavy and wealth is light' while here 'calamity vanishes and illness disperses'; Lin, the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'illness meets the encroachment of a curse' while here 'illness is cured at once'; Guai, the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'illness and lawsuit are delayed' while here 'illness is uprooted'; Dazhuang, the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'do not divine lawsuits or illness' while here 'all illness is removed'; Heng, the Zhouyi Shangzhan says 'illness must be treated quickly' while here 'if there is illness, it mends'. Xie, Sui, Yi (Nourishment), Dui, Cui and Xian are six in which the two books agree that the illness mends; for Sheng, Xiaoxu and Kun the Zhouyi Shangzhan does not speak of illness. (These hexagrams are in the supplemented part, whose origin is unknown; see the note below.) The books do not decide.

> **按**:这个卦是库里「补录」的网上传本，不是这部书原文里有的（原文只有二十六卦）；库里的说明写：这份传本每卦只有「卦辞」（七言四句）和「推断」（四言四句）两项，没有课名、六亲、地支、世应，拿它和原有二十六卦对，十七卦两项都对得上，三卦卦辞对得上、推断有出入，另有三对卦的内容整个互换（风地观与山地剥、火泽睽与风泽中孚、天泽履与风山渐），所以补录部分个别卦也可能挂错了卦；卦名已按卦画订正；传本里没有风火家人。「疾病口舌」是疾病和口舌是非并说。「交节」是过了节气。「五关脱难」是过五关、脱了难。补录部分的卦名已按卦画订正，不是原帖的写法。
>
> **Note:** These hexagrams are from the 'supplemented' copy found on the internet that the library appended, and are not in the original text of this book (the original has only twenty-six hexagrams); the library's note says: this copy gives each hexagram only two items, the 'hexagram verse' (four lines of seven characters) and the 'judgment' (four phrases of four characters), with no name of the reading, no six relations, no branches, no Self and Other; compared with the twenty-six original hexagrams, seventeen agree in both items, three agree in the verse but differ in the judgment, and three pairs of hexagrams have their contents swapped wholesale (Guan and Bo, Kui and Zhongfu, Lü and Jian), so some of the supplemented hexagrams may also be hung on the wrong hexagram; the names of the hexagrams have been corrected from the hexagram figures; the copy lacks Jiaren. 'Illness and quarrels' names illness and quarrels together. 'The turn of the season' is the passing of a solar term. 'Passing the five gates, escaping hardship' is getting through the five passes and escaping hardship. The names of the hexagrams in the supplemented part have been corrected from the hexagram figures and are not the original post's spelling.

相关:LY.ILL.WWJQ.03、LY.ILL.WWJQ.06、LY.ILL.ZSZ.03、LY.ILL.ZSZ.05、LY.ILL.ZSZ.07

---

## LY.ILL.YM.10 卦属反伏墓绝亦不治；病未久三冲而愈、病已久三冲而亡，此三者不论用神衰旺；外此专重用神——动散、月破、克空、日破、受伤无援、脱气者危，日月动变有一能救则危而复安

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦是反吟、伏吟、入墓、入绝的，也治不好，即使能治好也会落下病根。病没多久的，遇到三冲就好；病已经久了的，遇到三冲就死；这三种情形不论用神的衰旺。除此之外，就专门看重用神：遇到动散、月破、克空、日破、受伤没有救援、脱气的，危险；遇到日、月、动爻、变爻里有一个能救的，危险了又能转安。注里给了每一种的例子（七种情形都是大凶，得到日、月、动爻、变爻里有一个能救的，就要反过来看）。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A hexagram that is Reversed, Hidden, in a tomb or in Extinction is likewise untreatable, and even if it can be treated, it becomes a lingering ailment. If the illness is not old, the three clashes cure it; if it is already long, the three clashes bring death; in these three cases the strength or weakness of the Subject is not considered. Apart from this, the Subject is what matters above all: if it meets moving-and-scattering, Month-break, control-into-Void, Day-break, being wounded without help, or drained of qi, it is in danger; if any one of the Day, Month, moving or changed lines can rescue it, danger turns again to safety. The note gives an example of each (the seven cases are all great ill omens, but if any one of the Day, Month, moving or changed lines can rescue, then look at it the other way round).

**《易冒·疾病章第五十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1191 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 55: Illness", base text; Yi Mao, line 1191*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦属反伏墓绝，亦不治也，即能治亦成疾矣。 | 卦是反吟、伏吟、入墓、入绝的，也是治不好的，即使能治好，也会落下病根（成了老病）。 | A hexagram that is Reversed, Hidden, in a tomb or in Extinction is likewise untreatable, and even if it can be treated, it becomes a lingering ailment. |
| 病未久者，三冲而愈，病已久者，三冲而亡，此三者不论用神衰旺也。 | 病没多久的，遇到三冲就好；病已经久了的，遇到三冲就死；这三种情形不论用神的衰旺。 | If the illness is not old, the three clashes cure it; if it is already long, the three clashes bring death; in these three cases the strength or weakness of the Subject is not considered. |
| 外此，则专重用神矣，如遇动散、月破、克空、日破、受伤无援、脱气者危，遇日月动变有一能救者，危而复安。 | 除此之外，就专门看重用神了：如果遇到动散、月破、克空、日破、受伤没有救援、脱气的，危险；遇到日、月、动爻、变爻里有一个能救的，危险了又能转安。 | Apart from this, the Subject is what matters above all: if it meets moving-and-scattering, Month-break, control-into-Void, Day-break, being wounded without help, or drained of qi, it is in danger; if any one of the Day, Month, moving lines or changed lines can rescue it, danger turns again to safety. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1192 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1192*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 动散，如卯建丙寅日，占子病得渐之观是也； | 动散，比如卯月丙寅日，占儿子的病，得到渐卦变观卦，就是； | 'Moving and scattering' is, for example, in the Mao month on the Bingyin day, a son's illness divined and Jian (Gradual) changing into Guan obtained; |
| 月破，如癸卯岁卯建壬子日，占予妻病得离，遂亡本月是也； | 月破，比如癸卯年卯月壬子日，占我妻子的病，得到离卦，当月就死了，就是； | 'Month-break' is, for example, in the Guimao year, the Mao month, on the Renzi day, my wife's illness divined and Li obtained, and she died within that month; |
| 克空，如酉建丁未日，占父病得未济是也； | 克空，比如酉月丁未日，占父亲的病，得到未济卦，就是； | 'Control-into-Void' is, for example, in the You month on the Dingwei day, a father's illness divined and Weiji obtained; |
| 日破，如戌建辛酉日，占夫病得渐是也； | 日破，比如戌月辛酉日，占丈夫的病，得到渐卦，就是； | 'Day-break' is, for example, in the Xu month on the Xinyou day, a husband's illness divined and Jian (Gradual) obtained; |
| 受伤无援，如子建戊子日，占弟兄得鼎之旅，一占弟兄得离，皆为受伤无援； | 受伤无援，比如子月戊子日，占兄弟得鼎卦变旅卦，又一次占兄弟得离卦，都是受伤没有救援； | 'Wounded without help' is, for example, in the Zi month on the Wuzi day, a brother's illness divined and Ding changing into Lü obtained, and another divination of a brother's obtained Li, both wounded without help; |
| 脱气，如戌建戊辰日，占己病得萃之讼，乃为脱气。 | 脱气，比如戌月戊辰日，占自己的病，得到萃卦变讼卦，就是脱气。 | 'Drained of qi' is, for example, in the Xu month on the Wuchen day, one's own illness divined and Cui changing into Song obtained, which is drained of qi. |
| 以上七法系大凶，得日月动变有一能救者，则反观之。 | 以上七种情形都是大凶，但得到日、月、动爻、变爻里有一个能救的，就要反过来看。 | The above seven are all great ill omens, but if any one of the Day, Month, moving lines or changed lines can rescue, then look at it the other way round. |

> **按**:原文说「以上七法」，但列出的只有动散、月破、克空、日破、受伤无援、脱气六项名目；把「受伤」「无援」算两件，就是七种，书里没有说明。「动散」是动爻发动而被冲散；「克空」是被克又落空。三冲与久病新病的分法，易隐讲近病逢六冲则愈、久病逢冲则死的地方见 LY.ILL.YY.07，黄金策与增删卜易见 LY.ILL.ZS.02、LY.ILL.ZS.03。
>
> **Note:** The original says 'the above seven methods' but lists only six names — moving-and-scattering, Month-break, control-into-Void, Day-break, wounded-without-help and drained-of-qi; counting 'wounded' and 'without help' as two makes seven, which the book does not explain. 'Moving and scattering' is a moving line that is clashed apart; 'control into Void' is a line controlled and also Void. For the division of recent and long illness under the three clashes, where the Yi Yin says a recent illness is cured by a Six-Clash and a long illness dies, see LY.ILL.YY.07, and for the Huangjin Ce and Zengshan Buyi see LY.ILL.ZS.02 and LY.ILL.ZS.03.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.09、LY.ILL.YM.11、LY.ILL.YY.07、LY.ILL.ZS.02、LY.ILL.ZS.03、LY.ILL.HJC.37
