## LY.ILL.YM.11 考之占验：四灭没、十卦、无鬼无财、身空命空、凶煞皆不死，死者必用神死则死、用神生则生（神煞尤轻）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:从占验来看，四灭、四没的卦不死，十卦不死，没有鬼、没有财的不死，身空、命空的不死，遇到凶煞的不死；那么人之所以死，一定是用神死了他就死，用神生旺他就活。灭没：春天需、蒙，夏天观、蛊，秋天剥、节，冬天旅、临。十卦：明夷、观、贲、大畜、丰、同人、蛊、夬、需、临。凶煞：三坵五墓、丧车这一类。比如占父亲的病，六月得观卦，凭什么会死？用爻当日、月，卦里没有财、鬼，有什么凶？因为用爻坏而没有鬼，即使祈祷也得不到保佑；用爻旺而没有鬼，不祈祷也安康；没有财而用爻绝，天禄已尽；没有财而用爻旺，只是吃得少。甲子旬空亥，难道亥年生的人就不能问病？寅月破申，难道申年生的人就不能占病？所以应爻（空）不必作戒；身空的，有世爻、卦身可以凭据；本命不出现的，有变卦、伏爻可以取用。大体上专心看用爻来定生死安危，才真正得到《易》的宗旨，神煞就更轻了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Examining what divinations have proved: with the Four Extinguishing and Submerging hexagrams he does not die; with the ten hexagrams he does not die; with no ghost and no Wealth he does not die; with the Body Void or the destiny Void he does not die; with malign stars he does not die; so what he dies of must be that he dies when the Subject dies and lives when the Subject lives. The Extinguishing and Submerging: in spring Xu and Meng, in summer Guan and Gu, in autumn Bo and Jie, in winter Lü and Lin. The ten hexagrams: Mingyi, Guan, Bi (Adornment), Daxu, Feng, Tongren, Gu, Guai, Xu and Lin. Malign stars are such as Three Mounds and Five Tombs and the Funeral Carriage. For example, in divining a father's illness, if in the sixth month one obtains Guan, from what would he die? The Subject line sits on the Day and Month and the hexagram has neither Wealth nor ghost: what ill omen is there? For if the Subject line is spoiled and there is no ghost, prayer brings no protection; if strong and no ghost, he is well without praying; with no Wealth and the Subject in Extinction the heavenly emolument has ended; with no Wealth and the Subject strong he merely takes little food. If the Jiazi decade leaves Hai Void, may a person of the Hai natal branch not ask about illness? If the Yin month is a Month-break against Shen, may a person of the Shen natal branch not divine illness? So the Other line need not be a warning; if the Body is Void, the Self and Body can be relied on; if the natal branch does not appear, the changed hexagram and hidden lines can be taken. In general, to concentrate on the Subject line to fix life and death, safety and danger, truly grasps the intent of the Yi; the spirit-stars weigh lighter still.

**《易冒·疾病章第五十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1193 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 55: Illness", base text; Yi Mao, line 1193*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 考之占验，四灭没不死，十卦不死，无鬼无财不死，身空命空不死，凶煞不死，则其所以死者，必用神死则死，用神生则生尔。 | 从占验来看，四灭、四没的卦不死，十卦不死，没有鬼、没有财的不死，身空、命空的不死，遇到凶煞的不死；那么人之所以死，一定是用神死了他就死，用神生旺他就活。 | Examining what divinations have proved: with the Four Extinguishing and Submerging hexagrams he does not die; with the ten hexagrams he does not die; with no ghost and no Wealth he does not die; with the Body Void or the destiny Void he does not die; with malign stars he does not die; so what he dies of must be that he dies when the Subject dies and lives when the Subject lives. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1194 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1194*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 灭没，春需蒙，夏观蛊，秋剥节，冬旅临是也。 | 灭没：春天需、蒙，夏天观、蛊，秋天剥、节，冬天旅、临。 | The Extinguishing and Submerging: in spring Xu and Meng, in summer Guan and Gu, in autumn Bo and Jie, in winter Lü and Lin. |
| 十卦，明夷观贲大畜丰同人蛊夬需临是也。 | 十卦：明夷、观、贲、大畜、丰、同人、蛊、夬、需、临。 | The ten hexagrams: Mingyi, Guan, Bi (Adornment), Daxu, Feng, Tongren, Gu, Guai, Xu and Lin. |
| 凶煞，三坵五墓丧车之类。 | 凶煞：三坵五墓、丧车这一类。 | Malign stars are such as Three Mounds and Five Tombs and the Funeral Carriage. |
| 如占父病，六月得观，何从而死？ | 比如占父亲的病，六月得观卦，凭什么会死？ | For example, in divining a father's illness, if in the sixth month one obtains Guan, from what would he die? |
| 用居日月，卦无财鬼，何凶？ | 用爻当日、月，卦里没有财、鬼，有什么凶？ | The Subject line sits on the Day and Month and the hexagram has neither Wealth nor ghost: what ill omen is there? |
| 盖用坏无鬼，虽祷不祐，用旺无鬼，不祈亦康，无财用绝，天禄已终，无财用旺，饮食少进尔。 | 因为用爻坏而没有鬼，即使祈祷也得不到保佑；用爻旺而没有鬼，不祈祷也安康；没有财而用爻绝，天禄已尽；没有财而用爻旺，只是吃得少。 | For if the Subject line is spoiled and there is no ghost, prayer brings no protection; if the Subject line is strong and there is no ghost, he is well without praying; with no Wealth and the Subject in Extinction, the heavenly emolument has ended; with no Wealth and the Subject strong, he merely takes little food. |
| 甲子旬空亥，则亥命不可问病，寅月破申，则申命不可占疾乎。 | 甲子旬空亥，难道亥年生的人就不能问病？寅月破申，难道申年生的人就不能占病？ | If the Jiazi decade leaves Hai Void, may a person of the Hai natal branch not ask about illness? If the Yin month is a Month-break against Shen, may a person of the Shen natal branch not divine illness? |
| 故应不为戒，身空，有世爻卦身可凭，本命不现，有变卦伏爻可取。 | 所以应爻（空）不必作戒；身空的，有世爻、卦身可以凭据；本命不出现的，有变卦、伏爻可以取用。 | So the Other line need not be a warning; if the Body is Void, the Self line and the Body can be relied on; if the natal branch does not appear, the changed hexagram and hidden lines can be taken. |
| 大抵专务用爻，以定生死安危，诚得易旨也。 | 大体上专心看用爻来定生死安危，才真正得到《易》的宗旨。 | In general, to concentrate on the Subject line to fix life and death, safety and danger, truly grasps the intent of the Yi. |
| 神煞尤轻。 | 神煞就更轻了。 | The spirit-stars weigh lighter still. |

> **打架**:凶卦、凶煞碰上了，死不死：易冒说四灭没、十卦、凶煞（三坵五墓丧车）都不死，死不死只看用神生死，神煞尤轻；易隐则把黄泉杀、四灭卦、四没卦、棺椁杀列为占病逢之必死的卦，又把三坵五墓、丧门吊客、死符丧车等列为动而临用必死的凶煞（LY.ILL.YY.05、LY.ILL.YY.08）。同一批卦名和凶煞，两本书的结论相反；增删卜易的野鹤同样说卦名、卦身、蛇虎星煞都不验、只看用神（LY.ILL.ZS.01），与易冒一边。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether meeting the ill-omened hexagrams and stars means death: the Yi Mao says that the Four Extinguishing and Submerging hexagrams, the ten hexagrams and the malign stars (Three Mounds and Five Tombs, the Funeral Carriage) do not mean death, that death rests only on whether the Subject lives or dies, and that the spirit-stars weigh lighter still; the Yi Yin, on the other hand, lists the Yellow Springs, the Four Extinguishing, the Four Submerging and the Coffin killers as hexagrams that mean death for one who divines illness, and lists Three Mounds and Five Tombs, Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence, Death Tally and Funeral Carriage and the like as malign stars that mean death when they move and sit on or control the Subject (LY.ILL.YY.05 and LY.ILL.YY.08). The same hexagram names and stars, and the two books' conclusions are opposite; the Old Man of Yehe in the Zengshan Buyi likewise says hexagram names, the Body and the Snake, Tiger and other stars are not reliable and only the Subject counts (LY.ILL.ZS.01), and stands with the Yi Mao.

> **按**:这一条的口气是「我们试过、检验过」：开头是「考之占验」，是作者拿实际占验的结果来反驳一批流行的凶象。「灭没」的名单和易隐的四灭卦、四没卦（LY.ILL.YY.08）是同一批，易冒把它们合称「灭没」，两本书对季节的配法一致。「天禄」在这里是财。「甲子旬空亥」是甲子旬里空的是戌亥，亥命被旬空。
>
> **Note:** The tone here is 'we have tried and tested it': it opens 'examining what divinations have proved', with the author using actual results to refute a set of popular ill omens. The list of the 'Extinguishing and Submerging' is the same as the Yi Yin's Four Extinguishing and Four Submerging hexagrams (LY.ILL.YY.08), which the Yi Mao names together, and the two books match the seasons the same way. 'Heavenly emolument' here is Wealth. In 'the Jiazi decade leaves Hai Void' the Void of the Jiazi decade is Xu and Hai, so a Hai natal branch is left Void.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.10、LY.ILL.YM.12、LY.ILL.YY.05、LY.ILL.YY.08、LY.ILL.ZS.01、LY.ILL.HJC.37

---

## LY.ILL.YM.13 游魂归魂保于旅而不保于家；震巽坤艮危于老而不危于少（震棺巽椁艮塚坤墓）；女终男穷（归妹未济）非壮年之问

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:游魂、归魂的卦，在外做客时保得住，在家里却保不住；震、巽、坤、艮的卦，对老人危险，对年轻人不危险；女终、男穷（归妹、未济）不适合壮年人来问。注：游魂是去、归魂是回来，如果病在旅途中，路上就没有忌讳；震象棺，巽象椁，艮象坟冢，坤象坟墓，对上了年纪的人是大忌，有所准备反而更凶；未济、归妹，壮年人不适合；但也要兼用神参看。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In Wandering Soul and Returning Soul hexagrams he is safe abroad but not at home; in Zhen, Xun, Kun and Gen he is in danger if old but not if young; 'the woman's end' and 'the man's exhaustion' (Guimei and Weiji) are not fitting for a divination by a man in his prime. The note: Wandering is going out and Returning is coming back, and if the illness falls on a journey the road holds no taboo; Zhen is the coffin, Xun the outer coffin, Gen the mound and Kun the grave, a great taboo for the old, and to be prepared is worse; Weiji and Guimei are not fitting for the prime of life; but one must also consult the Subject.

**《易冒·疾病章第五十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1199 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 55: Illness", base text; Yi Mao, line 1199*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 游魂归魂，保于旅而不保于家，震巽坤艮，危于老而不危于少，女终男穷，非宜壮年之问也。 | 游魂、归魂的卦，在外做客时保得住，在家里却保不住；震、巽、坤、艮的卦，对老人危险，对年轻人不危险；女终、男穷（归妹、未济），不适合壮年人来问。 | In Wandering Soul and Returning Soul hexagrams he is safe abroad but not at home; in Zhen, Xun, Kun and Gen he is in danger if old but not if young; 'the woman's end' and 'the man's exhaustion' (Guimei and Weiji) are not fitting for a divination by a man in his prime. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1200 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1200*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 游往归来，病于客旅，道途不忌； | 游魂是去、归魂是回来，如果病在旅途中，路上就没有忌讳； | Wandering is going out and Returning is coming back, and if the illness falls on a journey, the road holds no taboo; |
| 震为棺，巽为椁，艮为塚，坤为墓，衰年大忌，有备更凶； | 震象棺，巽象椁，艮象坟冢，坤象坟墓，对上了年纪的人是大忌，有所准备反而更凶； | Zhen is the coffin, Xun the outer coffin, Gen the mound and Kun the grave; for the old it is a great taboo, and to be prepared is worse; |
| 未济归妹，壮岁非宜。 | 未济、归妹，壮年人不适合。 | Weiji and Guimei are not fitting for the prime of life. |
| 然亦兼用神而参。 | 但也要兼用神来参看。 | But one must also consult the Subject. |

> **按**:「女终男穷」按《序卦传》，归妹是女之终，未济是男之穷；书里没有再解释，注里用卦名点了出来。「有备更凶」是说预先准备后事反而更凶，书里没有解释。这一条和易隐「黄泉杀、棺椁杀」一类按卦名断死（LY.ILL.YY.08）相近，但易冒这里要「兼用神而参」，不单凭卦名。
>
> **Note:** 'The woman's end and the man's exhaustion' are, after the Xu Gua Zhuan, Guimei as the woman's end and Weiji as the man's exhaustion; the book does not explain further and the note names them by hexagram. 'To be prepared is worse' means that to prepare for the funeral in advance is worse, which the book does not explain. This entry is close to the Yi Yin's judging death by hexagram names, the 'Yellow Springs' and 'Coffin' killers (LY.ILL.YY.08), but here the Yi Mao says 'also consult the Subject' and does not rely on the hexagram name alone.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.12、LY.ILL.YM.14、LY.ILL.YY.08

## LY.ILL.YM.14 二生、两病、生之道、死之途：忌神摇而元神动是二生；元神动而仇神摇是两病；忌仇动而元神暗摇是生之道；无忌动而用神明坏是死之途

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:忌神发动了，而原神也动，叫做二生；原神动了，而仇神也动，叫做两病；忌神、仇神动了，原神暗中动，是生路；没有忌神动，而用神明明白白地坏了，是死路。注举例：未月丙申日，占母亲的病，得同人变噬嗑，申是忌神本来克卯，亥是原神，发动泄掉忌神的气而生卯木用爻，这是二生；卯月乙卯日，占妻子的病，得同人变咸，子孙发动生财，本来是原神帮我，但卯木仇神来克戌爻，又多一层病；未月壬寅日，占丈夫的病，得同人变既济，兄、子都动，伤官本应病重，但寅申冲财，暗发生亥，后来病好起来；卯月丙寅日，占儿子本人，得坤变损，卦里有父、兄发动，木来救子孙，但酉金明明受日绝、月破，所以救不了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Adversary moves and the Support also moves: this is called the double life; the Support moves and the enemy spirit also moves: the double illness; the Adversary and enemy spirit move and the Support stirs in secret: a road to life; the Adversary does not move but the Subject is plainly spoiled: a road to death. The note's examples: in the Wei month on the Bingshen day, a mother's illness was divined and Tongren changing into Shihe obtained: Shen is the Adversary and by nature controls Mao, while Hai is the Support and moves to drain the Adversary's qi and generate the Mao Wood Subject line — the double life; in the Mao month on the Yimao day, a wife's illness was divined and Tongren changing into Xian obtained: Output moves to generate Wealth, which should have been the Support helping me, but Mao Wood, the enemy spirit, controls the Xu line, adding one more illness; in the Wei month on the Renyin day, a husband's illness was divined and Tongren changing into Jiji obtained: Peer and Output both move and harm Pressure, so it should be grave, but Yin clashes Shen Wealth and secretly stirs it to generate Hai, and later he recovered; in the Mao month on the Bingyin day, a son himself was divined and Kun changing into Sun obtained: Resource and Peer move, and Wood comes to rescue Output, but You Metal is plainly in Extinction by the Day and in Month-break, so it could not be saved.

**《易冒·疾病章第五十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1201 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 55: Illness", base text; Yi Mao, line 1201*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 忌神摇矣，元神动焉，谓之二生也； | 忌神发动了，而原神也动，叫做二生； | The Adversary moves and the Support also moves: this is called the double life; |
| 元神动矣，仇神摇焉，谓之两病也； | 原神动了，而仇神也动，叫做两病； | the Support moves and the enemy spirit also moves: this is called the double illness; |
| 忌仇动矣，元神暗摇，生之道也； | 忌神、仇神动了，原神暗中动，是生路； | the Adversary and enemy spirit move and the Support stirs in secret: this is a road to life; |
| 无忌动矣，用神明坏，死之途也。 | 没有忌神动，而用神明明白白地坏了，是死路。 | the Adversary does not move but the Subject is plainly spoiled: this is a road to death. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1202 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1202*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如未建丙申日，占母病得同人之噬嗑，申乃忌神，本来克卯，亥乃元神，动泄忌神之气，而生卯木用爻，是为二生； | 比如未月丙申日，占母亲的病，得到同人变噬嗑，申是忌神本来克卯，亥是原神，发动泄掉忌神的气，而生卯木用爻，这就是二生； | For example, in the Wei month on the Bingshen day, a mother's illness was divined and Tongren changing into Shihe obtained: Shen is the Adversary and by nature controls Mao, while Hai is the Support and moves to drain the Adversary's qi and generate the Mao Wood Subject line — this is the double life; |
| 卯建乙卯日，占妻病得同人之咸，子动生财，本谓元神助我，卯木仇神相克戌爻，更多一病也； | 卯月乙卯日，占妻子的病，得到同人变咸，子孙发动生财，本来是原神帮我，但卯木仇神来克戌爻，又多了一层病； | in the Mao month on the Yimao day, a wife's illness was divined and Tongren changing into Xian obtained: Output moves to generate Wealth, which should have been the Support helping me, but Mao Wood, the enemy spirit, controls the Xu line, adding one more illness; |
| 如未建壬寅日，占夫病得同人之既济，兄子皆动，伤官应笃，寅申冲财，暗发生亥，后病得起； | 未月壬寅日，占丈夫的病，得到同人变既济，兄、子都动，伤到官爻，病本应重，但寅申冲财，暗中发动生亥，后来病好起来了； | in the Wei month on the Renyin day, a husband's illness was divined and Tongren changing into Jiji obtained: Peer and Output both move and harm Pressure, so it should be grave, but Yin clashes Shen Wealth and secretly stirs it to generate Hai, and later he recovered; |
| 如卯建丙寅日，占子身得坤之损，卦有父兄发动，木来救子，然酉金明受日绝月破，所以不能救也。 | 卯月丙寅日，占儿子本人，得到坤变损，卦里有父、兄发动，木来救子孙，但酉金明明受日绝、月破，所以救不了。 | in the Mao month on the Bingyin day, a son himself was divined and Kun changing into Sun obtained: Resource and Peer move, and Wood comes to rescue Output, but You Metal is plainly in Extinction by the Day and in Month-break, so it could not be saved. |
| 余仿此。 | 其余照此。 | The rest follow this. |

> **按**:「二生」是有两条生路（忌神被原神泄去、又生用神），「两病」是原神之外又有仇神一起动、多一层病；「仇神」是生忌神的爻。这一组和卜筮正宗讲原神忌神仇神的《原忌仇神论》（第 967 行起）是同一套用法。第三例里「伤官应笃」的「官」是丈夫的用神（占夫以官为用）。
>
> **Note:** The 'double life' is two roads to life (the Adversary drained by the Support, which also generates the Subject), and the 'double illness' is the enemy spirit moving together with the Support so that there is one more illness; the 'enemy spirit' (chou shen) is the line that generates the Adversary. This group is the same usage as the Bushi Zhengzong's essay on the Support, Adversary and enemy spirit (from line 967). In the third example the 'Pressure' of 'harming Pressure it should be grave' is the husband's Subject (Pressure is the Subject in divining for a husband).

相关:LY.ILL.YM.13、LY.ILL.YM.15、LY.ILL.BSZZ.01、LY.ILL.HJC.33

## LY.ILL.YM.15 非危之象：飞爻用神绝而伏爻用神生、内卦用神亡而外卦用神存、卦爻无用神而日月有用神，皆非危

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:飞爻上的用神绝了，但伏爻上的用神生旺，不危险；内卦的用神没了，但外卦的用神还在，不危险；卦爻里没有用神，但日、月里有用神，不危险。注举例：寅月丙寅日，占兄弟，得乾变需，申飞在用爻上，日破月破，却不知道酉伏在戌下，火生土而土生金，所以说不危险；辰月辛卯日，占儿子本人，得未济，外卦的未虽旬空，内卦的辰当月建，后来病好了；午月戊寅日，占妻子本人，得既济，卦里虽然没有用爻，但月是午、日是寅，原神和用神都出现，当月病就好了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Subject on the flying line is in Extinction but the Subject on the hidden line is born, there is no danger; if the Subject in the inner trigram is lost but the Subject in the outer trigram remains, there is no danger; if there is no Subject among the lines of the hexagram but the Day or Month holds the Subject, there is no danger. The note's examples: in the Yin month on the Bingyin day, a brother was divined and Qian changing into Xu obtained: Shen stood on the Subject line as the flying line, in Day-break and Month-break, but it was not noticed that You lay hidden beneath Xu, and Fire generates Earth and Earth generates Metal, so it is said there is no danger; in the Chen month on the Xinmao day, a son himself was divined and Weiji obtained: Wei in the outer trigram was in Void, but Chen in the inner trigram stood on the Month, and later he recovered; in the Wu month on the Wuyin day, a wife herself was divined and Jiji obtained: though there was no Subject line in the hexagram, the month was Wu and the day Yin, and the Support and the Subject both appeared, and that month she recovered.

**《易冒·疾病章第五十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1203 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 55: Illness", base text; Yi Mao, line 1203*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 飞爻之用神绝，伏爻之用神生，非危也； | 飞爻上的用神绝了，伏爻上的用神生旺，不危险； | If the Subject on the flying line is in Extinction but the Subject on the hidden line is born, there is no danger; |
| 内卦之用神亡，外卦之用神存，非危也； | 内卦的用神没了，外卦的用神还在，不危险； | if the Subject in the inner trigram is lost but the Subject in the outer trigram remains, there is no danger; |
| 卦爻之用神无，日月之用神有，非危也。 | 卦爻里没有用神，日、月里有用神，不危险。 | if there is no Subject among the lines of the hexagram but the Day or Month holds the Subject, there is no danger. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1204 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1204*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如寅建丙寅日，占弟兄得乾之需，申飞用爻，日破月破，而不知酉伏戌下，火生土而土生金，故曰非危也； | 比如寅月丙寅日，占兄弟，得乾变需，申飞在用爻上，日破月破，却不知道酉伏在戌下，火生土而土生金，所以说不危险； | For example, in the Yin month on the Bingyin day, a brother was divined and Qian changing into Xu obtained: Shen stood on the Subject line as the flying line, in Day-break and Month-break, but it was not noticed that You lay hidden beneath Xu, and Fire generates Earth and Earth generates Metal, so it is said there is no danger; |
| 如辰建辛卯日，占子身得未济，外未虽旬空，内辰当月建，后愈； | 如辰月辛卯日，占儿子本人，得未济，外卦的未虽旬空，内卦的辰当月建，后来病好了； | in the Chen month on the Xinmao day, a son himself was divined and Weiji obtained: Wei in the outer trigram was in Void, but Chen in the inner trigram stood on the Month, and later he recovered; |
| 如午建戊寅日，占妻身得既济，卦内虽无用爻，月午日寅，元用并现，当月病愈。 | 如午月戊寅日，占妻子本人，得既济，卦里虽然没有用爻，但月是午、日是寅，原神和用神都出现，当月病就好了。 | in the Wu month on the Wuyin day, a wife herself was divined and Jiji obtained: though there was no Subject line in the hexagram, the month was Wu and the day Yin, and the Support and the Subject both appeared, and that month she recovered. |

> **按**:「飞爻」是卦里现出来的那一爻，「伏爻」是伏在它下面的爻。第一个例子里申金飞在用爻上而日破月破，看起来危险，但酉金伏在戌土下面，火生土、土生金，伏着的用神得生，所以不危。「外未」「内辰」是外卦的未、内卦的辰。
>
> **Note:** The 'flying line' is the line that appears in the hexagram and the 'hidden line' the one hidden beneath it. In the first example Shen Metal stands on the Subject line as the flying line and is in Day-break and Month-break, which looks dangerous, but You Metal is hidden beneath Xu Earth, Fire generates Earth and Earth generates Metal, so the hidden Subject is born and there is no danger. 'The Wei of the outer' and 'the Chen of the inner' are the Wei in the outer trigram and the Chen in the inner.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.14、LY.ILL.YM.16、LY.ILL.HJC.47

## LY.ILL.YM.16 用神有一生则生、有一死则死：日月动爻克用而用得化生扶则非危；日月动爻不生不克而用神化克则危；变爻亦宜无病乃真有救

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:日、月、动爻克用爻，这叫做不得救，但用爻变出生它、扶它的，不危险；日、月、动爻对用神不生也不克，而用神变出克它的，就危险了。所以说：用神有一个生它的就活，有一个死的就死。注：这是说用神变生、变克是最要紧的办法，但变出的爻也应当没有毛病，才是真有救。比如未月甲辰日，占儿子本人，得咸变蹇，日、月克用爻，大凶，但用爻变出兄弟长生，所以不死；戌月戊申日，占父亲本人，得艮变蛊，用爻独自发动本该活，因为变出去在亥绝，就救不了了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Day, Month and moving lines control the Subject line, this is called no rescue, but if the Subject line changes into what generates or supports it, there is no danger; if the Day, Month and moving lines neither generate nor control the Subject, but the Subject changes into what controls it, it is in danger. So it is said: if the Subject has one source of life it lives, and if it has one death it dies. The note: this says that the Subject changing into generation or control is the most important method, but the changed line must also be without flaw for the rescue to be real: for example, in the Wei month on the Jiachen day, a son himself was divined and Xian changing into Jian (Obstruction) obtained; the Day and Month control the Subject line, a great ill omen, but the Subject changed into Peer at Birth, so he did not die; in the Xu month on the Wushen day, a father himself was divined and Gen changing into Gu obtained: the Subject line moved alone and should have lived, but it changed into Extinction at Hai and so could not be saved.

**《易冒·疾病章第五十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1205 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 55: Illness", base text; Yi Mao, line 1205*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 犹有日月动爻克用，是谓不救，而用爻得化生扶，非危也。 | 还有日、月、动爻克用爻，这叫做不得救，但用爻变出生它、扶它的，不危险。 | Again, if the Day, Month and moving lines control the Subject line, this is called no rescue, but if the Subject line changes into what generates or supports it, there is no danger. |
| 日月动爻，与用神不生不克，而用神化克则危矣。 | 日、月、动爻对用神不生也不克，而用神变出克它的，就危险了。 | If the Day, Month and moving lines neither generate nor control the Subject, but the Subject changes into what controls it, it is in danger. |
| 故曰用神有一生则生，有一死则死。 | 所以说：用神有一个生它的就活，有一个死的就死。 | So it is said: if the Subject has one source of life it lives, and if it has one death it dies. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1206 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1206*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此言用神化生化克为最要法，然变爻亦宜无病，乃真有救，如未建甲辰日，占子身得咸之蹇，日月克用大凶，而用化兄弟长生，是乃不死； | 这是说用神变生、变克是最要紧的办法，但变出的爻也应当没有毛病，才是真有救；比如未月甲辰日，占儿子本人，得咸变蹇，日、月克用爻，大凶，但用爻变出兄弟长生，所以不死； | This says that the Subject changing into generation or control is the most important method, but the changed line must also be without flaw for the rescue to be real: for example, in the Wei month on the Jiachen day, a son himself was divined and Xian changing into Jian (Obstruction) obtained; the Day and Month control the Subject line, a great ill omen, but the Subject changed into Peer at Birth, so he did not die; |
| 戌建戊申日，占父身得艮之蛊，用爻独发当生，以化绝于亥遂不救。 | 戌月戊申日，占父亲本人，得艮变蛊，用爻独自发动本该活，因为变出去在亥绝，就救不了了。 | in the Xu month on the Wushen day, a father himself was divined and Gen changing into Gu obtained: the Subject line moved alone and should have lived, but it changed into Extinction at Hai and so could not be saved. |

> **按**:这一条的核心是「用神有一生则生，有一死则死」：用神（所占那个人的爻）只要有一条生路就能活，只要有一条死路就死；注加了一层，变出去的爻自己也要没毛病。黄金策讲的「用绝逢生」「主衰得助」（LY.ILL.HJC.47、LY.ILL.HJC.48）与此相通。
>
> **Note:** The heart of this entry is 'the Subject with one source of life lives, with one death dies': as long as the Subject (the line of the person divined) has a road to life it lives, and a road to death it dies; the note adds that the line it changes into must itself be without flaw. The Huangjin Ce's 'Extinction meeting generation' and 'weak and yet helped' (LY.ILL.HJC.47 and LY.ILL.HJC.48) go with it.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.15、LY.ILL.YM.17、LY.ILL.HJC.47、LY.ILL.HJC.48、LY.ILL.HJC.44

---

## LY.ILL.YM.17 卦无用神：元神动则不死（趋生）；忌神动而用神伏反吉（避克）；亦必得日月之救

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦里没有用神，说是凶兆，但得到原神发动就不会死，因为它是在趋向生路；忌神发动而用神伏藏，反而吉，因为它避开了克；虽然如此，也必须得到日、月的救助。注举例：酉月戊申日，占妻子本人，得睽变损，卦里没有用爻，本来不是吉兆，喜的是子孙发动，到冬天病好了，这叫趋生；亥月甲申日，也是占妻子本人，得姤变升，忌神发动，本来是凶兆，喜的是用神不出现，伏在月建下面，后来到子月壬辰日，病势逐渐缓解，因为申子辰会成水局，忌神合成了原神，这叫避克。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A hexagram without the Subject is said to be an ill omen, but if the Support moves he does not die, because he is heading toward life; if the Adversary moves and the Subject is hidden it is instead auspicious, because it avoids the control; even so, it must also gain the rescue of the Day and Month. The note's examples: in the You month on the Wushen day, a wife herself was divined and Kui changing into Sun obtained: the hexagram had no Subject line and so was not auspicious, but happily Output moved, and by winter she recovered — this is 'heading toward life'; in the Hai month on the Jiashen day, again a wife herself was divined and Gou changing into Sheng obtained: the Adversary moved, which was an ill omen, but happily the Subject did not appear and was hidden beneath the Month, and later on the Renchen day of the Zi month the illness gradually eased, for Shen, Zi and Chen meet to form a Water frame and the Adversary is combined into the Support — this is 'avoiding control'.

**《易冒·疾病章第五十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1207 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 55: Illness", base text; Yi Mao, line 1207*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦无用神，云谓凶兆，然得元神动则不死，以其趋生也。 | 卦里没有用神，说是凶兆，但得到原神发动就不会死，因为它是在趋向生路。 | A hexagram without the Subject is said to be an ill omen, but if the Support moves he does not die, because he is heading toward life. |
| 忌神动而用神伏反吉，以其避克也，虽然，亦必得日月之救焉。 | 忌神发动而用神伏藏反而吉，因为它避开了克；虽然如此，也必须得到日、月的救助。 | If the Adversary moves and the Subject is hidden it is instead auspicious, because it avoids the control; even so, it must also gain the rescue of the Day and Month. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1208 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1208*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如酉建戊申日，占妻身得睽之损，卦无用爻，本非吉兆，喜得子孙发动，及冬病愈，是谓趋生也； | 如酉月戊申日，占妻子本人，得睽变损，卦里没有用爻，本来不是吉兆，喜的是子孙发动，到冬天病好了，这叫趋生； | In the You month on the Wushen day, a wife herself was divined and Kui changing into Sun obtained: the hexagram had no Subject line and so was not auspicious, but happily Output moved, and by winter she recovered — this is 'heading toward life'; |
| 如亥建甲申日，亦占妻身，得姤之升，忌神发动，本是凶兆，喜用神不现，伏于月建之下，后交子月壬辰日，病势渐解。 | 如亥月甲申日，也是占妻子本人，得姤变升，忌神发动，本来是凶兆，喜的是用神不出现，伏在月建下面，后来到子月壬辰日，病势逐渐缓解。 | in the Hai month on the Jiashen day, again a wife herself was divined and Gou changing into Sheng obtained: the Adversary moved, which was an ill omen, but happily the Subject did not appear and was hidden beneath the Month, and later on the Renchen day of the Zi month the illness gradually eased. |
| 盖申子辰会水局，忌神合为元神，是谓避克也。 | 因为申、子、辰会成水局，忌神合成了原神，这叫避克。 | For Shen, Zi and Chen meet to form a Water frame, the Adversary is combined into the Support, and this is 'avoiding control'. |

> **按**:「趋生」是用神不在，但原神发动，气往生的方向走；「避克」是忌神发动，用神却伏藏起来，躲开了被克。第二例里申子辰三合水局，把本来的忌神（水，克火用神？）合成了原神，所以化险。这两条和卜筮正宗《原忌仇神论》里的「避空、避凶」（第 970 行）是同一套讲法。
>
> **Note:** 'Heading toward life' is when the Subject is absent but the Support moves and the qi goes toward life; 'avoiding control' is when the Adversary moves and the Subject lies hidden and escapes being controlled. In the second example Shen, Zi and Chen form a Water frame in three-harmony, combining what had been the Adversary into the Support, and so the danger is turned. These two are the same manner of speaking as the 'avoiding Void' and 'avoiding ill' in the Bushi Zhengzong's essay on the Support, Adversary and enemy spirit (line 970).

相关:LY.ILL.YM.16、LY.ILL.YM.18、LY.ILL.HJC.37、LY.ILL.BSZZ.01

## LY.ILL.YY.05 生死·必死：用爻逢虎鬼与天刑飞廉大杀三坵五墓丧门吊客死炁死符丧车浴盆而动持动克必死；忌神动遇生忌之日死、静遇忌值之日死；用随鬼入墓、财动助鬼伤用、世应双空、财旺用空、鬼生命绝、鬼动化墓、月卦化墓、用化墓绝、用逢月绝皆死

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用爻遇到白虎、鬼，以及天刑、飞廉、大杀、三坵五墓、丧门、吊客、死炁、死符、丧车、浴盆这些凶煞，它们发动而临在用爻上或发动来克用爻，一定死。忌神发动的，遇到生忌神的那天死；忌神安静的，遇到忌神当值的那天死。此外用爻随鬼入墓、财爻发动帮鬼伤用、世应都落空、财旺而用爻落空、鬼旺而命爻绝、鬼动变入墓、月卦变入墓、用爻变入墓绝、用爻遇月绝，都是死。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Subject line meets the Tiger, the ghost, and the malign stars Heavenly Punishment, Feilian, Great Killing, Three Mounds and Five Tombs, Mourning Gate, Guest of Condolence, Death Qi, Death Tally, Funeral Carriage and Bath Basin, and they move to sit on it or move to control it, he will surely die. If the Adversary moves, he dies on the day that generates the Adversary; if the Adversary is still, he dies on the day the Adversary is on duty. Besides, he dies if the Subject line follows the ghost into a tomb, Wealth moves to help the ghost and harm the Subject line, the Self and the Other are both Void, Wealth is strong and the Subject line Void, the ghost is in Birth and the destiny in Extinction, the ghost moves and changes into a tomb, the month-hexagram changes into a tomb, the Subject line changes into a tomb or Extinction, or the Subject line meets Extinction in the Month.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2993 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 2993*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用爻逢虎鬼天刑飞廉大杀三坵五墓丧门吊客死炁死符丧车浴盆，动持动克者，必死也。 | 用爻遇到白虎、鬼，以及天刑、飞廉、大杀、三坵五墓、丧门、吊客、死炁、死符、丧车、浴盆这些凶煞，它们发动而临在用爻上，或发动来克用爻的，一定死。 | If the Subject line meets the Tiger, the ghost, and the malign stars Heavenly Punishment, Feilian, Great Killing, Three Mounds and Five Tombs, Mourning Gate, Guest of Condolence, Death Qi, Death Tally, Funeral Carriage and Bath Basin, and they move to sit on it or move to control it, he will surely die. |
| 忌神动者，遇生忌之日死也。 | 忌神发动的，遇到生忌神的那天死。 | If the Adversary moves, he dies on the day that generates the Adversary. |
| 忌神静者，遇忌值之日死也。 | 忌神安静的，遇到忌神当值的那天死。 | If the Adversary is still, he dies on the day the Adversary is on duty. |
| 用爻随鬼入墓者死。 | 用爻跟着鬼入墓的，死。 | If the Subject line follows the ghost into a tomb, he dies. |
| 财动助鬼伤用者死。 | 财爻发动帮助鬼、伤到用爻的，死。 | If Wealth moves to help the ghost and harm the Subject line, he dies. |
| 世应双空者死。 | 世爻、应爻都落空的，死。 | If the Self and the Other are both Void, he dies. |
| 财旺用空者死。 | 财爻旺而用爻落空的，死。 | If Wealth is strong and the Subject line Void, he dies. |
| 鬼生命绝者死。 | 鬼在长生而命爻绝的，死。 | If the ghost is in Birth and the destiny line in Extinction, he dies. |
| 鬼动化墓者死。 | 鬼动了变出墓的，死。 | If the ghost moves and changes into a tomb, he dies. |
| 月卦化墓者死。 | 月卦变入墓的，死。 | If the month-hexagram changes into a tomb, he dies. |
| 用化墓绝者死。 | 用爻变出墓、绝的，死。 | If the Subject line changes into a tomb or Extinction, he dies. |
| 用逢月绝者死。 | 用爻遇到月绝的，死。 | If the Subject line meets Extinction in the Month, he dies. |

**书中卦例** — 《易隐》第 3036–3037 行 · *Case from the book; Yi Yin, lines 3036–3037*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 庚寅年戊寅月丁卯日，父占子病，得临之损。 | 庚寅年戊寅月丁卯日，父亲占儿子的病，得到临卦变损卦。 | In the Gengyin year, the Wuyin month, on the Dingmao day, a father divined about his son's illness and obtained Lin changing into Sun. |
| 六爻酉金，子孙独发，化绝于寅，又绝于岁月建中，更加卯日冲破，果于木日死也。 | 六爻上的酉金子孙独自发动，变出寅而绝，又在岁、月建里绝，再加上卯日冲破它，果然在木日死了。 | The You Metal Output line on the sixth line moved alone and changed into Yin, where it is in Extinction, and it was also in Extinction in the Year and the Month, and the Mao day further clashed and broke it; and indeed he died on a Wood day. |

**书中卦例** — 《易隐》第 3039 行 · *Case from the book; Yi Yin, line 3039*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 庚寅年庚辰月丁卯日，妻占夫病，得涣之姤。 | 庚寅年庚辰月丁卯日，妻子占丈夫的病，得到涣卦变姤卦。 | In the Gengyin year, the Gengchen month, on the Dingmao day, a wife divined about her husband's illness and obtained Huan changing into Gou. |
| 鬼伏三爻下，值旬空，子为忌神，兄为讐神，二爻并发，独喜用爻伏藏耳。 | 官鬼伏在三爻下面，遇到旬空；子孙是忌神，兄弟是仇神，两爻一起发动，只喜欢用爻伏藏着。 | The Pressure line hides beneath the third line and meets Void; Output is the Adversary and Peer the enemy spirit, and both lines move together; the only mercy is that the Subject line is hidden. |
| 至乙亥日，透出用爻，才受忌伤，而夫死也。 | 到乙亥日，用爻透出来，才受到忌神的伤害，丈夫就死了。 | On the Yihai day the Subject line came out into view, and only then was it harmed by the Adversary, and the husband died. |

> **按**:这一条是「生死」段里断死的爻象；下一条断死的卦象、再下一条断死日。「虎」是白虎，「鬼」是官鬼；天刑、飞廉、大杀、三坵五墓、丧门、吊客、死炁、死符、丧车、浴盆都是凶的神煞，书里没有再解释。「月卦」书里没有解释，按字面译作月卦（应是按月起的卦身一类的说法）。两个断例挂在这里：临之损（用爻化绝又岁月绝再遭日冲，木日死）、涣之姤（用爻伏藏值旬空，到乙亥日透出才受忌伤而死）。
>
> **Note:** This entry gives the line-images that judge death in the section on life and death; the next gives the hexagram-images and the one after the days of death. 'Tiger' is the White Tiger and 'ghost' the Pressure line; Heavenly Punishment, Feilian, Great Killing, Three Mounds and Five Tombs, Mourning Gate, Guest of Condolence, Death Qi, Death Tally, Funeral Carriage and Bath Basin are all malign spirit-stars which the book does not explain further. 'Month-hexagram' is not explained in the book and is rendered literally (probably something of the kind of the Body lines taken by the month). Two cases hang here: Lin into Sun (the Subject line changing into Extinction, in Extinction in the Year and Month as well, and clashed by the day, dying on a Wood day) and Huan into Gou (the Subject line hidden and in Void, coming out on the Yihai day, harmed by the Adversary and dying).

相关:LY.ILL.YY.04、LY.ILL.YY.06、LY.ILL.HJC.41、LY.ILL.HJC.54、LY.ILL.ZSZ.01

## LY.ILL.YY.06 生死·死卦：卦身墓于世（损）、本宫外卦墓于内卦（遁豫升蛊井大有咸）、世坐本宫墓（噬嗑蒙泰归妹）、世坐鬼墓（乾丰中孚升家人旅）、世坐财墓（观解随益泰兑）、卦化墓绝、用逢月破者死

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:从卦象断死：卦身入墓在世爻的（损卦）；本宫的外卦入墓在内卦的（遁、豫、升、蛊、井、大有、咸）；世爻坐在本宫的墓上的（噬嗑、蒙、泰、归妹）；世爻坐在鬼的墓上的（乾、丰、中孚、升、家人、旅）；世爻坐在财的墓上的（财是禄命，最忌刑、冲、墓、绝；观、解、随、益、泰、兑）；卦变入墓、绝的；用爻遇月破的，都是死。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Death judged from the hexagram: the Body's tomb at the Self (the hexagram Sun); the outer trigram of the home palace having its tomb in the inner trigram (Dun, Yu, Sheng, Gu, Jing, Dayou, Xian); the Self sitting on the tomb of the home palace (Shihe, Meng, Tai, Guimei); the Self sitting on the ghost's tomb (Qian, Feng, Zhongfu, Sheng, Jiaren, Lü); the Self sitting on the tomb of Wealth (Wealth is the salary and destiny and fears punishment, clash, tomb and extinction; Guan, Xie, Sui, Yi, Tai, Dui); the hexagram changing into a tomb or Extinction; and the Subject line meeting Month-break: in each case he dies.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2993 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 2993*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦身墓于世者死也(损卦)。 | 卦身入墓在世爻的，死（损卦）。 | If the Body's tomb lies at the Self, he dies (the hexagram Sun). |
| 本宫外卦墓于内卦者死(遁豫升蛊井大有咸)。 | 本宫的外卦入墓在内卦的，死（遁、豫、升、蛊、井、大有、咸）。 | If the outer trigram of the home palace has its tomb in the inner trigram, he dies (Dun, Yu, Sheng, Gu, Jing, Dayou, Xian). |
| 世坐本宫墓者，死也(噬嗑蒙泰归妹)。 | 世爻坐在本宫的墓上的，死（噬嗑、蒙、泰、归妹）。 | If the Self sits on the tomb of the home palace, he dies (Shihe, Meng, Tai, Guimei). |
| 世坐鬼墓者死也(乾丰中孚升家人旅)。 | 世爻坐在鬼的墓上的，死（乾、丰、中孚、升、家人、旅）。 | If the Self sits on the ghost's tomb, he dies (Qian, Feng, Zhongfu, Sheng, Jiaren, Lü). |
| 世坐财墓者死也(财为禄命，忌刑冲墓绝，观解随益泰兑卦)。 | 世爻坐在财的墓上的，死（财是禄命，最忌刑、冲、墓、绝；观、解、随、益、泰、兑卦）。 | If the Self sits on the tomb of Wealth, he dies (Wealth is the salary and destiny and fears punishment, clash, tomb and extinction; Guan, Xie, Sui, Yi, Tai, Dui). |
| 卦化墓绝者死也(乾兑化艮坎，艮坤化巽离，化乾震巽化坤)。 | 卦变入墓、绝的，死（乾、兑变艮、坎；艮、坤变巽、离；变乾；震、巽变坤）。 | If the hexagram changes into a tomb or Extinction, he dies (Qian and Dui changing into Gen and Kan; Gen and Kun changing into Xun and Li; changing into Qian; Zhen and Xun changing into Kun). |
| 用逢月破者死。 | 用爻遇到月破的，死。 | If the Subject line meets Month-break, he dies. |

> **按**:括号里的卦名是书里自己注的，指明哪几个卦有这个象。「卦化墓绝」那一句括号里的名单在原文里是「乾兑化艮坎，艮坤化巽离，化乾震巽化坤」：第三个「化乾」没有主语，书里没有说明，译文照写。「旅」是旅卦（Lü），「履」是履卦（Lu），两个卦不要混。「财为禄命」的意思是财爻代表食禄和命，所以怕刑冲墓绝。
>
> **Note:** The hexagram names in brackets are the book's own and show which hexagrams carry the image. In the bracket of the sentence on the hexagram changing into a tomb or extinction, the original list is 'Qian and Dui changing into Gen and Kan, Gen and Kun changing into Xun and Li, changing into Qian, Zhen and Xun changing into Kun': the third 'changing into Qian' has no subject, which the book does not explain, and the translation follows the text. 'Lü' is the hexagram Lü (Travel) and 'Lu' the hexagram Lu (Treading); the two are not to be confused. 'Wealth is salary and destiny' means the Wealth line stands for one's pay and one's life, so it fears punishment, clash, tomb and extinction.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.05、LY.ILL.YY.07、LY.ILL.HJC.54

---

## LY.ILL.YY.08 生死·凶卦：黄泉杀（春大畜小畜履井复、夏遁睽临、秋明夷蛊旅、冬遁既济明夷归妹）、四灭卦（春蒙夏蛊秋剥冬旅）、四没卦（春需夏观秋节冬临）、棺椁杀（恒与益），占病逢之死

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:黄泉杀：春天是大畜、小畜、履、井、复，夏天是遁、睽、临，秋天是明夷、蛊、旅，冬天是遁、既济、明夷、归妹。四灭卦：春天蒙、夏天蛊、秋天剥、冬天旅。四没卦：春天需、夏天观、秋天节、冬天临。棺椁杀：恒和益。以上这些卦，占病的人逢到就会死。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Yellow Springs killer: in spring Daxu, Xiaoxu, Lu, Jing and Fu; in summer Dun, Kui and Lin; in autumn Mingyi, Gu and Lü; in winter Dun, Jiji, Mingyi and Guimei. The Four Extinguishing hexagrams: Meng in spring, Gu in summer, Bo in autumn, Lü in winter. The Four Submerging hexagrams: Xu in spring, Guan in summer, Jie in autumn, Lin in winter. The Coffin killer: Heng and Yi. One who divines about illness and meets any of these hexagrams dies.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2993 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 2993*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 黄泉杀者，春则大畜小畜履井复也。 | 黄泉杀：春天是大畜、小畜、履、井、复这几卦。 | The Yellow Springs killer: in spring it is Daxu, Xiaoxu, Lu, Jing and Fu. |
| 夏则遁睽临也。 | 夏天是遁、睽、临。 | In summer it is Dun, Kui and Lin. |
| 秋则明夷蛊旅也。 | 秋天是明夷、蛊、旅。 | In autumn it is Mingyi, Gu and Lü. |
| 冬则遁既济明夷归妹也。 | 冬天是遁、既济、明夷、归妹。 | In winter it is Dun, Jiji, Mingyi and Guimei. |
| 四灭卦者，春蒙夏蛊秋剥冬旅也。 | 四灭卦：春天蒙，夏天蛊，秋天剥，冬天旅。 | The Four Extinguishing hexagrams: spring Meng, summer Gu, autumn Bo, winter Lü. |
| 四没卦者，春需夏观秋节冬临也。 | 四没卦：春天需，夏天观，秋天节，冬天临。 | The Four Submerging hexagrams: spring Xu, summer Guan, autumn Jie, winter Lin. |
| 棺椁杀者，恒与益也(已上诸卦占病者死)。 | 棺椁杀：是恒和益（以上这些卦，占病的人会死）。 | The Coffin killer: Heng and Yi (for all the hexagrams above, one who divines about illness dies). |

> **按**:「遁」在黄泉杀的夏和冬里各出现一次，原文如此。这几组卦名书里只列名单，没有说为什么；「四灭」「四没」是按季节各取一卦的名目。「旅」（Lü）和「履」（Lu）是两个卦。「已上诸卦占病者死」是书里对这一组的总结，是说占病遇到这些卦就是死，写得很绝对，本库照抄，不替它担保。
>
> **Note:** 'Dun' appears once in summer and once in winter in the Yellow Springs killer, as in the original. The book gives only the lists of hexagram names and no reasons; the 'Four Extinguishing' and 'Four Submerging' are names for one hexagram per season. 'Lü' and 'Lu' are two hexagrams. 'For all the hexagrams above, one who divines about illness dies' is the book's own summing up of the group and is stated absolutely; the library copies it and does not stand surety for it.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.07、LY.ILL.YY.09

---

## LY.ILL.YY.10 生死·男女老少之忌：男忌官鬼长生日得病、女忌官鬼沐浴日得病；无龙福月解天医来救则鬼爻墓绝日必死；晁以道：用爻命爻不上卦者死；男忌鬼空、女忌财空，少年卦忌死囚休废、老人卦忌旺相

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:男的忌讳在官鬼长生的那天得病，女的忌讳在官鬼沐浴的那天得病。如果没有青龙、福德、月解、天医发动来生合用爻，鬼爻入墓、绝的那天一定死。晁以道说：用爻、命爻不上卦的，死。还有，男的忌讳鬼落空，女的忌讳财落空；少年占的卦忌讳死、囚、休、废，老人占的卦忌讳旺相。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A man should fear falling ill on the day the Pressure line is born, and a woman on the day the Pressure line is at Bath. If none of the Dragon, Fortune, Month Release or Heavenly Doctor moves to generate or combine with the Subject line, he will surely die on the day the Pressure line is in its tomb or Extinction. Chao Yidao says: if the Subject line and the destiny line do not appear in the hexagram, he dies. Further, a man should fear the ghost being Void and a woman Wealth being Void; a young person's hexagram fears Death, trapped, resting and abandoned, and an old person's hexagram fears being strong.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2993 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 2993*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又男忌官鬼长生日得病，女忌官鬼沐浴日得病。 | 又，男的忌讳在官鬼长生的那天得病，女的忌讳在官鬼沐浴的那天得病。 | Also a man should fear falling ill on the day the Pressure line is born, and a woman on the day the Pressure line is at Bath. |
| 若无龙福月解天医动来生合用爻者，鬼爻墓绝日必死也。 | 如果没有青龙、福德、月解、天医发动来生合用爻的，鬼爻墓、绝的那天一定死。 | If none of the Dragon, Fortune, Month Release or Heavenly Doctor moves to generate or combine with the Subject line, he will surely die on the day the Pressure line is in its tomb or Extinction. |
| 又晁以道曰，用爻命爻不上卦者死。 | 另外晁以道说：用爻、命爻不上卦的，死。 | Chao Yidao also says: if the Subject line and the destiny line do not appear in the hexagram, he dies. |
| 更男忌鬼空，女忌财空，少年卦忌死囚休废，老人卦忌旺相也。 | 还有，男的忌讳鬼落空，女的忌讳财落空，少年占的卦忌讳死、囚、休、废，老人占的卦忌讳旺相。 | Further, a man should fear the ghost being Void and a woman Wealth being Void; a young person's hexagram fears Death, trapped, resting and abandoned, and an old person's hexagram fears being strong. |

> **按**:这一条和黄金策讲的「主空无救」「用绝逢生」（LY.ILL.HJC.37、LY.ILL.HJC.47）相近，不同的是这里按男女、老少分了忌讳：男忌鬼空，女忌财空；少年怕死囚休废，老人怕旺相——老人怕旺相与黄金策「占病不喜旺相，太刚则折」（LY.ILL.HJC.36）同一个意思。「晁以道」是宋代人晁说之，书里只写了字。「死囚休废」是五行在季节里的四种无气状态。
>
> **Note:** This entry is close to the Huangjin Ce's 'the main image Void, no rescue' and 'Extinction meeting generation' (LY.ILL.HJC.37 and LY.ILL.HJC.47); the difference is that it divides the fears by sex and age: a man fears the ghost Void and a woman Wealth Void; the young fear Death, trapped, resting and abandoned and the old fear being strong — the old fearing strength agrees with the Huangjin Ce's 'in illness one does not like strength; too rigid, it snaps' (LY.ILL.HJC.36). 'Chao Yidao' is the Song man Chao Shuozhi, of whom the book gives only the style-name. 'Death, trapped, resting, abandoned' are the four forceless states of an element in a season.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.09、LY.ILL.YY.11、LY.ILL.HJC.36、LY.ILL.HJC.37、LY.ILL.HJC.47

## LY.ILL.ZS.01 断病生死全凭用神：卦名、卦身世身兼用、本命丧门、蛇虎星煞都不验，只有卦变六冲例外

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:断病的生死，全凭用神，别的都不验：只看卦名的（得明夷、大过、观、贲、蛊、夬、丰、同人、大畜、需就断必死），说本命临死气丧门、寿衣棺木要早备的，说螣蛇动主死、白虎动主丧的，都不灵。恶煞凶神就算临了身命，只要用神、世爻和身爻旺相，你说他是生是死？不看用神也能断生死的，只有卦变六冲一种。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Life and death in an illness are judged from the Subject alone, and nothing else proves true: judging from the hexagram name alone (Mingyi, Daguo, Guan, Bi, Gu, Guai, Feng, Tongren, Daxu and Xu being called surely fatal), saying that a natal line on the death spirit or the mourning gate means shroud and coffin must be ready early, saying that a moving Coiling Snake means death and a moving White Tiger mourning: none of these is reliable. When fierce spirits and ill stars already sit on the body and the natal line, yet the Subject and the Self and body lines are strong, who is to say he lives or dies? The one case in which life and death may be judged without looking at the Subject is a hexagram changing into a Six-Clash hexagram.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12628 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12628*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 野鹤曰：人有七情，致生百病，急于问卜以慰其心，奈何卜筮诸书舛错悖谬，令人反无定见，既取用神，又用卦身、世身，兼用本命，又有不看身命用神，单看卦验，卦得明夷、大过、观、贲、蛊、夬、丰、同人、大畜、需者，断之必死。 | 野鹤说：人有七情，生出百病，急着来问卜是想安慰自己的心，可是卜筮的书舛错悖谬，反而让人没有定见：已经取了用神，又用卦身、世身，还兼用本命；又有不看身命和用神、只看卦名的，得到明夷、大过、观、贲、蛊、夬、丰、同人、大畜、需这几卦，就断定必死。 | The Old Man of Yehe says: a person has the seven emotions, which breed a hundred illnesses, and he is in haste to ask by divination to comfort his heart; but the divination books are full of error and absurdity and leave people with no settled view: having taken the Subject, they also use the hexagram Body and the Self and body lines, and add the natal line besides; and some do not look at the body, the natal line or the Subject at all, and look only at the hexagram itself, judging that whoever gets Mingyi, Daguo, Guan, Bi, Gu, Guai, Feng, Tongren, Daxu or Xu will surely die. |
| 又云：本命临死气丧门，衾棺早备。 | 又说：本命临在死气、丧门上，寿衣棺木要早点备好。 | It also says: if the natal line sits on the death spirit or the mourning gate, have the shroud and coffin ready early. |
| 又云：蛇动主死，虎动主丧。 | 又说：螣蛇发动主死亡，白虎发动主丧事。 | It also says: a moving Coiling Snake means death, and a moving White Tiger mourning. |
| 以星煞六神而断生死，独不思恶煞凶神已临身命，而用神世身倘若旺相者，断之生耶？ | 拿星煞六神来断生死，偏偏不想想恶煞凶神已经临在身命上，而用神、世爻和身爻要是旺相，断他是生呢？ | They judge life and death by stars and the six spirits, yet never think: if fierce spirits and ill stars already sit on the body and the natal line while the Subject and the Self and body lines are strong, is he judged to live? |
| 死耶？ | 还是死呢？ | or to die? |
| 余试多年，或生或死，全凭用神，余皆不验。 | 我试了多年，或生或死，全凭用神，其余的都不灵验。 | I have tested it for many years: whether he lives or dies rests wholly on the Subject, and the rest proves unreliable. |
| 内有不看用神而断生死者，惟卦变六冲而已。 | 其中不看用神也能断生死的，只有卦变六冲这一种。 | Among them the only case in which life and death are judged without looking at the Subject is a hexagram changing into a Six-Clash hexagram. |

> **打架**:断生死看什么：黄金策的病体章除了用神，还讲卦身、世爻、本命爻（LY.ILL.HJC.35、HJC.41、HJC.53、HJC.54）；野鹤说这些并用是舛错，断生死全凭用神，只有卦变六冲例外。
>
> **Where they disagree:** What to look at in judging life and death: besides the Subject the Huangjin Ce's chapter on the patient's condition also speaks of the hexagram Body, the Self line and the natal line (LY.ILL.HJC.35, HJC.41, HJC.53, HJC.54); the Old Man of Yehe says that using them together is error, that life and death rest wholly on the Subject, and that a hexagram changing into Six-Clash is the only exception.

> **按**:野鹤这一段是对整个占病断法的总批评。他驳的是靠卦名、本命丧门、蛇虎星煞断生死的旧法；黄金策病体章断生死靠的是各爻的生克旺衰，不靠星煞。「衾棺」是入殓用的被和棺材。「明夷、大过、观、贲、蛊、夬、丰、同人、大畜、需」是那些书里断必死的十个卦名。
>
> **Note:** This passage is the Old Man's general criticism of the whole way of judging illness. What he rejects is the old practice of judging life and death by the hexagram name, by the natal line on the death spirit or mourning gate, and by the stars and the Snake and Tiger; the Huangjin Ce's chapter on the patient's condition judges by the generation, control and strength of the lines and not by stars. 'Quilt and coffin' is the shroud and coffin for the laying-out. Mingyi, Daguo, Guan, Bi, Gu, Guai, Feng, Tongren, Daxu and Xu are the ten hexagram names that those books judge surely fatal.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.35、LY.ILL.HJC.41、LY.ILL.HJC.53、LY.ILL.HJC.54、LY.ILL.ZS.02、LY.ILL.ZS.03、LY.ILL.ZSZ.02

## LY.ILL.ZS.02 六冲变冲久病难于调治：久病卦逢六冲、卦变六冲，不论用神衰旺，是不治之症；近病逢之不药而愈；久病逢冲必死，六冲变六冲更甚

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:久病遇到卦是六冲、又变六冲，不管用神旺衰，是治不好的病；近病遇到它，不吃药也会好。久病遇冲必死，六冲变六冲更是如此，就算用神临着月建、又有生它的爻，也活不了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In a long illness, a hexagram that is Six-Clash and changes into Six-Clash is an incurable illness whatever the strength of the Subject; a recent illness that meets it recovers without medicine. A long illness meeting a clash surely means death, and Six-Clash changing into Six-Clash all the more, so that even if the Subject sits on the Month and a line generates it, the patient cannot live.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12629 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12629*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 六冲变冲，久病难于调治。 | 六冲卦变成六冲卦，久病难以调治。 | A Six-Clash hexagram changing into Six-Clash: a long illness is hard to treat. |
| 久病者，卦逢六冲，卦变六冲，不论用神之衰旺，乃不治之症也。 | 久病的人，卦遇六冲，又变六冲，不管用神是旺是衰，就是治不好的病。 | For one long ill, when the hexagram meets Six-Clash and changes into Six-Clash, whether the Subject is strong or weak, it is an incurable illness. |
| 近病逢之，不药而愈。 | 近病遇到它，不吃药也会好。 | A recent illness meeting it recovers without medicine. |

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13071 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13071*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 野鹤曰：存前卦者，使知久病逢冲，用神虽临月建，又有戌土之生，亦不能活也； | 野鹤说：留下前面这一卦，是要让人知道久病遇冲，用神即使临着月建，又有戌土生它，也活不了； | The Old Man of Yehe says: the former hexagram is kept so that one may know that in a long illness meeting a clash, even if the Subject sits on the Month and Xu Earth generates it, the patient cannot live; |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 12994 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 12994*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又如：寅月乙酉日(旬空：午未)，占甥久病，得“坤之乾” | 又如：寅月乙酉日（旬空：午、未），占外甥的久病，得坤卦变乾卦。 | Another: in the Yin month, on a Yiyou day (void: Wu and Wei), a divination on a nephew's long illness gave Kun changing into Qian. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 13042 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 13042*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 久病逢冲必死，矧六冲变六冲乎？ | 久病遇冲必死，何况是六冲变六冲呢？ | A long illness meeting a clash surely means death, let alone Six-Clash changing into Six-Clash. |
| 三月必危。 | 三月一定危险。 | The third month will be dangerous. |
| 果卒于丑月。 | 果然死在丑月。 | He did die in the Chou month. |
| 应三月者，酉子为用神，动而逢合之月也，又是冲去戌土，酉金无以为生也。 | 应验在三月，是因为酉金子孙是用神，动了遇到合的月份；又是冲走了戌土，酉金没有东西生它。 | It came true in the third month because the You Metal Output line is the Subject, and the month is one in which it moves and meets a combination; besides, Xu Earth is clashed away, and You Metal has nothing to generate it. |

> **按**:书里没有交代断语里的「三月」和实际死的「丑月」是怎么算的，译文按字面。「酉子」按上下文是坤宫里酉金的子孙爻（坤属土，土生金）。野鹤的注留这一卦，是要说明：久病逢冲，用神就算临月建、又有生它的爻，也活不了；同一条注的后半（趋避之法）对应下面的 LY.ILL.ZS.17。
>
> **Note:** The book does not say how the 'third month' of the judgment relates to the 'Chou month' in which he in fact died, and the translation follows the letter. 'You Output' is, from the context, the You Metal Output line in the Kun palace (Kun belongs to Earth and Earth generates Metal). The Old Man's note keeps this hexagram to show that in a long illness meeting a clash, the Subject cannot live even if it sits on the Month and a line generates it; the second half of the same note (the method of avoidance) matches LY.ILL.ZS.17 below.

相关:LY.ILL.ZS.03、LY.ILL.ZS.16、LY.ILL.ZS.17

## LY.ILL.ZS.03 卦变绝克新疾亦主危亡：六冲变六冲近病也危，是因为化回头克（巽木变乾金、艮坤变震巽）；化比和、化克去、化回头生，近病吉久病凶（六冲之故）；此外必看用神

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:六冲卦变成六冲卦，新得的病也主危亡，是因为变出了回头克：比如巽木变乾金、艮坤变震巽，都叫回头相克，虽然没有入墓入绝，也主危亡。如果变出比和、变出把克去掉的、或者变出回头生，近病吉、久病凶，这是六冲的缘故。除了这种冲克以外，一定要看用神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When a Six-Clash hexagram changes into Six-Clash, even a recent illness means danger of death, because of a returning control: Xun Wood changing into Qian Metal, or Gen or Kun changing into Zhen or Xun, are all called a returning control, and though there is no tomb or extinction, they still mean danger of death. If it changes into peace, into a control that removes the danger, or into a returning generation, a recent illness is lucky and a long one unlucky, because of the Six-Clash. Apart from this clash and control, one must look at the Subject.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12630 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12630*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦变绝克，新疾亦主危亡。 | 卦变出绝克，新得的病也主危亡。 | A hexagram changing into a fatal control: even a new illness means danger of death. |
| 卦变者，亦是六冲而变六冲，何其近病亦危也？ | 所说的卦变，也是六冲卦变六冲卦，为什么近病也危险呢？ | The 'hexagram change' here is also a Six-Clash hexagram changing into Six-Clash; why is even a recent illness in danger? |
| 乃因化回头克耳。 | 是因为变出了回头克。 | It is because of the returning control. |
| 如巽木变乾金、艮坤变震巽，皆谓之回头相克，虽非墓绝，亦主危亡； | 比如巽木变乾金、艮坤变震巽，都叫回头相克，虽然没有入墓入绝，也主危亡； | For example Xun Wood changing into Qian Metal, or Gen and Kun changing into Zhen and Xun, are all called mutual returning control, and though there is no tomb or extinction, they still mean danger of death; |
| 若化比和、化克去，及化回头生，近病吉，久病凶，六冲之故也。 | 如果变出比和、变出把克去掉的、以及变出回头生，近病吉，久病凶，这是六冲的缘故。 | if it changes into peace, into a control that removes the danger, or into a returning generation, a recent illness is lucky and a long one unlucky, because of the Six-Clash. |
| 除此冲克之外，必看用神。 | 除了这种冲克以外，一定要看用神。 | Apart from this clash and control, one must look at the Subject. |

> **按**:「回头相克」是动爻变出来的爻反过来克它自己。「巽木变乾金、艮坤变震巽」是八经卦之间的变化：乾金克巽木，震巽木克艮坤土。「化克去」书里没有解释，大概是变出的爻把克用神的那一爻克掉了。
>
> **Note:** A 'returning control' is when the line that a moving line changes into controls that line in return. 'Xun Wood changing into Qian Metal, Gen and Kun into Zhen and Xun' are changes between the eight trigrams: Qian Metal controls Xun Wood, and Zhen and Xun Wood control Gen and Kun Earth. The book does not explain 'changing into a control that removes'; it probably means that the line changed into controls away the line that was controlling the Subject.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.44、LY.ILL.ZS.02

## LY.ILL.ZS.04 用遇旬空近病不忧、用逢月破久病难安：自占病世为用，占父母兄弟以其为用；近病用神旬空或化空，无日月动爻冲克则冲空实空之日即愈，逢冲克病虽重不死；月破看用神衰旺；久病用神空破，旺相也无法医治

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:自己占病，世爻是用神；占父母、兄弟的病，拿父母、兄弟做用神。近病：用神落旬空或变空，如果没有日月和动爻来冲克，冲空、实空的日子病就好；如果遇到冲克，病再重也不会死。用神逢月破，要看用神旺衰：旺的，到实破那天或出了这个月就好；衰又受克的，一定危险。久病：用神落旬空或月破的，就算用神旺相，也没法医治。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In divining one's own illness the Self line is the Subject; in divining a parent or brother, the Resource or Peer line is the Subject. A recent illness whose Subject falls into Void or changes into Void heals on the day the Void is clashed or filled if no Day, Month or moving line clashes or controls it; if it meets clash or control, even a heavy illness does not lead to death. If the Subject meets Month-break, look at whether it is strong or weak: if strong, the patient recovers on the day the break is filled or once the month has passed; if weak and controlled, he is surely in danger. In a long illness, if the Subject falls into Void or Month-break, then even if it is strong nothing can be done to treat it.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12631–12635 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, lines 12631–12635*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用遇寻空，近病何须忧虑； | 用神遇到旬空，近病何必忧虑； | The Subject meeting Void: why should a recent illness be feared; |
| 用逢月破，久病难许安宁。 | 用神逢月破，久病难得安宁。 | the Subject meeting Month-break: a long illness can hardly be at peace. |
| 自占病，世为用； | 自己占病，世爻是用神； | In divining one's own illness, the Self line is the Subject; |
| 占父母兄弟，以父母兄弟为用。 | 占父母、兄弟的病，拿父母、兄弟做用神。 | in divining a parent or a brother, the Resource line or the Peer line is the Subject. |
| 余皆在用神章内详之。 | 其余的都在用神章里详细讲了。 | The rest is set out in detail in the chapter on the Subject. |
| 近病者，用神动静，而值旬空及化空者，若无日月动爻冲克，许冲空、实空之日即愈； | 近病：用神动也好静也好，落了旬空或变出空的，如果没有日月和动爻来冲克，断在冲空、实空的日子就好； | For a recent illness, if the Subject, moving or still, falls into Void or changes into Void, and no Day, Month or moving line clashes or controls it, promise recovery on the day the Void is clashed or filled; |
| 倘逢冲克，病虽重而不死。 | 如果遇到冲克，病再重也不会死。 | if it meets clash or control, the illness, though heavy, does not lead to death. |
| 若值月破，须看用神之衰旺，旺则愈于实破之日，及出月而愈； | 如果逢月破，要看用神的旺衰，旺的，到实破那天以及出了这个月就好； | If it meets Month-break, one must see whether the Subject is strong or weak: if strong, he recovers on the day the break is filled, or when the month is out; |
| 衰而受克者，必危。 | 衰又受克的，一定危险。 | if weak and controlled, he is surely in danger. |
| 久病者，用神值旬空月破者，即使用神旺相，亦无法医治之。 | 久病的，用神落了旬空、月破，即使用神旺相，也没有办法医治。 | In a long illness, if the Subject meets Void or Month-break, then even if the Subject is strong, nothing can be done to treat it. |

> **按**:「寻空」原文如此，按上下文是「旬空」。「冲空」是用冲把落空的爻冲开；「实空」是落空的那个地支值日，把空填实，「实破」同理。这一条讲的是近病和久病要分开断，是下一条（LY.ILL.ZS.05）驳「主空无救」的根据。
>
> **Note:** 'Xunkong' is written 'searching for the void' in the original, and from the context it is Void. To 'clash Void' is to clash open a line that has fallen into Void; to 'fill Void' is for the branch of the Void line to rule the day and fill the void, and 'fill a break' is the same. This entry judges recent and long illness separately, and is the ground of the next entry (LY.ILL.ZS.05), which rejects 'a Void main image cannot be saved'.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.37、LY.ILL.ZS.05、LY.ILL.DYTJ.51

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.05 主空无救不分近久非经验之说：野鹤驳《海底眼》《黄金策》，近病久病要分；觉子验得近病旬空又逢三合六合必成久病而终

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:主象落空，不能一概说没救、会死在半路，近病和久病要分开断（近病落空，空一出就好）。觉子补充他验过的：近病落旬空，如果又碰上三合、六合，一定拖成久病最后死掉。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Void main image cannot be judged in one breath as beyond rescue and doomed to die on the way: recent and long illness must be told apart (a recent illness that falls into Void heals when the Void is out). Juezi adds what he has verified: a recent illness that falls into Void and also meets a three-harmony or a six-combine surely turns into a long illness and ends in death.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12637 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12637*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 野鹤曰：《海底眼》、《黄金策》皆云：主空无救，中道而殂，不分近病久病，非经验之说也。 | 野鹤说：《海底眼》和《黄金策》都说主象落空就没有救、会死在半路，不分近病久病，这不是经验之谈。 | The Old Man of Yehe says: the Haidi Yan and the Huangjin Ce both say that a Void main image cannot be saved and one dies on the way, without distinguishing recent from long illness; this is not a saying drawn from experience. |

**觉子(李文辉,《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12637 行 · *Juezi (Li Wenhui, Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12637*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 觉子曰：予得验者，近病值旬空，若逢三合六合者，必成久病而终。 | 觉子说：我验过的是，近病遇到旬空，如果又碰上三合、六合，一定拖成久病而死。 | Juezi says: what I have verified is that a recent illness meeting Void, if it also meets a three-harmony or a six-combine, surely turns into a long illness and ends in death. |

> **打架**:主象落空要不要紧：黄金策（LY.ILL.HJC.37）和《海底眼》说主象落空就没救，不分近病久病；野鹤说这不是经验之谈，近病落空到冲空、实空的日子就好（LY.ILL.ZS.04）。觉子又补一条：近病落旬空遇三合、六合会拖成久病而死。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether a Void main image matters: the Huangjin Ce (LY.ILL.HJC.37) and the Haidi Yan say that a Void main image has no rescue, without distinguishing recent from long illness; the Old Man of Yehe says this is not drawn from experience, and that a recent illness that falls into Void heals on the day the Void is clashed or filled (LY.ILL.ZS.04). Juezi adds a point: a recent illness in Void that meets a three-harmony or a six-combine drags into a long illness and ends in death.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.37、LY.ILL.ZS.04、LY.ILL.ZS.16、LY.ILL.DYTJ.51

## LY.ILL.ZS.06 用化鬼、鬼化用慎防不测，忌化用、用化忌最难调治：自占病世爻不宜变鬼或化回头克，占兄弟妻子不宜鬼与其用神互变，又不宜兄变财财化兄父化子子化父；父动化鬼是化生，轻病即愈，久病鬼化父、父化鬼皆主危亡

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用神变成官鬼、官鬼变成用神，要慎防不测；忌神变成用神、用神变成忌神，最难调治。自己占病，世爻不宜变鬼，也不宜变出回头克；占兄弟、妻子的病，鬼与兄弟、妻子不宜互变，兄变财、财变兄、父变子、子变父也不宜。鬼是父母的元神，父母爻动、变成鬼，是化生，病轻的马上好；久病的，鬼变父、父变鬼，都主危亡。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Subject changing into Pressure or Pressure changing into the Subject calls for great care against the unforeseen; the Adversary changing into the Subject or the Subject into the Adversary is hardest to treat. In one's own illness the Self line should not change into Pressure or into a returning control; in a brother's or a wife's illness Pressure and the Peer or Wealth line should not change into each other, and neither should Peer into Wealth, Wealth into Peer, Resource into Output or Output into Resource. Pressure is the Support of Resource, so Resource moving and changing into Pressure is a changing into generation and a light illness heals at once; in a long illness, Pressure changing into Resource or Resource into Pressure always means danger of death.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12638 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12638*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用化鬼、鬼化用，慎防不测； | 用神变成鬼、鬼变成用神，要慎防不测； | The Subject changing into Pressure, or Pressure changing into the Subject: beware of the unforeseen; |
| 忌化用，用化忌，最难调治。 | 忌神变成用神、用神变成忌神，最难调治。 | the Adversary changing into the Subject, or the Subject into the Adversary, is hardest to treat. |
| 自占病，不宜世爻变鬼，及化回头克； | 自己占病，世爻不宜变成鬼，也不宜变出回头克； | In divining one's own illness the Self line should not change into Pressure or into a returning control; |
| 占弟兄、妻子，皆不宜鬼变弟兄、妻子，及弟兄、妻子而变鬼。 | 占兄弟、妻子的病，都不宜鬼变成兄弟、妻子，也不宜兄弟、妻子变成鬼。 | in divining a brother or a wife and children, Pressure should not change into the Peer line or the Wealth line, nor those lines change into Pressure. |
| 又不宜兄变财、财化兄、父化子、子化父。 | 又不宜兄变财、财变兄、父变子、子变父。 | Nor should Peer change into Wealth, Wealth into Peer, Resource into Output, or Output into Resource. |
| 野鹤曰：鬼，乃父母之元神，父动化鬼，乃为化生，轻病者即愈； | 野鹤说：鬼是父母的元神，父母爻动、变成鬼，是化生，病轻的马上好； | The Old Man of Yehe says: Pressure is the Support of Resource, and Resource moving and changing into Pressure is a changing into generation, so a light illness heals at once; |
| 久病者，鬼化父、父化鬼，则皆主危亡。 | 久病的，鬼变父、父变鬼，都主危亡。 | in a long illness, Pressure changing into Resource or Resource into Pressure always means danger of death. |
| 或曰：用化鬼即为凶兆，假使又值旬空，近病者何以断之？ | 有人说：用神变鬼就是凶兆，假如又落旬空，近病的怎么断？ | Someone says: the Subject changing into Pressure is at once an ill omen; suppose it is also Void, how is a recent illness to be judged? |
| 余曰：曾有占验，请试详： | 我说：曾经有过验的卦，请你仔细看： | I say: there was a divination once verified; please examine it in detail: |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 12639 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 12639*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如：申月庚寅日(旬空：午未)，占子近病，得“恒之解” | 比如：申月庚寅日（旬空：午、未），占儿子的近病，得恒卦变解卦。 | For example: in the Shen month, on a Gengyin day (void: Wu and Wei), a divination on a son's recent illness gave Heng changing into Xie. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 12645 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 12645*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 断曰：鬼变子孙，夭折之兆，幸子孙临旬空，近病即愈，恐其难过午年。 | 断语：鬼变成子孙，是夭折的兆头，幸好子孙临旬空，近病马上就好，恐怕他过不了午年。 | Judgment: Pressure changing into Output is a sign of dying young; luckily Output sits on Void, so the recent illness heals at once, but I fear he will hardly pass the Wu year. |
| 果在出空之日而愈。 | 果然在出空的那天好了。 | He did recover on the day the Void was out. |
| 辰年占卦，至午年痘症而死。 | 辰年占的卦，到午年出痘症死了。 | The divination was made in a Chen year, and in the Wu year he died of smallpox. |

> **打架**:「用化鬼」是不是必死：黄金策旧注读作用爻发动又变出鬼爻、没有解救必死（LY.ILL.HJC.44），王洪绪反对拿「用神变官鬼必死」来断（同条）；野鹤的说法在两者之间：用化鬼是凶兆，轻病（父动化鬼是化生）和近病落旬空的可以愈，久病则鬼化父、父化鬼都主危亡。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether 'the Subject changing into Pressure' is surely fatal: the old commentary of the Huangjin Ce reads it as the Subject line moving and also changing out a Pressure line, and with no rescue he surely dies (LY.ILL.HJC.44), while Wang Hongxu rejects judging 'the Subject changing into Pressure is surely fatal' (same entry); the Old Man of Yehe stands between the two: the Subject changing into Pressure is an ill omen, but a light illness (Resource moving into Pressure is a changing into generation) and a recent illness whose Subject falls into Void can recover, and in a long illness Pressure changing into Resource or Resource into Pressure always means danger of death.

> **按**:书中例里，儿子占病，用神是子孙，「鬼变子孙」就是官鬼变成用神。子孙落旬空，所以近病出空之日就好了；但「用化鬼」这个凶兆没有消失，后来在午年死于痘症。
>
> **Note:** In the case, a son's illness is divined, so the Subject is Output, and 'Pressure changing into Output' is Pressure changing into the Subject. Output being in Void, the recent illness healed on the day the Void was out; but the ill omen of 'the Subject changing into Pressure' did not go away, and he later died of smallpox in the Wu year.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.44、LY.ILL.ZS.03、LY.ILL.ZS.07

## LY.ILL.ZS.07 动墓绝、化墓绝须逢生旺，日月克、动爻克最怕休囚：用神入墓入绝或动而化墓化绝，全看旺衰；日月动爻冲克也看用神旺衰，旺者冲去克神之日愈，衰者生助克神之日危

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用神入墓、入绝，或者动了变入墓、变入绝，全看用神旺衰：旺的不必担心，衰的确实令人担忧。日月、动爻来冲克用神，也要看用神旺衰：旺相的，到冲走克神的那天就好；衰弱的，到生助克神的那天就危险。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Subject that enters a tomb or extinction, or moves and changes into tomb or extinction, is judged wholly by whether it is strong or weak: a strong one need not be feared, a weak one is truly a cause for worry. When the Day, Month or a moving line clashes or controls the Subject, one also sees whether the Subject is strong or weak: if strong, it heals on the day the controlling spirit is clashed away; if weak, it is in danger on the day the controlling spirit is generated or helped.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12646 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12646*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 动墓绝、化墓绝，须逢生旺； | 用神动而入墓入绝、或变入墓绝，要遇到生旺才行； | A Subject that moves into a tomb or extinction, or changes into tomb or extinction, must meet birth or strength; |
| 日月克、动爻克，最怕休囚。 | 被日月克、被动爻克，最怕用神休囚。 | controlled by the Day or Month or by a moving line, what is most feared is that it be resting or trapped. |
| 用神逢墓绝，及动而化墓、化绝，全看旺衰，用神旺者何须虑，用神衰者实堪忧。 | 用神遇到墓绝，以及动而变入墓、变入绝，全看旺衰：用神旺的何必担心，用神衰的确实令人担忧。 | A Subject that meets tomb or extinction, or moves and changes into tomb or extinction, is judged wholly by whether it is strong or weak: a strong Subject has nothing to fear, a weak one is truly a cause for worry. |
| 日月动爻冲克者，亦须看用神旺衰，旺相者，冲去克神之日而愈，衰弱者，生助克神之日而危。 | 日月、动爻冲克用神的，也要看用神旺衰：旺相的，到冲走克神的那天就好；衰弱的，到生助克神的那天就危险。 | When the Day, Month or a moving line clashes or controls it, one must also see whether the Subject is strong or weak: if strong, it heals on the day the controlling spirit is clashed away; if weak, it is in danger on the day the controlling spirit is generated or helped. |

> **按**:这一条讲墓绝不是一见就死，要看用神本身旺不旺。和黄金策病体章的 LY.ILL.HJC.41、HJC.54（世鬼入墓、幽明两墓）相比，野鹤把墓绝的后果全交给旺衰去定。
>
> **Note:** This entry says that tomb and extinction do not mean death at first sight but depend on whether the Subject itself is strong. Compared with LY.ILL.HJC.41 and HJC.54 in the Huangjin Ce's chapter on the patient's condition (Pressure on the Self line entering the tomb, the dark and bright tombs), the Old Man of Yehe leaves the outcome of tomb and extinction wholly to strength and weakness.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.41、LY.ILL.HJC.47、LY.ILL.ZS.08

## LY.ILL.ZS.08 散破无援脱气、忌摇元动仇兴：用神临散、遇破、全无生扶、脱气，四者若无根蒂少吉多凶；忌神动克用神，有元神发动可救，只恐仇神又动则凶危

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用神临在散上、遇到破、完全没有生扶、或者被脱气，这四种情形要是没有根基，少吉多凶。忌神动来克用神，如果有元神发动，就许它有救，只怕仇神又动，就主凶险危急。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Subject sits on dispersal, meets a break, has no generation or support at all, or is drained of qi, and it has no root in any of these four cases, there is little luck and much ill. When the Adversary moves and controls the Subject, if the Support moves, promise rescue; the only fear is that the Enemy spirit moves as well, and then it means danger and misfortune.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12647 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12647*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 散破无援脱气，忌摇元动仇兴。 | 散、破、没有援助、脱气；忌神摇动、元神发动、仇神兴起。 | Scattered, broken, without help, drained of qi; the Adversary stirs, the Support moves, the Enemy rises. |
| 用神临散、遇破，及全无生扶、或脱气，此四者若无根蒂，少吉多凶。 | 用神临在散上、遇到破，以及完全没有生扶、或者被脱气，这四种情形要是没有根基，少吉多凶。 | If the Subject sits on dispersal, meets a break, has no generation or support at all, or is drained of qi, and it has no root in any of these four cases, there is little luck and much ill. |
| 忌神动克用神，若有元神发动，许之有救，只恐仇神又动，当主凶危，宜于元神章内详之。 | 忌神动来克用神，如果有元神发动，就许它有救，只怕仇神又动，就主凶险危急，要在元神章里详看。 | When the Adversary moves and controls the Subject, if the Support moves, promise rescue; the only fear is that the Enemy spirit moves as well, and then it means danger and misfortune; see the chapter on the Support for the details. |

> **按**:「散」「破」是爻被日月冲散、冲破；「脱气」是爻的力量被它生出的爻泄走。「仇神」是和元神作对的那一类爻（生忌神、克元神），详见书里的元神章。
>
> **Note:** 'Dispersed' and 'broken' are a line clashed apart or broken by the Day or Month; 'drained of qi' is a line whose strength is leaked away by the line it generates. The 'Enemy spirit' is the kind of line that works against the Support (it generates the Adversary and controls the Support); see the chapter on the Support in the book.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.37、LY.ILL.ZS.07

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.13 用绝逢生危而有救、主衰得助重亦何妨：用爻受克制刑冲但有日月动爻一个生扶，是绝处逢生临危有救；用神不宜太弱，得生扶拱合重病也不至死；觉子：用神也不宜太旺，过犹不及（旺极的老人卦全胜，壬戌日死）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用爻在绝地遇到生，危险却有救；主象衰弱得到帮助，病重又有什么妨碍。用爻受到克制、刑冲，只要日月、动爻有一个来生扶它，就是绝处逢生，临危有救。用神不宜太弱，太弱就体虚，一时难以痊愈，但只要得到生扶拱合，就算得重病，也不至于死。觉子补充：用神也不宜太旺，易理贵在中庸，过头和不及一样。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Subject meeting life in extinction is in danger yet rescued; a weak main image that is helped, however heavy the illness, what does it matter? If the Subject line is controlled, punished or clashed, but the Day, Month or a moving line has one that generates and supports it, this is meeting life in a place of extinction, and in danger it is rescued. The Subject should not be too weak; if too weak the body is frail and cannot quickly heal, but if it gets generation, support, embracing or combining, then even in a heavy illness he does not die. Juezi adds: the Subject should not be too strong either; the principle of the Changes prizes the mean, and going too far is like falling short.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12730 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12730*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用绝逢生，危而有救，主衰得助，重亦何妨。 | 用爻在绝地遇到生，危险却有救；主象衰弱得到帮助，病重又有什么妨碍。 | The Subject meeting life in extinction is in danger yet rescued; a weak main image that is helped, however heavy the illness, what does it matter? |
| 用爻受克制、刑冲，但得日月动爻有一而生扶者，乃为绝处逢生，临危有救。 | 用爻受到克制、刑冲，只要日月、动爻有一个来生扶它，就是绝处逢生，临危有救。 | If the Subject line is controlled, punished or clashed, but the Day, Month or a moving line has one that generates and supports it, this is meeting life in a place of extinction, and in danger it is rescued. |
| 用神不宜太弱，太弱则体虚，卒难痊愈，但得生扶拱合，虽得重病，亦不至死。 | 用神不宜太弱，太弱就体虚，一时难以痊愈，但只要得到生扶拱合，就算得重病，也不至于死。 | The Subject should not be too weak; if too weak the body is frail and cannot quickly heal, but if it gets generation, support, embracing or combining, then even in a heavy illness he does not die. |

**觉子(李文辉,《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 12730 行 · *Juezi (Li Wenhui, Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 12730*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 觉子曰：用神亦不宜太旺，易理贵乎中庸，过犹不及。 | 觉子说：用神也不宜太旺，易理贵在中庸，过头和不及一样。 | Juezi says: the Subject should not be too strong either; the principle of the Changes prizes the mean, and going too far is like falling short. |

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 12774 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 12774*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此公寿逾八旬，占病得此，或曰：世临日辰，当权得令，又有寅木生之，应不药而愈。 | 这位老先生年过八十，占病得到这一卦，有人说：世爻临着日辰，当权得令，又有寅木生它，应当不吃药就好。 | This gentleman was over eighty, and having got this hexagram in divining his illness, someone said: the Self line sits on the Day, holds power and is in season, and Yin Wood generates it besides, so he should recover without medicine. |
| 余曰：过十八壬戌日，则无碍矣。 | 我说：过了十八日壬戌日，就没有妨碍了。 | I said: once the eighteenth day, the Renxu day, is past, there will be no harm. |
| 已果卒于壬戌日寅时。 | 后来果然死在壬戌日寅时。 | He in fact died on the Renxu day, at the Yin hour. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 12775 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 12775*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 或问：卦旺极，何所见而断其戍日之危也？ | 有人问：卦旺到了极点，凭什么看出来断他戌日有危险？ | Someone asked: the hexagram is at the extreme of strength; what did you see that made you judge danger on the Xu day? |
| 余曰：正为旺极耳，器满则倾，理之常也，衰极之老人而得全胜之卦，如日将坠而见霞，灯将然而放亮，岂能久乎？ | 我说：正因为旺到了极点。器皿满了就会倾倒，这是常理；衰弱到极点的老人得到全盛的卦，就像太阳快落时出现晚霞，灯快灭时放出亮光，怎么能长久呢？ | I said: precisely because it is at the extreme of strength. A vessel that is full tips over, which is the way of things; a very old and failing man who gets a hexagram of complete strength is like the sun about to set showing its glow, or a lamp about to go out flaring up: how can it last? |
| 断壬戌日者，旺火而入墓也，今死寅时者，逢三合也。 | 断在壬戌日，是因为旺火入了墓；现在死在寅时，是因为遇到了三合。 | I judged the Renxu day because strong Fire enters its tomb; that he died in the Yin hour is because it met a three-harmony. |

> **按**:这一条是黄金策病体章 LY.ILL.HJC.47（用绝逢生）和 LY.ILL.HJC.48（主衰得助）的合并重写，觉子那一句「用神也不宜太旺」对应 LY.ILL.HJC.36（太刚则折）。书中例是自占病的一卦：卦旺到极点，老人反而在壬戌日死了，用来说明旺过头也不好。案语「过十八壬戌日」按字面译。
>
> **Note:** This entry is a combined rewriting of LY.ILL.HJC.47 (the Subject meeting life in extinction) and LY.ILL.HJC.48 (a weak main image that is helped) in the chapter on the patient's condition, and Juezi's line 'the Subject should not be too strong either' matches LY.ILL.HJC.36 (too rigid, it snaps). The case is a divination on one's own illness: the hexagram is at the extreme of strength and the old man on the contrary dies on the Renxu day, which shows that strength carried too far is not good either. The phrase 'once the eighteenth, the Renxu day, is past' is translated as it stands.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.36、LY.ILL.HJC.47、LY.ILL.HJC.48

## LY.ILL.ZS.35 不起之症卦中不见其医、立愈之灾爻中不报用药：用神化鬼、鬼化用神、忌神化六亲、六亲化忌神、用神化绝化克化墓、久病逢冲逢空、随鬼入墓，都是无药可医；近病六冲或用神旬空或化回头生、自占子孙持世，不药而愈，也不现医

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占医生的卦里看不到医生，有两种情形。一种是治不好的病，神不让医生出现：用神变鬼、鬼变用神，忌神变六亲、六亲变忌神，用神变绝、变克、变墓，久病遇冲遇空，随着鬼入墓，这些都是没有药可医的病。另一种是马上就好的病，神也不让医生出现：近病如果不是卦得六冲，就是用神值旬空、或用神变回头生；自己占病，子孙持世，这些都是不吃药就好的。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In a hexagram that divines a doctor, the doctor may fail to appear, in two cases. One is an illness that cannot be cured, where the spirit does not let the doctor appear: the Subject changing into Pressure or Pressure into the Subject, the Adversary into a relation or a relation into the Adversary, the Subject changing into extinction, into a control or into a tomb, a long illness meeting a clash or Void, following Pressure into the tomb; all these are illnesses that no medicine can treat. The other is an illness that heals at once, where the spirit also does not let the doctor appear: a recent illness is either in a Six-Clash hexagram, or has its Subject in Void or changing into a returning generation; in divining one's own illness, Output holds the Self line; in all these the patient recovers without medicine.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13224 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13224*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 不起之症，卦中不见其医； | 治不好的病，卦里看不到那位医生； | In an incurable illness, the doctor is not seen in the hexagram; |
| 立愈之灾，爻中不报用药。 | 马上就好的灾病，爻里不显示用什么药。 | in a trouble that heals at once, the lines do not report any medicine to use. |
| 屡见占医之卦，无药可医之症者，神不现其医矣。 | 多次见到占医生的卦，遇到没有药可医的病，神就不让那位医生出现。 | I have often seen in hexagrams divining a doctor that for an illness that no medicine can treat, the spirit does not let the doctor appear. |
| 若用神化鬼、鬼化用神，忌神化六亲、六亲化忌神，及用神化绝、化克、化墓，久病逢冲逢空，随鬼入墓者，皆无药可医之症也，故神不现其医。 | 如果用神变鬼、鬼变用神，忌神变六亲、六亲变忌神，以及用神变绝、变克、变墓，久病遇冲遇空，随着鬼入墓的，都是没有药可医的病，所以神不让医生出现。 | If the Subject changes into Pressure or Pressure into the Subject, the Adversary into a relation or a relation into the Adversary, or the Subject changes into extinction, into a control or into a tomb, or a long illness meets a clash or Void, or follows Pressure into the tomb: all are illnesses that no medicine can treat, and so the spirit does not let the doctor appear. |
| 立愈之疾，神亦不现其医； | 马上就好的病，神也不让医生出现； | in an illness that heals at once, the spirit also does not let the doctor appear; |
| 近病者，若非卦得六冲，即现用值旬空，或用神化回头生； | 近病的，如果不是卦得六冲，就是用神值旬空，或者用神变回头生； | for a recent illness, it is either that the hexagram is Six-Clash, or that the Subject falls into Void, or that the Subject changes into a returning generation; |
| 自占者，子孙持世，此皆不药而愈也。 | 自己占的，子孙持世，这些都是不吃药就好的。 | in divining oneself, Output holding the Self line: in all these the patient recovers without medicine. |

> **按**:这一条把前面几条（LY.ILL.ZS.02、ZS.06、ZS.07、ZS.13）里的死症和不药而愈的情形放到一处，用「卦里看不看得见医生」来当记号。它说的是占医生的卦，不是占病的卦。
>
> **Note:** This entry gathers the fatal cases and the cases of recovery without medicine from several earlier entries (LY.ILL.ZS.02, ZS.06, ZS.07 and ZS.13) and uses 'whether the doctor can be seen in the hexagram' as the sign. It speaks of the hexagram that divines a doctor, not of the one that divines the illness.

相关:LY.ILL.ZS.02、LY.ILL.ZS.06、LY.ILL.ZS.07、LY.ILL.ZS.13

## LY.ILL.ZSZ.02 八卦断例·占病逢观、大有、大畜、损、无妄、兑、谦：病痊、病瘥、病脱(病会好)

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:周易尚占的八卦断例是一卦一行断辞，下面七个卦的断辞明说病会好：观「财聚病痊」，大有「讼散病痊」，大畜「婚成病脱」，损「婚成病瘥」，无妄「病瘥讼和」，兑「病瘥婚成」，谦「身吉病瘥」。痊、瘥是痊愈，病脱是病脱去了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In the judgments by example of the Zhouyi Shangzhan each hexagram has one line of verdicts, and in these seven the line says outright that the illness will be well: Guan, 'wealth gathers, the illness is cured'; Dayou, 'the lawsuit disperses, the illness is cured'; Daxu, 'marriage is made, the illness drops away'; Sun, 'marriage is made, the illness is cured'; Wuwang, 'the illness is cured, the lawsuit is settled'; Dui, 'the illness is cured, marriage is made'; Qian (Humility), 'the person is fortunate, the illness is cured'. 'Cured' and 'recovered' both mean full recovery, and 'drops away' means the illness is shed.

**《周易尚占·八卦断例章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 890 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter of Judgments by Example for the Eight Palaces", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 890*

> **打架**:按卦名断病，各书不一样，而这七个卦里的观、大畜，别的书说占病凶。观卦：这里说「财聚病痊」，可是阐奥歌章（LY.ILL.CA.03）说「夏观」凶，卜筮全书疾病论（LY.ILL.BSQS.35）说「夏嫌观蛊」，卜筮元龟一段（LY.ILL.DYTJ.15）和阐奥歌章第五首（LY.ILL.CA.06）都把观列进占病的凶卦，天玄赋（LY.ILL.TXF.06）说观「切忌世身入墓」（有条件的），增删卜易（LY.ILL.ZS.01）转述的「断之必死」的十个卦里也有观、大畜。大畜卦这里说「婚成病脱」，天玄赋却说大畜「莫逢财鬼俱兴」。另一方面，野鹤（LY.ILL.ZS.01）和易林补遗（LY.ILL.EL.13）根本反对按卦名断病，说断病全凭用神、「休把卦名推祸福」。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Judging illness by hexagram name differs from book to book, and of these seven the other books call Guan and Daxu ominous for illness. Guan: here it says 'wealth gathers, the illness is cured', but the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.03) calls 'Guan in summer' ominous, the Bushi Quanshu's essay on illness (LY.ILL.BSQS.35) says 'summer dislikes Guan and Gu', the passage of the Bushi Yuangui (LY.ILL.DYTJ.15) and the fifth poem of the Chan'ao Gezhang (LY.ILL.CA.06) both put Guan among the ominous hexagrams for illness, the Tianxuan Fu (LY.ILL.TXF.06) says of Guan 'above all avoid the Self or the Body entering the tomb' (a conditional statement), and the ten hexagrams of 'judge death' reported in the Zengshan Buyi (LY.ILL.ZS.01) include Guan and Daxu. For Daxu the line here says 'marriage is made, the illness drops away', while the Tianxuan Fu says of Daxu 'do not meet Wealth and Pressure both stirring'. On the other side, the Old Man of Yehe (LY.ILL.ZS.01) and the Yilin Buyi (LY.ILL.EL.13) are against judging illness by hexagram name at all, saying that life and death in illness rest wholly on the Subject, 'do not push fortune and misfortune from the name of the hexagram'. The books do not decide.

> **按**:八卦断例章一卦一行断辞，每行八个词组：头一个解释卦名的字义，后面几个分别讲事、人口、田蚕、官司、婚姻、出行、病、孕等。所以病只是其中的一个词组，这里把整行录出来，病的那一句在观卦是第七个、大有第五个、大畜第六个、损第三个、无妄第五个、兑第三个、谦第七个词组。这些断辞只按卦名给，没有讲用神、世应、动变，也不分近病久病。大有卦这一行开头没有「X者Y也」的释名，是「盛大丰育」四个字，照原文。第三个卦「畜者聚也」是大畜（艮宫第三卦），不是小畜，小畜另有一行「小畜止也」，那一行没有讲病，这里不用。「病脱」的「脱」是病去掉了。原文里整行没有标点，词组之间只有空格，译文分词组对齐。
>
> **Note:** The chapter of judgments by example gives each hexagram one line of verdicts, in eight phrases: the first explains the sense of the hexagram's name, and the rest speak in turn of affairs, household members, fields and silkworms, lawsuits, marriage, travel, illness, pregnancy and so on. Illness is therefore only one phrase among them, and the whole line is taken here, with the phrase on illness being the seventh for Guan, the fifth for Dayou, the sixth for Daxu, the third for Sun, the fifth for Wuwang, the third for Dui and the seventh for Qian (Humility). These verdicts are given by hexagram name alone, with nothing said of the Subject, Self and Other or moving lines, and no distinction between recent and long illness. The line of Dayou has no explanation of the name of the form 'X means Y' at its head, but the four characters 'flourishing, grand, abundant, nourishing', as in the original. The 'Xu means gathering' of the third hexagram is Daxu (the third hexagram of the Gen palace) and not Xiaoxu, which has a line of its own, 'Xiaoxu means holding back', that does not speak of illness and is not used here. The 'drops away' of 'the illness drops away' is the illness being shed. The whole line has no punctuation in the original, only spaces between the phrases, and the translation is aligned phrase by phrase.

相关:LY.ILL.ZSZ.03、LY.ILL.ZSZ.04、LY.ILL.ZSZ.05、LY.ILL.ZS.01、LY.ILL.EL.13、LY.ILL.CA.03、LY.ILL.CA.06、LY.ILL.DYTJ.15、LY.ILL.BSQS.35、LY.ILL.TXF.06

## LY.ILL.ZSZ.05 八卦断例·占病逢坎、噬嗑、蛊、离、困、睽、小过、明夷：病凶、病重、件件遭伤、有惊、有忧

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:周易尚占八卦断例里，这八个卦的断辞说病凶险、沉重或有忧：坎「孕病皆凶」，噬嗑「病重讼凶」，蛊「孕吉病凶」，离「病重财轻」，困「宅身孕病件件遭伤」，睽「问病有惊」，小过「官阻病忧」，明夷「占家父病」。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In the judgments by example of the Zhouyi Shangzhan the line of each of these eight hexagrams says that illness is ominous, heavy or a worry: Kan, 'pregnancy and illness are both ominous'; Shihe, 'illness is heavy and the lawsuit is ill'; Gu, 'pregnancy is auspicious, illness is ominous'; Li, 'illness is heavy and wealth is light'; Kun (Confinement), 'dwelling, person, pregnancy, illness: each one is hurt'; Kui, 'asking about illness: there is a fright'; Xiaoguo, 'office is blocked and illness is a worry'; Mingyi, 'divining the household: the father is ill'.

| 凡事不顺 | 凡事不顺。 | Nothing goes smoothly. |

| 切宜谨慎 | 务必谨慎。 | be sure to be careful. |

> **打架**:别的书的占病凶卦名单里，同人、需、剥、节、蒙在内，周易尚占对这几个卦的断辞里却没有讲病：同人是「婚孕皆成、所求皆至」，需是「不宜轻举、所谋不成」，剥是「退则获安、进则不利」，节是「身命不宁」，蒙是「却宜占病」（LY.ILL.ZSZ.06）。阐奥歌章第三首（LY.ILL.CA.03）、第五首（LY.ILL.CA.06）、卜筮元龟一段（LY.ILL.DYTJ.15）和增删卜易转述的十卦（LY.ILL.ZS.01）都有同人；需、剥、节见于卜筮元龟一段和阐奥歌章；蒙只见于卜筮全书疾病论（LY.ILL.BSQS.35）。没有说的不等于说了好，这几个卦占病吉凶，周易尚占这一行没有表态。反过来，这里的坎、噬嗑、蛊、离、困、睽、小过，别的凶卦名单里大多没有（只有蛊、困在），是周易尚占独有的断语。书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Among the other books' lists of ominous hexagrams for illness are Tongren, Xu, Bo, Jie and Meng, yet the lines of the Zhouyi Shangzhan for these hexagrams do not speak of illness: Tongren is 'marriage and pregnancy both succeed, all that is sought arrives', Xu is 'rash moves do not suit, what is planned does not succeed', Bo is 'to withdraw is to gain safety, to advance is unfavourable', Jie is 'the person and fate are unsettled', and Meng is 'but it suits divining illness' (LY.ILL.ZSZ.06). Tongren is in the third (LY.ILL.CA.03) and fifth (LY.ILL.CA.06) poems of the Chan'ao Gezhang, in the passage of the Bushi Yuangui (LY.ILL.DYTJ.15) and in the ten hexagrams reported in the Zengshan Buyi (LY.ILL.ZS.01); Xu, Bo and Jie are in the Bushi Yuangui passage and the Chan'ao Gezhang; Meng is only in the Bushi Quanshu's essay on illness (LY.ILL.BSQS.35). Not saying is not saying good: for the verdict on illness of these hexagrams the line of the Zhouyi Shangzhan takes no position. Conversely, of the hexagrams here, Kan, Shihe, Li, Kui and Xiaoguo are for the most part absent from the other lists of ominous hexagrams (only Gu and Kun (Confinement) are in them), so these verdicts are peculiar to the Zhouyi Shangzhan. The books do not decide.

> **按**:这八行里，坎「孕病皆凶」、蛊「孕吉病凶」把病和怀孕并说，困是「宅身孕病」四件并说，都是一句话管几件事。明夷「占孕母忧，占家父病」两句对着读：占怀孕时母亲有忧，占家宅时父亲有病；「占家父病」只有四个字，这个读法是按上一句的格式推的。噬嗑一行的「求财且且」「惟道有者」两处意思不清，「道有者」读来像「有道者」的倒文，译文都照字面，没有改字。噬嗑的头两个词组「噬者啮也」「嗑者合也」都是在解释卦名。「婚寡」译作婚姻孤寡，「孕双」译作怀孕得双胎，都是字面的读法。
>
> **Note:** In these eight lines, Kan, 'pregnancy and illness are both ominous', and Gu, 'pregnancy is auspicious, illness is ominous', speak of illness and pregnancy together, and Kun (Confinement) names 'dwelling, person, pregnancy, illness' together, each a single phrase covering several matters. For Mingyi the two phrases 'divining pregnancy: the mother is in anxiety' and 'divining the household: the father is ill' are read in parallel: in divining pregnancy the mother is anxious, in divining the household the father is ill; 'divining the household: the father is ill' is only four characters in the book, and this reading is inferred from the pattern of the previous phrase. In the line of Shihe, 'seeking wealth: barely so' and 'only those who have the Way' are unclear in sense, and the second looks like an inverted form of 'those who have the Way'; both are translated literally and no character has been changed. The first two phrases of Shihe, 'Shi means to bite' and 'He means to close together', both explain the name of the hexagram. 'Marriage is lonely' translates the literal 'marriage widowed' and 'twins' of Li translates the literal 'pregnancy double'.

相关:LY.ILL.ZSZ.02、LY.ILL.ZSZ.03、LY.ILL.ZSZ.04、LY.ILL.ZSZ.06、LY.ILL.CA.03、LY.ILL.CA.06、LY.ILL.DYTJ.15、LY.ILL.ZS.01、LY.ILL.BSQS.35
